# ============================================================
# DIAGNOSTIC: 16 IMAGES FROM THE FAILED 256-IMAGE TRAIN SUBSET
# Fresh U-Net, batch 8 (4 authentic + 4 tampered), 150 epochs.
# No modification to V4/B/C models or checkpoints.
# ============================================================
import gc
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from IPython.display import display
from torch.utils.data import Subset
from forgery_localization.models.unet import UNet
def diagnose_16_from_subset256(source_loader):
    SEED = 42
--- PAGE 84 ---
    EPOCHS = 150
    LR = 1e-3
    LOG_EPOCHS = {0, 1, 25, 50, 75, 100, 150}
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    source = source_loader.dataset
    if not isinstance(source, Subset) or len(source) != 256:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "train_loader phải chứa Subset 256 ảnh của Experiment B/C. "
            "Không dùng v4_train_loader toàn bộ dataset cho cell này."
        )
    # Map subset positions to original dataset indices.
    # Also unwrap the known augmentation wrapper, if present.
    def resolve_sample(dataset, index):
        while True:
            if isinstance(dataset, Subset):
                index = int(dataset.indices[index])
                dataset = dataset.dataset
            elif hasattr(dataset, "base_dataset"):
                dataset = dataset.base_dataset
            else:
                return dataset, index
    references = [
        resolve_sample(source, i)
        for i in range(len(source))
    ]
    if any(not hasattr(ds, "records") for ds, _ in references):
        raise RuntimeError("Không tìm thấy .records của dataset gốc.")
    def get_label(position):
        dataset, index = references[position]
        label = str(dataset.records[index]["label"]).lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(label, label)
    authentic_positions = [
        i for i in range(256)
        if get_label(i) == "authentic"
    ]
    tampered_positions = [
        i for i in range(256)
        if get_label(i) == "tampered"
    ]
    assert len(authentic_positions) >= 8
    assert len(tampered_positions) >= 8
    # Reproducible selection. Do not reselect based on the results.
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    selected = (
        rng.choice(authentic_positions, 8, replace=False).tolist()
        + rng.choice(tampered_positions, 8, replace=False).tolist()
    )
    print("Source subset size:", len(source))
    print("Selected positions within subset:", selected)
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()]
        if device.type == "cuda"
        else []
    )
    history = []
    sample_rows = []
    # Preserve surrounding notebook RNG state.
--- PAGE 85 ---
    with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
        torch.manual_seed(SEED)
        image_list, mask_list = [], []
        # Load each selected sample once, without the known flip wrapper.
        for position in selected:
            dataset, index = references[position]
            image, mask = dataset[index]
            record = dataset.records[index]
            image_list.append(image)
            mask_list.append(mask)
            sample_rows.append({
                "subset_position": position,
                "dataset_index": index,
                "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                "label": get_label(position),
                "gt_foreground_pct": 100 * mask.float().mean().item(),
            })
        images = torch.stack(image_list).to(
            device, dtype=torch.float32
        )
        masks = torch.stack(mask_list).to(
            device, dtype=torch.float32
        )
        del image_list, mask_list
        assert images.shape == (16, 3, 256, 256), images.shape
        assert masks.shape == (16, 1, 256, 256), masks.shape
        assert torch.isfinite(images).all().item()
        assert torch.isfinite(masks).all().item()
        assert 0 <= images.min().item() <= images.max().item() <= 1
        assert ((masks == 0) | (masks == 1)).all().item()
        positive = masks.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
        assert not positive[:8].any().item()
        assert positive[8:].all().item()
        print(f"\nSelected GT foreground: {100 * masks.mean().item():.4f}%")
        print("\nSELECTED SAMPLES")
        display(pd.DataFrame(sample_rows))
        # Same model and objective as the previous diagnostic.
        torch.manual_seed(SEED)
        model = UNet(
            in_channels=3,
            out_channels=1,
            base_channels=16,
        ).to(device)
        optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
            model.parameters(),
            lr=LR,
            weight_decay=1e-4,
        )
        pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
        def loss_fn(logits, targets):
            logits = logits.float()
            targets = targets.float()
            bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                logits,
                targets,
                pos_weight=pos_weight,
            )
            probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
            intersection = (probabilities * targets).sum()
            denominator = probabilities.sum() + targets.sum()
            soft_dice_loss = 1 - (
                (2 * intersection + 1.0)
--- PAGE 86 ---
                / (denominator + 1.0)
            )
            return bce + soft_dice_loss, bce, soft_dice_loss
        @torch.no_grad()
        def evaluate():
            model.eval()
            logits = model(images)
            loss, bce, soft_dice = loss_fn(logits, masks)
            predictions = logits.sigmoid() > 0.5
            truth = masks.bool()
            tp = (predictions & truth).sum().item()
            fp = (predictions & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn = (~predictions & truth).sum().item()
            intersection = (
                predictions & truth
            ).flatten(1).sum(1).float()
            denominator = (
                predictions.flatten(1).sum(1)
                + truth.flatten(1).sum(1)
            ).float()
            per_image_dice = (
                (2 * intersection + 1e-6)
                / (denominator + 1e-6)
            )
            metrics = {
                "eval_loss": loss.item(),
                "bce": bce.item(),
                "soft_dice_loss": soft_dice.item(),
                "micro_dice_05": (
                    2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1)
                ),
                "tampered_dice_05": (
                    per_image_dice[positive].mean().item()
                ),
                "predicted_positive_pct": (
                    100 * predictions.float().mean().item()
                ),
                # Authentic masks are zero: predicted positives are FP.
                "authentic_false_positive_pct": (
                    100
                    * predictions[~positive].float().mean().item()
                ),
            }
            return metrics, per_image_dice, predictions
        # Two fixed balanced batches: each contains 4 Au + 4 Tp.
        batches = [
            [0, 1, 2, 3, 8, 9, 10, 11],
            [4, 5, 6, 7, 12, 13, 14, 15],
        ]
        print("\nFRESH MODEL | LR=1e-3 | BATCH=8 | 150 EPOCHS")
        for epoch in range(EPOCHS + 1):
            if epoch > 0:
                model.train()
                for indices in batches:
                    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                    logits = model(images[indices])
                    loss, _, _ = loss_fn(logits, masks[indices])
                    if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                        raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite loss")
                    loss.backward()
                    for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                        if not parameter.requires_grad:
--- PAGE 87 ---
                            continue
                        if parameter.grad is None:
                            raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                        if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                            raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                    optimizer.step()
                    del logits, loss
            if epoch in LOG_EPOCHS:
                metrics, _, _ = evaluate()
                if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in metrics.values()):
                    raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation metrics")
                history.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "optimizer_steps": epoch * 2,
                    **metrics,
                })
                print(
                    f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | "
                    f"Loss {metrics['eval_loss']:.4f} | "
                    f"Micro Dice {metrics['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                    f"Tampered Dice {metrics['tampered_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                    f"Authentic FP "
                    f"{metrics['authentic_false_positive_pct']:.2f}%"
                )
        # Final per-image results: do not hide difficult samples.
        _, per_image_dice, predictions = evaluate()
        for i, row in enumerate(sample_rows):
            row["predicted_positive_pct"] = (
                100 * predictions[i].float().mean().item()
            )
            row["tampered_dice_05"] = (
                per_image_dice[i].item()
                if row["label"] == "tampered"
                else float("nan")
            )
        del model, optimizer, images, masks, predictions, per_image_dice
        gc.collect()
        if device.type == "cuda":
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    return pd.DataFrame(history), pd.DataFrame(sample_rows)

    )

            in_channels=3,
            out_channels=1,
            base_channels=16,
        ).to(device)
        optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
            model.parameters(),
            lr=LR,
            weight_decay=1e-4,
        )
        pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
        def loss_fn(logits, targets):
            logits = logits.float()
            targets = targets.float()
            bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                logits,
                targets,
                pos_weight=pos_weight,
            )
            probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
            intersection = (probabilities * targets).sum()
            denominator = probabilities.sum() + targets.sum()
            soft_dice_loss = 1 - (
                (2 * intersection + 1.0)
                / (denominator + 1.0)
            )
            return bce + soft_dice_loss, bce, soft_dice_loss
--- PAGE 91 ---
        @torch.no_grad()
        def evaluate():
            model.eval()
            logits = model(images)
            loss, bce, soft_dice = loss_fn(logits, masks)
            predictions = logits.sigmoid() > 0.5
            truth = masks.bool()
            tp = (predictions & truth).sum().item()
            fp = (predictions & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn = (~predictions & truth).sum().item()
            intersection = (
                predictions & truth
            ).flatten(1).sum(1).float()
            denominator = (
                predictions.flatten(1).sum(1)
                + truth.flatten(1).sum(1)
            ).float()
            per_image_dice = (
                (2 * intersection + 1e-6)
                / (denominator + 1e-6)
            )
            metrics = {
                "eval_loss": loss.item(),
                "bce": bce.item(),
                "soft_dice_loss": soft_dice.item(),
                "micro_dice_05": (
                    2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1)
                ),
                "tampered_dice_05": (
                    per_image_dice[positive].mean().item()
                ),
                "predicted_positive_pct": (
                    100 * predictions.float().mean().item()
                ),
                # Authentic masks are zero: predicted positives are FP.
                "authentic_false_positive_pct": (
                    100
                    * predictions[~positive].float().mean().item()
                ),
            }
            return metrics, per_image_dice, predictions
        # Two fixed balanced batches: each contains 4 Au + 4 Tp.
        batches = [
            [0, 1, 2, 3, 8, 9, 10, 11],
            [4, 5, 6, 7, 12, 13, 14, 15],
        ]
        print("\nFRESH MODEL | LR=1e-3 | BATCH=8 | 150 EPOCHS")
        for epoch in range(EPOCHS + 1):
            if epoch > 0:
                model.train()
                for indices in batches:
                    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                    logits = model(images[indices])
                    loss, _, _ = loss_fn(logits, masks[indices])
                    if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                        raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite loss")
                    loss.backward()
                    for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                        if not parameter.requires_grad:
                            continue
                        if parameter.grad is None:
                            raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
--- PAGE 92 ---
                        if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                            raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                    optimizer.step()
                    del logits, loss
            if epoch in LOG_EPOCHS:
                metrics, _, _ = evaluate()
                if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in metrics.values()):
                    raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation metrics")
                history.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "optimizer_steps": epoch * 2,
                    **metrics,
                })
                print(
                    f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | "
                    f"Loss {metrics['eval_loss']:.4f} | "
                    f"Micro Dice {metrics['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                    f"Tampered Dice {metrics['tampered_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                    f"Authentic FP "
                    f"{metrics['authentic_false_positive_pct']:.2f}%"
                )
        # Final per-image results: do not hide difficult samples.
        _, per_image_dice, predictions = evaluate()
        for i, row in enumerate(sample_rows):
            row["predicted_positive_pct"] = (
                100 * predictions[i].float().mean().item()
            )
            row["tampered_dice_05"] = (
                per_image_dice[i].item()
                if row["label"] == "tampered"
                else float("nan")
            )
        del model, optimizer, images, masks, predictions, per_image_dice
        gc.collect()
        if device.type == "cuda":
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    return pd.DataFrame(history), pd.DataFrame(sample_rows)

import numpy as np

import torch.nn.functional as F
from pathlib import Path
from IPython.display import display
from forgery_localization.models.unet import UNet
def run_hard4_diagnostic(source_loader):
    SEED = 42
    STEPS = 300
    LR = 1e-3
    LOG_STEPS = {0, 1, 25, 50, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300}
    # Authentic trước, tampered sau.
    indices = [2347, 3403, 7700, 8693]
    expected_names = [
        "Au_cha_30565.jpg",
        "Au_pla_20011.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NNN_S_N_cha10140_cha10140_12198.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_pla20022_pla20022_01922.tif",
    ]
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    dataset = source_loader.dataset
    while hasattr(dataset, "base_dataset"):
        dataset = dataset.base_dataset
    if not hasattr(dataset, "records"):
        raise RuntimeError("Cần v4_train_loader của dataset gốc.")
    # Kiểm tra tên file để tránh dùng sai index sau khi đổi manifest.
    for index, expected in zip(indices, expected_names):
        actual = Path(dataset.records[index]["image_path"]).name
        if actual != expected:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Index {index} đang trỏ tới {actual}, "
                f"không phải {expected}. Dừng để kiểm tra manifest."
            )
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()]
        if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    history = []
    with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
        torch.manual_seed(SEED)
        # Đọc một lần: dữ liệu giữ nguyên ở mọi step.
        samples = [dataset[i] for i in indices]
        images = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).to(
            device, dtype=torch.float32
        )
        masks = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).to(
            device, dtype=torch.float32
        )
        del samples
        assert images.shape == (4, 3, 256, 256)
        assert masks.shape == (4, 1, 256, 256)
        assert torch.isfinite(images).all().item()
        assert torch.isfinite(masks).all().item()
        assert 0 <= images.min().item() <= images.max().item() <= 1
        assert ((masks == 0) | (masks == 1)).all().item()
        assert masks[:2].sum().item() == 0
        assert (masks[2:].flatten(1).sum(1) > 0).all().item()
        print("Indices:", indices)
        print(
            "GT foreground từng ảnh (%):",
            (100 * masks.flatten(1).mean(1)).cpu().tolist()
        )
--- PAGE 96 ---
        # Model mới hoàn toàn, không sao chép trọng số V4.
        torch.manual_seed(SEED)
        model = UNet(
            in_channels=3,
            out_channels=1,
            base_channels=16,
        ).to(device)
        optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
            model.parameters(),
            lr=LR,
            weight_decay=1e-4,
        )
        pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
        def loss_fn(logits):
            logits = logits.float()
            bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                logits, masks, pos_weight=pos_weight
            )
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            intersection = (probs * masks).sum()
            denominator = probs.sum() + masks.sum()
            dice_loss = 1 - (
                (2 * intersection + 1.0)
                / (denominator + 1.0)
            )
            return bce + dice_loss, bce, dice_loss
        @torch.no_grad()
        def evaluate(step):
            model.eval()
            logits = model(images)
            loss, bce, dice_loss = loss_fn(logits)
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            preds = probs > 0.5
            truth = masks.bool()
            tp = (preds & truth).sum().item()
            fp = (preds & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn = (~preds & truth).sum().item()
            result = {
                "step": step,
                "loss": loss.item(),
                "bce": bce.item(),
                "soft_dice_loss": dice_loss.item(),
                "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
                "authentic_fp_pct": (
                    100 * preds[:2].float().mean().item()
                ),
            }
            for position, index in [(2, 7700), (3, 8693)]:
                p = preds[position]
                gt = truth[position]
                intersection = (p & gt).sum().item()
                denominator = p.sum().item() + gt.sum().item()
                result[f"dice_{index}"] = (
                    2 * intersection / max(denominator, 1)
                )
                result[f"prob_in_mask_{index}"] = (
                    probs[position][gt].mean().item()
                )
                result[f"prob_background_{index}"] = (
                    probs[position][~gt].mean().item()
                )
            if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in result.values()):
                raise RuntimeError("Evaluation có giá trị không hữu hạn.")
            return result
--- PAGE 97 ---
        print("\nFRESH MODEL | 4 FIXED IMAGES | 300 STEPS")
        for step in range(STEPS + 1):
            if step > 0:
                model.train()
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                logits = model(images)
                loss, _, _ = loss_fn(logits)
                if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite loss tại step {step}")
                loss.backward()
                for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                    if not parameter.requires_grad:
                        continue
                    if parameter.grad is None:
                        raise RuntimeError(f"Thiếu gradient: {name}")
                    if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                        raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                optimizer.step()
                del logits, loss
            if step in LOG_STEPS:
                result = evaluate(step)
                history.append(result)
                print(
                    f"Step {step:03d} | Loss {result['loss']:.4f} | "
                    f"Dice 7700 {result['dice_7700']:.4f} | "
                    f"Dice 8693 {result['dice_8693']:.4f} | "
                    f"Authentic FP {result['authentic_fp_pct']:.2f}%"
                )
        model.eval()
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
    # Giữ model và dữ liệu để xem kết quả, không lưu checkpoint.
    return model, images, masks, pd.DataFrame(history)
if "v4_train_loader" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError("Cần chạy cell tạo v4_train_loader trước.")
hard4_model, hard4_images, hard4_masks, hard4_history = (
    run_hard4_diagnostic(v4_train_loader)
)

def run_fixed16_300(source_loader):
    SEED = 42
    EPOCHS = 300
    LR = 1e-3
    LOG_EPOCHS = {0, 1, 25, 50, 75, 100, 150, 200, 250, 300}
    # Đúng bộ 16 ảnh đã chạy, không chọn lại theo kết quả.
    indices = [
        2347, 3403, 856, 3032, 2462, 2241, 2008, 1985,
        7700, 8281, 6166, 8448, 6811, 7071, 8693, 4762,
    ]
    expected_names = [
        "Au_cha_30565.jpg",
        "Au_pla_20011.jpg",
        "Au_arc_30094.jpg",
        "Au_nat_30260.jpg",
        "Au_ind_00025.jpg",
        "Au_cha_30376.jpg",
        "Au_cha_10175.jpg",
        "Au_cha_10150.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NNN_S_N_cha10140_cha10140_12198.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRD_S_N_ind00028_ind00028_00895.tif",
        "Tp_D_NRN_S_N_pla00008_pla00009_10936.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_arc00051_arc00051_11208.jpg",
        "Tp_S_CRN_S_B_arc00095_arc00095_01035.tif",
        "Tp_S_NND_S_N_sec20028_sec20028_01618.tif",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_pla20022_pla20022_01922.tif",
        "Tp_D_CRN_M_O_art10113_art10112_11560.jpg",
    ]
    dataset = source_loader.dataset
    while hasattr(dataset, "base_dataset"):
        dataset = dataset.base_dataset
    if not hasattr(dataset, "records"):
        raise RuntimeError("Cần v4_train_loader của dataset gốc.")
    for index, expected in zip(indices, expected_names):
        actual = Path(dataset.records[index]["image_path"]).name
        if actual != expected:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Index {index} đang trỏ tới {actual}. "
                "Manifest đã thay đổi; dừng để kiểm tra."
            )
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    print("Indices:", indices)
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()]
        if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    history = []
    per_image_history = []
    with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
        torch.manual_seed(SEED)
        # Đọc một lần, cố định input trong toàn bộ thí nghiệm.
        samples = [dataset[i] for i in indices]
        images = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).to(
            device, dtype=torch.float32
        )
        masks = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).to(
            device, dtype=torch.float32
        )
        del samples
--- PAGE 100 ---
        assert images.shape == (16, 3, 256, 256)
        assert masks.shape == (16, 1, 256, 256)
        assert torch.isfinite(images).all().item()
        assert torch.isfinite(masks).all().item()
        assert 0 <= images.min().item() <= images.max().item() <= 1
        assert ((masks == 0) | (masks == 1)).all().item()
        positive = masks.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
        assert not positive[:8].any().item()
        assert positive[8:].all().item()
        print(f"GT foreground: {100 * masks.mean().item():.4f}%")
        print("Kỳ vọng gần 1.8255% như lần trước.")
        # Model mới; không dùng trọng số hard4_model hoặc V4.
        torch.manual_seed(SEED)
        model = UNet(
            in_channels=3,
            out_channels=1,
            base_channels=16,
        ).to(device)
        optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
            model.parameters(),
            lr=LR,
            weight_decay=1e-4,
        )
        pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
        def loss_fn(logits, targets):
            logits = logits.float()
            targets = targets.float()
            bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
            )
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
            denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
            soft_dice = 1 - (
                (2 * intersection + 1.0)
                / (denominator + 1.0)
            )
            return bce + soft_dice, bce, soft_dice
        @torch.no_grad()
        def evaluate(epoch):
            model.eval()
            logits = model(images)
            loss, bce, soft_dice = loss_fn(logits, masks)
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            preds = probs > 0.5
            truth = masks.bool()
            tp = (preds & truth).sum().item()
            fp = (preds & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn = (~preds & truth).sum().item()
            intersections = (
                preds & truth
            ).flatten(1).sum(1).float()
            denominators = (
                preds.flatten(1).sum(1)
                + truth.flatten(1).sum(1)
            ).float()
            dice_per_image = (
                (2 * intersections + 1e-6)
                / (denominators + 1e-6)
            )
            metrics = {
--- PAGE 101 ---
                "epoch": epoch,
                "optimizer_steps": epoch * 2,
                "eval_loss": loss.item(),
                "bce": bce.item(),
                "soft_dice_loss": soft_dice.item(),
                "micro_dice_05": (
                    2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1)
                ),
                "tampered_dice_05": (
                    dice_per_image[positive].mean().item()
                ),
                "authentic_fp_pct": (
                    100 * preds[:8].float().mean().item()
                ),
            }
            for position, index in enumerate(indices):
                if position < 8:
                    continue
                gt = truth[position]
                record = {
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "dataset_index": index,
                    "filename": expected_names[position],
                    "dice_05": dice_per_image[position].item(),
                    "gt_foreground_pct": (
                        100 * masks[position].mean().item()
                    ),
                    "predicted_positive_pct": (
                        100 * preds[position].float().mean().item()
                    ),
                    "prob_in_mask": probs[position][gt].mean().item(),
                    "prob_background": (
                        probs[position][~gt].mean().item()
                    ),
                }
                per_image_history.append(record)
                if index in (7700, 8693):
                    metrics[f"dice_{index}"] = record["dice_05"]
            if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in metrics.values()):
                raise RuntimeError("Evaluation không hữu hạn.")
            return metrics
        # Giữ đúng hai batch của thí nghiệm 16 ảnh trước.
        batches = [
            [0, 1, 2, 3, 8, 9, 10, 11],
            [4, 5, 6, 7, 12, 13, 14, 15],
        ]
        for epoch in range(EPOCHS + 1):
            if epoch > 0:
                model.train()
                for batch_indices in batches:
                    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                    logits = model(images[batch_indices])
                    loss, _, _ = loss_fn(
                        logits, masks[batch_indices]
                    )
                    if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                        raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite loss")
                    loss.backward()
                    for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                        if not parameter.requires_grad:
                            continue
                        if parameter.grad is None:
                            raise RuntimeError(f"Thiếu gradient: {name}")
                        if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                            raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
--- PAGE 102 ---
                    optimizer.step()
                    del logits, loss
            if epoch in LOG_EPOCHS:
                metrics = evaluate(epoch)
                history.append(metrics)
                print(
                    f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | "
                    f"Steps {epoch * 2:03d} | "
                    f"Loss {metrics['eval_loss']:.4f} | "
                    f"Micro Dice {metrics['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                    f"Dice 7700 {metrics['dice_7700']:.4f} | "
                    f"Dice 8693 {metrics['dice_8693']:.4f}"
                )
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        model.eval()
        del optimizer
    return (
        model,
        images,
        masks,
        pd.DataFrame(history),
        pd.DataFrame(per_image_history),
        indices,
    )

def compare_learning_rate_decay(images_input, masks_input, indices):
    SEED = 42
    SPLIT_EPOCH = 150
    FINAL_EPOCH = 300
    expected_indices = [
        2347, 3403, 856, 3032, 2462, 2241, 2008, 1985,
        7700, 8281, 6166, 8448, 6811, 7071, 8693, 4762,
    ]
    if list(indices) != expected_indices:
        raise RuntimeError("Bộ ảnh không trùng với thí nghiệm 16 ảnh trước.")

Model chẩn đoán được giữ trong fixed16_model.
Không cập nhật V4/B/C hoặc ghi checkpoint.    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    # Clone để không thay đổi các tensor của thí nghiệm trước.
    images = images_input.detach().clone().to(
        device, dtype=torch.float32
    )
    masks = masks_input.detach().clone().to(
        device, dtype=torch.float32
    )
    assert images.shape == (16, 3, 256, 256)
    assert masks.shape == (16, 1, 256, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(images).all().item()
    assert torch.isfinite(masks).all().item()
    assert ((masks == 0) | (masks == 1)).all().item()
    positive = masks.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
    assert not positive[:8].any().item()
    assert positive[8:].all().item()
    print("Device:", device)
    print(f"GT foreground: {100 * masks.mean().item():.4f}%")
    batches = [
        [0, 1, 2, 3, 8, 9, 10, 11],
        [4, 5, 6, 7, 12, 13, 14, 15],
    ]
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_fn(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        targets = targets.float()
        bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        dice_loss = 1 - (
            (2 * intersection + 1.0)
            / (denominator + 1.0)
        )
        return bce + dice_loss
    def train_epoch(model, optimizer):
        model.train()
        for batch_indices in batches:
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            logits = model(images[batch_indices])
            loss = loss_fn(logits, masks[batch_indices])
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite loss")
            loss.backward()
            for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                if not parameter.requires_grad:
                    continue
                if parameter.grad is None:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Thiếu gradient: {name}")
                if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
            optimizer.step()
    @torch.no_grad()
    def evaluate(model, epoch, phase, lr):
        model.eval()
        logits = model(images)
--- PAGE 106 ---
        loss = loss_fn(logits, masks)
        pred = logits.sigmoid() > 0.5
        truth = masks.bool()
        tp = (pred & truth).sum().item()
        fp = (pred & ~truth).sum().item()
        fn = (~pred & truth).sum().item()
        intersection = (pred & truth).flatten(1).sum(1).float()
        denominator = (
            pred.flatten(1).sum(1)
            + truth.flatten(1).sum(1)
        ).float()
        per_image = (
            (2 * intersection + 1e-6)
            / (denominator + 1e-6)
        )
        row = {
            "phase": phase,
            "epoch": epoch,
            "optimizer_steps": epoch * 2,
            "lr": lr,
            "eval_loss": loss.item(),
            "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "tampered_dice_05": per_image[positive].mean().item(),
            "authentic_fp_pct": (
                100 * pred[:8].float().mean().item()
            ),
        }
        # Lưu Dice của toàn bộ 8 ảnh tampered.
        for position in range(8, 16):
            row[f"dice_{indices[position]}"] = per_image[position].item()
        numeric_values = [
            value for value in row.values()
            if isinstance(value, (int, float))
        ]
        if not all(np.isfinite(value) for value in numeric_values):
            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation metrics")
        print(
            f"{phase:6s} | Epoch {epoch:03d} | LR {lr:.0e} | "
            f"Loss {row['eval_loss']:.4f} | "
            f"Micro {row['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
            f"7700 {row['dice_7700']:.4f} | "
            f"8693 {row['dice_8693']:.4f}"
        )
        return row
    def cpu_snapshot(value):
        """Sao chép state sang CPU để giữ riêng từng nhánh."""
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_snapshot(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()]
        if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    # Lưu và khôi phục thiết lập của notebook sau thí nghiệm.
    old_deterministic = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn_only = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_deterministic = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    history = []
--- PAGE 107 ---
    final_states = {}
    try:
        # Giảm tính không xác định; không bảo đảm giống nhau giữa
        # các phiên bản PyTorch hoặc phần cứng khác nhau.
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
            torch.manual_seed(SEED)
            model = UNet(
                in_channels=3,
                out_channels=1,
                base_channels=16,
            ).to(device)
            optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                model.parameters(),
                lr=1e-3,
                weight_decay=1e-4,
            )
            print("\n=== CHUNG: 150 LƯỢT ĐẦU ===")
            history.append(evaluate(model, 0, "SHARED", 1e-3))
            for epoch in range(1, SPLIT_EPOCH + 1):
                train_epoch(model, optimizer)
                if epoch % 10 == 0:
                    history.append(
                        evaluate(model, epoch, "SHARED", 1e-3)
                    )
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            # Cả hai nhánh bắt đầu từ cùng weights và AdamW state.
            split_model_state = cpu_snapshot(model.state_dict())
            split_optimizer_state = cpu_snapshot(optimizer.state_dict())
            split_cpu_rng = torch.get_rng_state().clone()
            split_cuda_rng = (
                torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device).clone()
                if device.type == "cuda" else None
            )
            del model, optimizer
            gc.collect()
            if device.type == "cuda":
                torch.cuda.empty_cache()
            for branch, learning_rate in [
                ("A", 1e-3),
                ("B", 3e-4),
            ]:
                print(
                    f"\n=== NHÁNH {branch}: "
                    f"LR={learning_rate} TỪ LƯỢT 151 ==="
                )
                model = UNet(
                    in_channels=3,
                    out_channels=1,
                    base_channels=16,
                ).to(device)
                model.load_state_dict(split_model_state)
                optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                    model.parameters(),
                    lr=1e-3,
                    weight_decay=1e-4,
                )
                optimizer.load_state_dict(
                    copy.deepcopy(split_optimizer_state)
                )
                # Phải đổi LR sau khi load optimizer state.
--- PAGE 108 ---
                for group in optimizer.param_groups:
                    group["lr"] = learning_rate
                torch.set_rng_state(split_cpu_rng)
                if device.type == "cuda":
                    torch.cuda.set_rng_state(split_cuda_rng, device)
                # Hai dòng epoch 150 phải có metric giống nhau.
                history.append(
                    evaluate(
                        model, SPLIT_EPOCH, branch, learning_rate
                    )
                )
                for epoch in range(
                    SPLIT_EPOCH + 1, FINAL_EPOCH + 1
                ):
                    train_epoch(model, optimizer)
                    if epoch % 10 == 0:
                        history.append(
                            evaluate(
                                model, epoch, branch, learning_rate
                            )
                        )
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                final_states[branch] = cpu_snapshot(model.state_dict())
                del model, optimizer
                gc.collect()
                if device.type == "cuda":
                    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    finally:
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_deterministic, warn_only=old_warn_only
        )
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_deterministic
    return pd.DataFrame(history), final_states

    )
    axes[1].plot(
        data["epoch"], data["dice_7700"], label=phase
    )
    axes[2].plot(
        data["epoch"], data["dice_8693"], label=phase
    )
for ax, title in zip(
    axes,
    ["Micro Dice", "Dice ảnh 7700", "Dice ảnh 8693"],
):
    ax.set_title(title)
    ax.set_xlabel("Epoch")
    ax.set_ylim(0, 1)
    ax.axvline(150, color="gray", linestyle="--", alpha=0.6)
    ax.grid(alpha=0.3)
    ax.legend()
plt.tight_layout()
plt.show()

# ============================================================
# DIAGNOSTIC: 32 FIXED TRAIN IMAGES
# - Keep the previous 16 images
# - Add 8 authentic + 8 tampered from the SAME 256-image subset
# - Fresh U-Net, LR=1e-3, weighted BCE + GLOBAL Dice
# - 4 fixed balanced batches: 4 authentic + 4 tampered each
# - Save separate diagnostic checkpoints for resuming
# ============================================================
import copy
import gc
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from IPython.display import display
from torch.utils.data import Subset
from forgery_localization.models.unet import UNet
--- PAGE 111 ---
# ------------------------------------------------------------
# USER SETTINGS
# ------------------------------------------------------------
TOTAL_EPOCHS = 300
# First run: leave None.
# To resume: paste the exact last.pt path printed by this cell.
RESUME_PATH = None
# /content is temporary storage.
# Change to a mounted Google Drive directory if persistence is needed.
OUTPUT_ROOT = Path("/content/diagnostics")
def run_diagnostic32(source_loader, total_epochs, output_root, resume_path=None):
    SEED = 42
    LR = 1e-3
    WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4
    LOG_EVERY = 10
    old_indices = [
        2347, 3403, 856, 3032, 2462, 2241, 2008, 1985,
        7700, 8281, 6166, 8448, 6811, 7071, 8693, 4762,
    ]
    old_names = [
        "Au_cha_30565.jpg",
        "Au_pla_20011.jpg",
        "Au_arc_30094.jpg",
        "Au_nat_30260.jpg",
        "Au_ind_00025.jpg",
        "Au_cha_30376.jpg",
        "Au_cha_10175.jpg",
        "Au_cha_10150.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NNN_S_N_cha10140_cha10140_12198.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRD_S_N_ind00028_ind00028_00895.tif",
        "Tp_D_NRN_S_N_pla00008_pla00009_10936.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_arc00051_arc00051_11208.jpg",
        "Tp_S_CRN_S_B_arc00095_arc00095_01035.tif",
        "Tp_S_NND_S_N_sec20028_sec20028_01618.tif",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_pla20022_pla20022_01922.tif",
        "Tp_D_CRN_M_O_art10113_art10112_11560.jpg",
    ]
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    source = source_loader.dataset
    if not isinstance(source, Subset) or len(source) != 256:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "train_loader phải chứa Subset TRAIN 256 ảnh của B/C."
        )
    def resolve_sample(dataset, index):
        while True:
            if isinstance(dataset, Subset):
                index = int(dataset.indices[index])
                dataset = dataset.dataset
            elif hasattr(dataset, "base_dataset"):
                dataset = dataset.base_dataset
            else:
                return dataset, index
    references = [resolve_sample(source, i) for i in range(256)]
    base = references[0][0]
    if not hasattr(base, "records"):
        raise RuntimeError("Dataset gốc không có .records.")
    if any(dataset is not base for dataset, _ in references):
        raise RuntimeError("Subset phải dùng cùng một dataset gốc.")
    source_ids = [index for _, index in references]
--- PAGE 112 ---
    if len(set(source_ids)) != 256:
        raise RuntimeError("Subset TRAIN chứa index trùng nhau.")
    source_ids_set = set(source_ids)
    if not set(old_indices).issubset(source_ids_set):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Subset 256 hiện tại không chứa đủ 16 ảnh cũ. "
            "Không tiếp tục với một subset khác."
        )
    for index, expected in zip(old_indices, old_names):
        actual = Path(base.records[index]["image_path"]).name
        if actual != expected:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Index {index}: {actual} khác {expected}."
            )
    def label_of(index):
        label = str(base.records[index]["label"]).strip().lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(label, label)
    # Select additions only from the remaining 256-image TRAIN subset.
    remaining = sorted(source_ids_set - set(old_indices))
    auth_candidates = [
        i for i in remaining if label_of(i) == "authentic"
    ]
    tamp_candidates = [
        i for i in remaining if label_of(i) == "tampered"
    ]
    if min(len(auth_candidates), len(tamp_candidates)) < 8:
        raise RuntimeError("Không đủ ảnh để thêm 8 authentic + 8 tampered.")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    added_auth = rng.choice(
        auth_candidates, 8, replace=False
    ).tolist()
    added_tamp = rng.choice(
        tamp_candidates, 8, replace=False
    ).tolist()
    # Positions 0–15 are authentic; 16–31 are tampered.
    # First two batches preserve the previous 16-image grouping.
    indices = (
        old_indices[:8] + added_auth
        + old_indices[8:] + added_tamp
    )
    assert len(indices) == len(set(indices)) == 32
    batches = [
        list(range(start, start + 4))
        + list(range(16 + start, 20 + start))
        for start in (0, 4, 8, 12)
    ]
    samples = [base[i] for i in indices]
    images_cpu = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).float()
    masks_cpu = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).float()
    del samples
    assert images_cpu.shape == (32, 3, 256, 256)
    assert masks_cpu.shape == (32, 1, 256, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(images_cpu).all().item()
    assert torch.isfinite(masks_cpu).all().item()
    assert 0 <= images_cpu.min().item() <= images_cpu.max().item() <= 1
    assert ((masks_cpu == 0) | (masks_cpu == 1)).all().item()
    positive = masks_cpu.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
    assert not positive[:16].any().item()
    assert positive[16:].all().item()
    # Fingerprint the actual preprocessed tensors for safe resume.
    import hashlib
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    digest.update(images_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    digest.update(masks_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    tensor_hash = digest.hexdigest()
--- PAGE 113 ---
    sample_rows = []
    for position, index in enumerate(indices):
        sample_rows.append({
            "position": position,
            "dataset_index": index,
            "filename": Path(base.records[index]["image_path"]).name,
            "label": label_of(index),
            "selection": "previous16" if index in old_indices else "added16",
            "gt_foreground_pct": (
                100 * masks_cpu[position].mean().item()
            ),
        })
    print("Images: 32 | Batches per epoch: 4")
    print("Maximum optimizer steps:", total_epochs * 4)
    print(f"GT foreground: {100 * masks_cpu.mean().item():.4f}%")
    display(pd.DataFrame(sample_rows))
    config = {
        "seed": SEED,
        "lr": LR,
        "weight_decay": WEIGHT_DECAY,
        "base_channels": 16,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,
        "threshold": 0.5,
        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_global_dice_smooth1",
        "indices": indices,
        "batches": batches,
        "tensor_sha256": tensor_hash,
    }
    if resume_path is None:
        run_dir = Path(output_root) / (
            "fixed32_" + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
        )
        run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        saved = None
    else:
        resume_path = Path(resume_path)
        saved = torch.load(
            resume_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
        )
        if saved.get("kind") != "fixed32_diagnostic_v1":
            raise RuntimeError("Checkpoint không phải của cell chẩn đoán này.")
        if saved["config"] != config:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Dữ liệu hoặc cấu hình khác checkpoint. Không resume."
            )
        if saved["epoch"] > total_epochs:
            raise RuntimeError("TOTAL_EPOCHS nhỏ hơn epoch đã lưu.")
        run_dir = resume_path.parent
    pd.DataFrame(sample_rows).to_csv(
        run_dir / "selected_samples.csv", index=False
    )
    images = images_cpu.to(device)
    masks = masks_cpu.to(device)
    del images_cpu, masks_cpu
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        targets = targets.float()
        # Return sums so evaluation loss can be computed globally
        # across all 32 images, independently of evaluation batch size.
        bce_sum = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight, reduction="sum"
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        return bce_sum, intersection, denominator
--- PAGE 114 ---
    @torch.no_grad()
    def evaluate(model, epoch):
        model.eval()
        total_bce = total_intersection = total_denominator = 0.0
        pixel_count = 0
        tp = fp = fn = 0
        auth_pred = auth_pixels = 0
        details = []
        # Evaluate in chunks to reduce GPU memory use.
        for start in range(0, 32, 8):
            stop = start + 8
            targets = masks[start:stop]
            logits = model(images[start:stop])
            bce_sum, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(
                logits, targets
            )
            total_bce += bce_sum.item()
            total_intersection += intersection.item()
            total_denominator += denominator.item()
            pixel_count += targets.numel()
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            pred = probs > 0.5
            truth = targets.bool()
            tp += (pred & truth).sum().item()
            fp += (pred & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn += (~pred & truth).sum().item()
            for local in range(stop - start):
                position = start + local
                gt = truth[local]
                prediction = pred[local]
                is_tampered = position >= 16
                if is_tampered:
                    intersection_i = (prediction & gt).sum().item()
                    denominator_i = (
                        prediction.sum().item() + gt.sum().item()
                    )
                    dice = 2 * intersection_i / denominator_i
                    prob_inside = probs[local][gt].mean().item()
                else:
                    dice = None
                    prob_inside = None
                    auth_pred += prediction.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += prediction.numel()
                details.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    **sample_rows[position],
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": (
                        100 * prediction.float().mean().item()
                    ),
                    "prob_in_mask": prob_inside,
                })
        bce = total_bce / pixel_count
        soft_dice_loss = 1 - (
            (2 * total_intersection + 1)
            / (total_denominator + 1)
        )
        tampered = [r for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        dice_lookup = {
            r["dataset_index"]: r["dice_05"] for r in tampered
        }
        result = {
            "epoch": epoch,
            "optimizer_steps": epoch * 4,
            "eval_loss": bce + soft_dice_loss,
            "bce": bce,
            "soft_dice_loss": soft_dice_loss,
            "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
--- PAGE 115 ---
            "tampered_dice_05": float(
                np.mean([r["dice_05"] for r in tampered])
            ),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(
                r["dice_05"] < 0.1 for r in tampered
            ),
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_pred / max(auth_pixels, 1),
            "dice_7700": dice_lookup[7700],
            "dice_8693": dice_lookup[8693],
        }
        if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in result.values()):
            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation metrics")
        return result, details
    def cpu_snapshot(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_snapshot(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def atomic_save(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    old_deterministic = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn_only = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_deterministic = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    history = []
    per_image_history = []
    best_macro = -1.0
    start_epoch = 0
    print("\nOutput directory:", run_dir)
    print("Fresh training" if saved is None else "Resuming training")
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
            torch.manual_seed(SEED)
            model = UNet(
                in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
            ).to(device)
            optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY
            )
            if saved is not None:
                model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
                optimizer.load_state_dict(saved["optimizer_state"])
                history = saved["history"]
                per_image_history = saved["per_image_history"]
                best_macro = saved["best_macro"]
                start_epoch = saved["epoch"] + 1
                torch.set_rng_state(saved["torch_rng"])
                if device.type == "cuda" and saved["cuda_rng"] is not None:
                    torch.cuda.set_rng_state(saved["cuda_rng"], device)
                del saved
            for epoch in range(start_epoch, total_epochs + 1):
--- PAGE 116 ---
                if epoch > 0:
                    model.train()
                    for batch_indices in batches:
                        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                        targets = masks[batch_indices]
                        logits = model(images[batch_indices])
                        bce_sum, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(
                            logits, targets
                        )
                        loss = (
                            bce_sum / targets.numel()
                            + 1
                            - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
                        )
                        if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite training loss")
                        loss.backward()
                        for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                            if not parameter.requires_grad:
                                continue
                            if parameter.grad is None:
                                raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                            if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                                raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                        optimizer.step()
                        del logits, loss, bce_sum, intersection, denominator
                if epoch % LOG_EVERY == 0 or epoch == total_epochs:
                    metrics, details = evaluate(model, epoch)
                    history.append(metrics)
                    per_image_history.extend(details)
                    print(
                        f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Steps {epoch * 4:04d} | "
                        f"Loss {metrics['eval_loss']:.4f} | "
                        f"Micro {metrics['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                        f"Mean Tp {metrics['tampered_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                        f"Tp Dice<0.1: {metrics['tampered_dice_below_01']}/16 | "
                        f"7700 {metrics['dice_7700']:.4f} | "
                        f"8693 {metrics['dice_8693']:.4f}"
                    )
                    model_state = cpu_snapshot(model.state_dict())
                    # Diagnostic selection based on TRAIN metrics only.
                    # This is NOT a model selected for final research evaluation.
                    if metrics["tampered_dice_05"] > best_macro:
                        best_macro = metrics["tampered_dice_05"]
                        atomic_save({
                            "kind": "fixed32_diagnostic_best_weights",
                            "epoch": epoch,
                            "config": config,
                            "metrics": metrics,
                            "model_state": model_state,
                        }, run_dir / "best_train_macro.pt")
                    atomic_save({
                        "kind": "fixed32_diagnostic_v1",
                        "epoch": epoch,
                        "config": config,
                        "model_state": model_state,
                        "optimizer_state": cpu_snapshot(optimizer.state_dict()),
                        "history": history,
                        "per_image_history": per_image_history,
                        "best_macro": best_macro,
                        "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                        "cuda_rng": (
                            torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                            if device.type == "cuda" else None
                        ),
                    }, run_dir / "last.pt")
--- PAGE 117 ---
                    pd.DataFrame(history).to_csv(
                        run_dir / "history.csv", index=False
                    )
                    pd.DataFrame(per_image_history).to_csv(
                        run_dir / "per_image_history.csv", index=False
                    )
                    del model_state
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            model.eval()
    finally:
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_deterministic, warn_only=old_warn_only
        )
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_deterministic
    return (
        model,
        pd.DataFrame(history),
        pd.DataFrame(per_image_history),
        run_dir,
    )

# ------------------------------------------------------------
# USER SETTINGS
# ------------------------------------------------------------
TOTAL_EPOCHS = 300
# First run: leave None.
# To resume: paste the exact last.pt path printed by this cell.
RESUME_PATH = None
# /content is temporary storage.
# Change to a mounted Google Drive directory if persistence is needed.
OUTPUT_ROOT = Path("/content/diagnostics")
def run_diagnostic32(source_loader, total_epochs, output_root, resume_path=None):
    SEED = 42
    LR = 1e-3
    WEIGHT_DECAY = 1e-4
    LOG_EVERY = 10
    old_indices = [
        2347, 3403, 856, 3032, 2462, 2241, 2008, 1985,
        7700, 8281, 6166, 8448, 6811, 7071, 8693, 4762,
    ]
    old_names = [
        "Au_cha_30565.jpg",
        "Au_pla_20011.jpg",
        "Au_arc_30094.jpg",
        "Au_nat_30260.jpg",
        "Au_ind_00025.jpg",
        "Au_cha_30376.jpg",
        "Au_cha_10175.jpg",
        "Au_cha_10150.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NNN_S_N_cha10140_cha10140_12198.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRD_S_N_ind00028_ind00028_00895.tif",
        "Tp_D_NRN_S_N_pla00008_pla00009_10936.jpg",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_arc00051_arc00051_11208.jpg",
        "Tp_S_CRN_S_B_arc00095_arc00095_01035.tif",
        "Tp_S_NND_S_N_sec20028_sec20028_01618.tif",
        "Tp_S_NRN_S_N_pla20022_pla20022_01922.tif",
        "Tp_D_CRN_M_O_art10113_art10112_11560.jpg",
    ]
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    source = source_loader.dataset
    if not isinstance(source, Subset) or len(source) != 256:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "train_loader phải chứa Subset TRAIN 256 ảnh của B/C."
        )
    def resolve_sample(dataset, index):
        while True:
            if isinstance(dataset, Subset):
                index = int(dataset.indices[index])
                dataset = dataset.dataset
            elif hasattr(dataset, "base_dataset"):
                dataset = dataset.base_dataset
            else:
                return dataset, index
    references = [resolve_sample(source, i) for i in range(256)]
    base = references[0][0]
    if not hasattr(base, "records"):
        raise RuntimeError("Dataset gốc không có .records.")
    if any(dataset is not base for dataset, _ in references):
        raise RuntimeError("Subset phải dùng cùng một dataset gốc.")
    source_ids = [index for _, index in references]
--- PAGE 121 ---
CSV history: /content/diagnostics/fixed32_20261116_102543_622777/history.csv
Model giữ trong RAM: diag32_model
Không cập nhật V4/B/C hoặc test split.
    if len(set(source_ids)) != 256:
        raise RuntimeError("Subset TRAIN chứa index trùng nhau.")
    source_ids_set = set(source_ids)
    if not set(old_indices).issubset(source_ids_set):
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Subset 256 hiện tại không chứa đủ 16 ảnh cũ. "
            "Không tiếp tục với một subset khác."
        )
    for index, expected in zip(old_indices, old_names):
        actual = Path(base.records[index]["image_path"]).name
        if actual != expected:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Index {index}: {actual} khác {expected}."
            )
    def label_of(index):
        label = str(base.records[index]["label"]).strip().lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(label, label)
    # Select additions only from the remaining 256-image TRAIN subset.
    remaining = sorted(source_ids_set - set(old_indices))
    auth_candidates = [
        i for i in remaining if label_of(i) == "authentic"
    ]
    tamp_candidates = [
        i for i in remaining if label_of(i) == "tampered"
    ]
    if min(len(auth_candidates), len(tamp_candidates)) < 8:
        raise RuntimeError("Không đủ ảnh để thêm 8 authentic + 8 tampered.")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
    added_auth = rng.choice(
        auth_candidates, 8, replace=False
    ).tolist()
    added_tamp = rng.choice(
        tamp_candidates, 8, replace=False
    ).tolist()
    # Positions 0–15 are authentic; 16–31 are tampered.
    # First two batches preserve the previous 16-image grouping.
    indices = (
        old_indices[:8] + added_auth
        + old_indices[8:] + added_tamp
    )
    assert len(indices) == len(set(indices)) == 32
    batches = [
        list(range(start, start + 4))
        + list(range(16 + start, 20 + start))
        for start in (0, 4, 8, 12)
    ]
    samples = [base[i] for i in indices]
    images_cpu = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).float()
    masks_cpu = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).float()
    del samples
    assert images_cpu.shape == (32, 3, 256, 256)
    assert masks_cpu.shape == (32, 1, 256, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(images_cpu).all().item()
    assert torch.isfinite(masks_cpu).all().item()
    assert 0 <= images_cpu.min().item() <= images_cpu.max().item() <= 1
    assert ((masks_cpu == 0) | (masks_cpu == 1)).all().item()
    positive = masks_cpu.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
    assert not positive[:16].any().item()
    assert positive[16:].all().item()
    # Fingerprint the actual preprocessed tensors for safe resume.
    import hashlib
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    digest.update(images_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    digest.update(masks_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    tensor_hash = digest.hexdigest()
--- PAGE 122 ---
    sample_rows = []
    for position, index in enumerate(indices):
        sample_rows.append({
            "position": position,
            "dataset_index": index,
            "filename": Path(base.records[index]["image_path"]).name,
            "label": label_of(index),
            "selection": "previous16" if index in old_indices else "added16",
            "gt_foreground_pct": (
                100 * masks_cpu[position].mean().item()
            ),
        })
    print("Images: 32 | Batches per epoch: 4")
    print("Maximum optimizer steps:", total_epochs * 4)
    print(f"GT foreground: {100 * masks_cpu.mean().item():.4f}%")
    display(pd.DataFrame(sample_rows))
    config = {
        "seed": SEED,
        "lr": LR,
        "weight_decay": WEIGHT_DECAY,
        "base_channels": 16,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,
        "threshold": 0.5,
        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_global_dice_smooth1",
        "indices": indices,
        "batches": batches,
        "tensor_sha256": tensor_hash,
    }
    if resume_path is None:
        run_dir = Path(output_root) / (
            "fixed32_" + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
        )
        run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        saved = None
    else:
        resume_path = Path(resume_path)
        saved = torch.load(
            resume_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
        )
        if saved.get("kind") != "fixed32_diagnostic_v1":
            raise RuntimeError("Checkpoint không phải của cell chẩn đoán này.")
        if saved["config"] != config:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Dữ liệu hoặc cấu hình khác checkpoint. Không resume."
            )
        if saved["epoch"] > total_epochs:
            raise RuntimeError("TOTAL_EPOCHS nhỏ hơn epoch đã lưu.")
        run_dir = resume_path.parent
    pd.DataFrame(sample_rows).to_csv(
        run_dir / "selected_samples.csv", index=False
    )
    images = images_cpu.to(device)
    masks = masks_cpu.to(device)
    del images_cpu, masks_cpu
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        targets = targets.float()
        # Return sums so evaluation loss can be computed globally
        # across all 32 images, independently of evaluation batch size.
        bce_sum = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight, reduction="sum"
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        return bce_sum, intersection, denominator
--- PAGE 123 ---
    @torch.no_grad()
    def evaluate(model, epoch):
        model.eval()
        total_bce = total_intersection = total_denominator = 0.0
        pixel_count = 0
        tp = fp = fn = 0
        auth_pred = auth_pixels = 0
        details = []
        # Evaluate in chunks to reduce GPU memory use.
        for start in range(0, 32, 8):
            stop = start + 8
            targets = masks[start:stop]
            logits = model(images[start:stop])
            bce_sum, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(
                logits, targets
            )
            total_bce += bce_sum.item()
            total_intersection += intersection.item()
            total_denominator += denominator.item()
            pixel_count += targets.numel()
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            pred = probs > 0.5
            truth = targets.bool()
            tp += (pred & truth).sum().item()
            fp += (pred & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn += (~pred & truth).sum().item()
            for local in range(stop - start):
                position = start + local
                gt = truth[local]
                prediction = pred[local]
                is_tampered = position >= 16
                if is_tampered:
                    intersection_i = (prediction & gt).sum().item()
                    denominator_i = (
                        prediction.sum().item() + gt.sum().item()
                    )
                    dice = 2 * intersection_i / denominator_i
                    prob_inside = probs[local][gt].mean().item()
                else:
                    dice = None
                    prob_inside = None
                    auth_pred += prediction.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += prediction.numel()
                details.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    **sample_rows[position],
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": (
                        100 * prediction.float().mean().item()
                    ),
                    "prob_in_mask": prob_inside,
                })
        bce = total_bce / pixel_count
        soft_dice_loss = 1 - (
            (2 * total_intersection + 1)
            / (total_denominator + 1)
        )
        tampered = [r for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        dice_lookup = {
            r["dataset_index"]: r["dice_05"] for r in tampered
        }
        result = {
            "epoch": epoch,
            "optimizer_steps": epoch * 4,
            "eval_loss": bce + soft_dice_loss,
            "bce": bce,
            "soft_dice_loss": soft_dice_loss,
            "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
--- PAGE 124 ---
            "tampered_dice_05": float(
                np.mean([r["dice_05"] for r in tampered])
            ),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(
                r["dice_05"] < 0.1 for r in tampered
            ),
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_pred / max(auth_pixels, 1),
            "dice_7700": dice_lookup[7700],
            "dice_8693": dice_lookup[8693],
        }
        if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in result.values()):
            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation metrics")
        return result, details
    def cpu_snapshot(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_snapshot(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_snapshot(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def atomic_save(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    old_deterministic = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn_only = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_deterministic = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    history = []
    per_image_history = []
    best_macro = -1.0
    start_epoch = 0
    print("\nOutput directory:", run_dir)
    print("Fresh training" if saved is None else "Resuming training")
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
            torch.manual_seed(SEED)
            model = UNet(
                in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
            ).to(device)
            optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=WEIGHT_DECAY
            )
            if saved is not None:
                model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
                optimizer.load_state_dict(saved["optimizer_state"])
                history = saved["history"]
                per_image_history = saved["per_image_history"]
                best_macro = saved["best_macro"]
                start_epoch = saved["epoch"] + 1
                torch.set_rng_state(saved["torch_rng"])
                if device.type == "cuda" and saved["cuda_rng"] is not None:
                    torch.cuda.set_rng_state(saved["cuda_rng"], device)
                del saved
            for epoch in range(start_epoch, total_epochs + 1):
--- PAGE 125 ---
                if epoch > 0:
                    model.train()
                    for batch_indices in batches:
                        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                        targets = masks[batch_indices]
                        logits = model(images[batch_indices])
                        bce_sum, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(
                            logits, targets
                        )
                        loss = (
                            bce_sum / targets.numel()
                            + 1
                            - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
                        )
                        if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite training loss")
                        loss.backward()
                        for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                            if not parameter.requires_grad:
                                continue
                            if parameter.grad is None:
                                raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                            if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                                raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                        optimizer.step()
                        del logits, loss, bce_sum, intersection, denominator
                if epoch % LOG_EVERY == 0 or epoch == total_epochs:
                    metrics, details = evaluate(model, epoch)
                    history.append(metrics)
                    per_image_history.extend(details)
                    print(
                        f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Steps {epoch * 4:04d} | "
                        f"Loss {metrics['eval_loss']:.4f} | "
                        f"Micro {metrics['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                        f"Mean Tp {metrics['tampered_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                        f"Tp Dice<0.1: {metrics['tampered_dice_below_01']}/16 | "
                        f"7700 {metrics['dice_7700']:.4f} | "
                        f"8693 {metrics['dice_8693']:.4f}"
                    )
                    model_state = cpu_snapshot(model.state_dict())
                    # Diagnostic selection based on TRAIN metrics only.
                    # This is NOT a model selected for final research evaluation.
                    if metrics["tampered_dice_05"] > best_macro:
                        best_macro = metrics["tampered_dice_05"]
                        atomic_save({
                            "kind": "fixed32_diagnostic_best_weights",
                            "epoch": epoch,
                            "config": config,
                            "metrics": metrics,
                            "model_state": model_state,
                        }, run_dir / "best_train_macro.pt")
                    atomic_save({
                        "kind": "fixed32_diagnostic_v1",
                        "epoch": epoch,
                        "config": config,
                        "model_state": model_state,
                        "optimizer_state": cpu_snapshot(optimizer.state_dict()),
                        "history": history,
                        "per_image_history": per_image_history,
                        "best_macro": best_macro,
                        "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                        "cuda_rng": (
                            torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                            if device.type == "cuda" else None
                        ),
                    }, run_dir / "last.pt")
--- PAGE 126 ---
                    pd.DataFrame(history).to_csv(
                        run_dir / "history.csv", index=False
                    )
                    pd.DataFrame(per_image_history).to_csv(
                        run_dir / "per_image_history.csv", index=False
                    )
                    del model_state
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            model.eval()
    finally:
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_deterministic, warn_only=old_warn_only
        )
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_deterministic
    return (
        model,
        pd.DataFrame(history),
        pd.DataFrame(per_image_history),
        run_dir,
    )

for root in search_roots:
    if not root.exists():
        continue
    for current, directories, filenames in os.walk(root):
        directories[:] = [
            name for name in directories if name not in skip_dirs
        ]
        for filename in filenames:
            if filename not in wanted:
                continue
            path = Path(current) / filename
            identity = str(path.resolve())
            if identity not in seen:
                seen.add(identity)
                found.append(path)
print("=== FILES FOUND ===")
for path in found:
    print(path)
print("\n=== CHECKPOINT IDENTIFICATION ===")
diagnostic_checkpoints = []
for path in found:
    if path.suffix != ".pt":
        continue
    try:
        # Chỉ đọc checkpoint của bạn, không nạp vào model.
        checkpoint = torch.load(
            path,
            map_location="cpu",
            weights_only=True,
        )
        if not isinstance(checkpoint, dict):
            print(path, "→ Không phải checkpoint dictionary")
            continue
        kind = checkpoint.get("kind", "không có trường kind")
        epoch = checkpoint.get("epoch", "không rõ")
        print(f"\nFile: {path}")
        print("Kind:", kind)
        print("Epoch:", epoch)
        if kind == "fixed32_diagnostic_v1":
            diagnostic_checkpoints.append(path)
            print("ĐÚNG loại checkpoint chẩn đoán 32 ảnh.")
            print(
                "Số ảnh trong cấu hình:",
                len(checkpoint.get("config", {}).get("indices", [])),
            )
        del checkpoint
    except Exception as error:
        print(f"\nKhông đọc được {path}: {error}")
print("\n=== SUMMARY ===")
print(
    "Checkpoint fixed32 tìm thấy:",
    len(diagnostic_checkpoints),
)
if not diagnostic_checkpoints:
    print(
        "Chưa tìm thấy trong các thư mục đã quét. "
        "Kiểm tra bản tải xuống, Drive tài khoản cũ "
        "hoặc thư mục đã chia sẻ."
    )
print("Không train, không cập nhật model, không sửa file.")

from pathlib import Path
import json
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Subset
assert "train_dataset" in globals(), "Chạy startup trước."
assert "val_dataset" in globals(), "Chạy startup trước."
assert torch.cuda.is_available(), "Bật GPU trước khi train."
assert len(train_dataset) == 8765
assert len(val_dataset) == 1768
SEED = 42
rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED)
# Giữ đúng thuật toán và thứ tự gọi RNG trong notebook trước.
def select_balanced(dataset, n_auth, n_tamp):
    auth = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(dataset.records)
        if str(r["label"]).lower() in ("authentic", "au")
    ]
    tamp = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(dataset.records)
        if str(r["label"]).lower() in ("tampered", "tp")
    ]
    assert len(auth) >= n_auth
    assert len(tamp) >= n_tamp
    selected = (
        rng.choice(auth, n_auth, replace=False).tolist()
        + rng.choice(tamp, n_tamp, replace=False).tolist()
    )
    rng.shuffle(selected)
    return selected
train_indices = select_balanced(train_dataset, 128, 128)
val_indices = select_balanced(val_dataset, 64, 64)
# Kiểm tra cách chọn 32 ảnh của cell chẩn đoán trước.
old16 = [

    7700, 8281, 6166, 8448, 6811, 7071, 8693, 4762,
]
