    "Subset tái tạo không chứa đủ 16 ảnh cũ. Dừng và gửi lỗi."
)

def save_selection(dataset, indices, filename):
    rows = []
    for index in indices:
        record = dataset.records[index]
        rows.append({
            "dataset_index": index,
            "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
            "image_path": str(record["image_path"]),
            "mask_path": (
                str(record["mask_path"])
                if record["mask_path"] else ""
            ),
            "label": record["label"],
            "source_group": record["source_group"],
        })
    pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(
        selection_dir / filename, index=False
    )
save_selection(train_dataset, train_indices, "train256.csv")
save_selection(val_dataset, val_indices, "validation128.csv")
save_selection(train_dataset, expected32, "diagnostic32.csv")

    json.dumps({
        "seed": SEED,
        "status": "Reconstructed using the earlier selection algorithm",
        "validation_matches_previous_run": "Not independently verified",
    }, indent=2),
    encoding="utf-8",
)

import copy
import gc
import hashlib
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

from IPython.display import display
from forgery_localization.models.unet import UNet
# -------------------- SETTINGS --------------------
SELECTION_DIR = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/diagnostics/"
    "restored_selection_20261116_111713_399032"
)
OUTPUT_ROOT = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/diagnostics"
)
TOTAL_EPOCHS = 300
# First run: None.
# To resume: replace None with the last.pt path printed by this cell.
RESUME_PATH = None
def run_fixed32_again(
    dataset, selection_dir, output_root, total_epochs=300, resume_path=None
):
    seed = 42
    lr = 1e-3
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    selection_path = selection_dir / "diagnostic32.csv"
    if not selection_path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(selection_path)
    selection = pd.read_csv(selection_path, keep_default_na=False)
    indices = selection["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
    assert len(indices) == len(set(indices)) == 32
    # Check file identities before reading the samples.
    for row in selection.itertuples(index=False):
        record = dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
        actual = Path(record["image_path"]).name
        if actual != row.filename:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Index {row.dataset_index}: {actual} != {row.filename}"
            )
        if str(record["label"]) != str(row.label):
            raise RuntimeError("Label changed since selection was saved.")
    # Load once: no changing input or random augmentation during training.
    samples = [dataset[index] for index in indices]
    images_cpu = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).float()
    masks_cpu = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).float()
    del samples
    assert images_cpu.shape == (32, 3, 256, 256)
    assert masks_cpu.shape == (32, 1, 256, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(images_cpu).all().item()
    assert torch.isfinite(masks_cpu).all().item()
    assert 0 <= images_cpu.min().item() <= images_cpu.max().item() <= 1
    assert ((masks_cpu == 0) | (masks_cpu == 1)).all().item()
    positive = masks_cpu.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
    assert not positive[:16].any().item()
    assert positive[16:].all().item()
    # Bind resume to the actual image/mask tensors.
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    digest.update(images_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    digest.update(masks_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
--- PAGE 134 ---
    config = {
        "seed": seed,
        "lr": lr,
        "weight_decay": 1e-4,
        "base_channels": 16,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,
        "threshold": 0.5,
        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_global_dice_smooth1",
        "indices": indices,
        "tensor_sha256": digest.hexdigest(),
    }
    if resume_path is None:
        run_dir = output_root / (
            "fixed32_rerun_"
            + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
        )
        run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        checkpoint = None
    else:
        resume_path = Path(resume_path)
        if resume_path.name != "last.pt":
            raise ValueError("Resume bằng last.pt, không dùng best_train_macro.pt.")
        checkpoint = torch.load(
            resume_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
        )
        if checkpoint.get("kind") != "fixed32_drive_v1":
            raise ValueError("Checkpoint không thuộc cell này.")
        if checkpoint["config"] != config:
            raise ValueError("Dữ liệu hoặc cấu hình đã thay đổi.")
        if checkpoint["epoch"] > total_epochs:
            raise ValueError("TOTAL_EPOCHS nhỏ hơn epoch đã lưu.")
        run_dir = resume_path.parent
    selection.to_csv(run_dir / "selected_samples.csv", index=False)
    # Keep the reconstructed validation identities beside the checkpoints.
    for filename in ("train256.csv", "validation128.csv", "notes.json"):
        source = selection_dir / filename
        if source.is_file():
            target = run_dir / filename
            if target.exists() and target.read_bytes() != source.read_bytes():
                raise RuntimeError(f"Selection backup mismatch: {filename}")
            if not target.exists():
                target.write_bytes(source.read_bytes())
    print("Images: 32 | Batches per epoch: 4")
    print(f"GT foreground: {100 * masks_cpu.mean().item():.4f}%")
    print("Output directory:", run_dir)
    print("Resume checkpoint:", run_dir / "last.pt")
    images = images_cpu.to(device)
    masks = masks_cpu.to(device)
    del images_cpu, masks_cpu
    # Same four balanced batches as the previous 32-image experiment.
    batches = [
        list(range(start, start + 4))
        + list(range(16 + start, 20 + start))
        for start in (0, 4, 8, 12)
    ]
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        targets = targets.float()
        bce_sum = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits,
            targets,
            pos_weight=pos_weight,
            reduction="sum",
        )
        probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
--- PAGE 135 ---
        intersection = (probabilities * targets).sum()
        denominator = probabilities.sum() + targets.sum()
        return bce_sum, intersection, denominator
    @torch.no_grad()
    def evaluate(model, epoch):
        model.eval()
        bce_total = intersection_total = denominator_total = 0.0
        pixel_count = 0
        tp = fp = fn = 0
        auth_predictions = auth_pixels = 0
        details = []
        for start in range(0, 32, 8):
            targets = masks[start:start + 8]
            logits = model(images[start:start + 8])
            if not torch.isfinite(logits).all().item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation logits.")
            bce_sum, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(logits, targets)
            bce_total += bce_sum.item()
            intersection_total += intersection.item()
            denominator_total += denominator.item()
            pixel_count += targets.numel()
            probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
            predictions = probabilities > 0.5
            truth = targets.bool()
            tp += (predictions & truth).sum().item()
            fp += (predictions & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn += (~predictions & truth).sum().item()
            for local in range(8):
                position = start + local
                pred = predictions[local]
                gt = truth[local]
                tampered = position >= 16
                if tampered:
                    numerator = 2 * (pred & gt).sum().item()
                    denominator_i = pred.sum().item() + gt.sum().item()
                    dice = numerator / denominator_i
                    probability_in_mask = probabilities[local][gt].mean().item()
                else:
                    dice = None
                    probability_in_mask = None
                    auth_predictions += pred.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += pred.numel()
                details.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "dataset_index": indices[position],
                    "filename": selection.iloc[position]["filename"],
                    "label": "tampered" if tampered else "authentic",
                    "gt_foreground_pct": (
                        100 * targets[local].mean().item()
                    ),
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": (
                        100 * pred.float().mean().item()
                    ),
                    "prob_in_mask": probability_in_mask,
                })
        tampered_rows = [r for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        lookup = {r["dataset_index"]: r["dice_05"] for r in tampered_rows}
        bce = bce_total / pixel_count
        soft_dice_loss = 1 - (
            (2 * intersection_total + 1)
            / (denominator_total + 1)
        )
--- PAGE 136 ---
        metrics = {
            "epoch": epoch,
            "optimizer_steps": epoch * 4,
            "eval_loss": bce + soft_dice_loss,
            "bce": bce,
            "soft_dice_loss": soft_dice_loss,
            "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "tampered_dice_05": float(
                np.mean([r["dice_05"] for r in tampered_rows])
            ),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(
                r["dice_05"] < 0.1 for r in tampered_rows
            ),
            "authentic_fp_pct": (
                100 * auth_predictions / max(auth_pixels, 1)
            ),
            "dice_7700": lookup[7700],
            "dice_8693": lookup[8693],
        }
        if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in metrics.values()):
            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation metrics.")
        return metrics, details
    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def save_checkpoint(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    old_deterministic = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn_only = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_deterministic = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    history = []
    per_image_history = []
    best_macro = -1.0
    start_epoch = 0
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
            torch.manual_seed(seed)
            model = UNet(
                in_channels=3,
                out_channels=1,
                base_channels=16,
            ).to(device)
            optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                model.parameters(),
                lr=lr,
                weight_decay=1e-4,
            )
            if checkpoint is not None:
                model.load_state_dict(checkpoint["model_state"])
                optimizer.load_state_dict(checkpoint["optimizer_state"])
--- PAGE 137 ---
                history = checkpoint["history"]
                per_image_history = checkpoint["per_image_history"]
                best_macro = checkpoint["best_macro"]
                start_epoch = checkpoint["epoch"] + 1
                torch.set_rng_state(checkpoint["torch_rng"])
                if device.type == "cuda" and checkpoint["cuda_rng"] is not None:
                    torch.cuda.set_rng_state(checkpoint["cuda_rng"], device)
                print("Resume từ epoch:", start_epoch - 1)
                del checkpoint
            else:
                print("Fresh training — model mới.")
            for epoch in range(start_epoch, total_epochs + 1):
                if epoch > 0:
                    model.train()
                    for batch_indices in batches:
                        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                        targets = masks[batch_indices]
                        logits = model(images[batch_indices])
                        bce_sum, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(
                            logits, targets
                        )
                        loss = (
                            bce_sum / targets.numel()
                            + 1
                            - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
                        )
                        if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite training loss.")
                        loss.backward()
                        for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                            if not parameter.requires_grad:
                                continue
                            if parameter.grad is None:
                                raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                            if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                                raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                        optimizer.step()
                        del logits, loss, bce_sum, intersection, denominator
                if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == total_epochs:
                    metrics, details = evaluate(model, epoch)
                    history.append(metrics)
                    per_image_history.extend(details)
                    state = cpu_copy(model.state_dict())
                    if metrics["tampered_dice_05"] > best_macro:
                        best_macro = metrics["tampered_dice_05"]
                        save_checkpoint({
                            "kind": "fixed32_drive_best_weights",
                            "epoch": epoch,
                            "config": config,
                            "metrics": metrics,
                            "model_state": state,
                        }, run_dir / "best_train_macro.pt")
                    save_checkpoint({
                        "kind": "fixed32_drive_v1",
                        "epoch": epoch,
                        "config": config,
                        "model_state": state,
                        "optimizer_state": cpu_copy(optimizer.state_dict()),
                        "history": history,
                        "per_image_history": per_image_history,
                        "best_macro": best_macro,
                        "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                        "cuda_rng": (
--- PAGE 138 ---
                            torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                            if device.type == "cuda" else None
                        ),
                    }, run_dir / "last.pt")
                    pd.DataFrame(history).to_csv(
                        run_dir / "history.csv", index=False
                    )
                    pd.DataFrame(per_image_history).to_csv(
                        run_dir / "per_image_history.csv", index=False
                    )
                    print(
                        f"Epoch {epoch:03d} | Steps {epoch * 4:04d} | "
                        f"Loss {metrics['eval_loss']:.4f} | "
                        f"Micro {metrics['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                        f"Mean Tp {metrics['tampered_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                        f"Dice<0.1: {metrics['tampered_dice_below_01']}/16 | "
                        f"7700 {metrics['dice_7700']:.4f} | "
                        f"8693 {metrics['dice_8693']:.4f} | SAVED",
                        flush=True,
                    )
                    del state
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            model.eval()
            del optimizer
    finally:
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_deterministic, warn_only=old_warn_only
        )
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_deterministic
    return model, pd.DataFrame(history), pd.DataFrame(per_image_history), run_dir

from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Subset
from IPython.display import display
from forgery_localization.models.unet import UNet
RUN_DIR = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/diagnostics/"
    "fixed32_rerun_20261116_111932_445281"
)
if "val_dataset" not in globals() or "train_dataset" not in globals():
    raise RuntimeError("Chạy cell startup trước.")
saved = torch.load(
    RUN_DIR / "last.pt",
    map_location="cpu",
    weights_only=True,
)
assert saved["kind"] == "fixed32_drive_v1"
assert saved["epoch"] == 300
selection = pd.read_csv(
    RUN_DIR / "validation128.csv",
    keep_default_na=False,
)
assert len(selection) == 128
indices = selection["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
assert len(set(indices)) == 128
# Kiểm tra danh tính ảnh theo danh sách đã lưu.
validation_records = []
for row in selection.itertuples(index=False):
    record = val_dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
    assert Path(record["image_path"]).name == row.filename
    assert str(record["label"]) == str(row.label)
    assert str(record["source_group"]) == str(row.source_group)
    validation_records.append(record)
# Kiểm tra overlap giữa 32 ảnh đã train và 128 ảnh validation.
training_records = [
    train_dataset.records[i]
    for i in saved["config"]["indices"]
]
for field in ("image_path", "source_group", "image_hash", "mask_path"):
    if not all(field in r for r in training_records + validation_records):
        raise RuntimeError(f"Thiếu trường kiểm tra overlap: {field}")
    def values(records):
        return {
            str(r[field])
            for r in records
            if r[field] is not None and str(r[field]) != ""
        }
    overlap = values(training_records) & values(validation_records)
    if overlap:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Phát hiện overlap {field}: {list(overlap)[:3]}. "
            "Dừng để kiểm tra, không bỏ qua lỗi."
        )
loader = DataLoader(
    Subset(val_dataset, indices),
    batch_size=8,
    shuffle=False,
    num_workers=0,
)
device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
evaluation_model = UNet(
    in_channels=3,
    out_channels=1,
--- PAGE 141 ---
    base_channels=16,
).to(device)
evaluation_model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
evaluation_model.eval()
tp = fp = fn = 0
auth_fp = auth_pixels = 0
rows = []
offset = 0
with torch.inference_mode():
    for images, masks in loader:
        images = images.to(device, dtype=torch.float32)
        masks = masks.to(device, dtype=torch.float32)
        assert ((masks == 0) | (masks == 1)).all().item()
        logits = evaluation_model(images)
        assert torch.isfinite(logits).all().item()
        predictions = logits.sigmoid() > 0.5
        truth = masks.bool()
        for j in range(images.shape[0]):
            record = validation_records[offset + j]
            pred, gt = predictions[j], truth[j]
            is_tampered = str(record["label"]).lower() in ("tampered", "tp")
            assert gt.any().item() == is_tampered
            image_tp = (pred & gt).sum().item()
            image_fp = (pred & ~gt).sum().item()
            image_fn = (~pred & gt).sum().item()
            tp += image_tp
            fp += image_fp
            fn += image_fn
            if is_tampered:
                dice = (
                    2 * image_tp
                    / (2 * image_tp + image_fp + image_fn)
                )
            else:
                dice = np.nan
                auth_fp += pred.sum().item()
                auth_pixels += pred.numel()
            rows.append({
                "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                "label": "tampered" if is_tampered else "authentic",
                "dice_05": dice,
                "gt_foreground_pct": 100 * masks[j].mean().item(),
                "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * pred.float().mean().item(),
                "tp": image_tp,
                "fp": image_fp,
                "fn": image_fn,
            })
        offset += images.shape[0]
assert offset == 128
details = pd.DataFrame(rows)
tampered = details[details["label"] == "tampered"]
assert len(tampered) == 64
summary = pd.DataFrame([{
    "checkpoint_epoch": saved["epoch"],
    "validation_images": 128,
    "threshold": 0.5,
    "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
    "pixel_precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
    "pixel_recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
    "mean_tampered_dice": tampered["dice_05"].mean(),
    "tampered_dice_below_01": int((tampered["dice_05"] < 0.1).sum()),
    "authentic_false_positive_pct": 100 * auth_fp / max(auth_pixels, 1),
}])

# ============================================================
# TRAIN 256 / VALIDATE 128 — 50 EPOCHS
# Fresh U-Net, balanced batches, explicit overlap checks.
# Save directly to Drive; test split is never loaded.
# ============================================================
import copy
import hashlib
import json
import random
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Subset, Sampler
from IPython.display import display
from forgery_localization.models.unet import UNet
# -------------------- SETTINGS --------------------
SELECTION_DIR = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/diagnostics/"
    "restored_selection_20261116_111713_399032"
)
OUTPUT_ROOT = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/checkpoints"
--- PAGE 143 ---
)
TOTAL_EPOCHS = 50
SEED = 42
LR = 1e-3
THRESHOLD = 0.5
EVAL_EVERY = 5
# First run: None.
# Resume: paste this experiment's last.pt path.
RESUME_PATH = None
def run_train256():
    if not Path("/content/drive/MyDrive").is_dir():
        raise RuntimeError("Google Drive chưa được mount.")
    if "train_dataset" not in globals() or "val_dataset" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError("Chạy cell startup để tạo train_dataset/val_dataset.")
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    else:
        print("CPU mode: training sẽ chậm.")
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Restore exact saved selections.
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    def restore_selection(dataset, filename, count):
        table = pd.read_csv(
            SELECTION_DIR / filename, keep_default_na=False
        )
        ids = table["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
        if len(ids) != count or len(set(ids)) != count:
            raise ValueError(f"Invalid selection: {filename}")
        records = []
        for row in table.itertuples(index=False):
            record = dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
            if (
                Path(record["image_path"]).name != row.filename
                or str(record["label"]) != str(row.label)
                or str(record["source_group"]) != str(row.source_group)
            ):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Selection mismatch: {filename}, index {row.dataset_index}"
                )
            records.append(record)
        return Subset(dataset, ids), records, table
    train_subset, train_records, train_table = restore_selection(
        train_dataset, "train256.csv", 256
    )
    val_subset, val_records, val_table = restore_selection(
        val_dataset, "validation128.csv", 128
    )
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Check full TRAIN256/VAL128 overlap.
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    def resolved_values(records, field):
        values = set()
        for record in records:
            if field not in record:
                raise ValueError(f"Records thiếu {field}")
            value = record[field]
            if value is None or str(value) == "":
                continue
            values.add(
                str(Path(value).resolve())
                if field.endswith("_path") else str(value)
            )
        return values
--- PAGE 144 ---
    for field in ("image_path", "mask_path", "source_group", "image_hash"):
        overlap = (
            resolved_values(train_records, field)
            & resolved_values(val_records, field)
        )
        if overlap:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Cross-split overlap: {field}: {sorted(overlap)[:5]}. "
                "Dừng để sửa split, không bỏ qua kiểm tra."
            )
    print("TRAIN256 / VAL128 overlap checks: PASSED")
    print("Kiểm tra này không phát hiện mọi quan hệ donor/near-duplicate chưa biết.")
    def normalized_label(record):
        value = str(record["label"]).lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(value, value)
    train_auth = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records)
        if normalized_label(r) == "authentic"
    ]
    train_tamp = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records)
        if normalized_label(r) == "tampered"
    ]
    val_auth = sum(normalized_label(r) == "authentic" for r in val_records)
    val_tamp = sum(normalized_label(r) == "tampered" for r in val_records)
    assert len(train_auth) == len(train_tamp) == 128
    assert val_auth == val_tamp == 64
    # Inspect every preprocessed sample and bind resume to actual tensors.
    def fingerprint(subset, records):
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        positive_pixels = total_pixels = 0
        for i, record in enumerate(records):
            image, mask = subset[i]
            assert image.shape == (3, 256, 256)
            assert mask.shape == (1, 256, 256)
            assert image.dtype == mask.dtype == torch.float32
            assert torch.isfinite(image).all().item()
            assert torch.isfinite(mask).all().item()
            assert 0 <= image.min().item() <= image.max().item() <= 1
            assert ((mask == 0) | (mask == 1)).all().item()
            assert mask.any().item() == (
                normalized_label(record) == "tampered"
            )
            identity = {
                "image_path": str(record["image_path"]),
                "mask_path": str(record["mask_path"]),
                "source_group": str(record["source_group"]),
                "label": normalized_label(record),
            }
            digest.update(json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True).encode())
            digest.update(image.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
            digest.update(mask.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
            positive_pixels += mask.sum().item()
            total_pixels += mask.numel()
        return digest.hexdigest(), 100 * positive_pixels / total_pixels
    train_hash, train_fg = fingerprint(train_subset, train_records)
    val_hash, val_fg = fingerprint(val_subset, val_records)
    print(f"Train: 256 images | GT foreground: {train_fg:.4f}%")
    print(f"Val:   128 images | GT foreground: {val_fg:.4f}%")
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Balanced train sampling: all 256 images once per epoch.
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    class BalancedBatchSampler(Sampler):
        def __init__(self):
            self.epoch = 1
--- PAGE 145 ---
        def __len__(self):
            return 32
        def __iter__(self):
            rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED + self.epoch)
            auth = rng.permutation(train_auth)
            tamp = rng.permutation(train_tamp)
            batches = []
            for start in range(0, 128, 4):
                batch = np.concatenate([
                    auth[start:start + 4],
                    tamp[start:start + 4],
                ])
                rng.shuffle(batch)
                batches.append(batch.tolist())
            rng.shuffle(batches)
            yield from batches
    sampler = BalancedBatchSampler()
    training_loader = DataLoader(
        train_subset,
        batch_sampler=sampler,
        num_workers=0,
        pin_memory=device.type == "cuda",
    )
    train_eval_loader = DataLoader(
        train_subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
    )
    validation_loader = DataLoader(
        val_subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
    )
    config = {
        "seed": SEED,
        "lr": LR,
        "weight_decay": 1e-4,
        "base_channels": 16,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,
        "threshold": THRESHOLD,
        "eval_every": EVAL_EVERY,
        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_batch_global_dice_smooth1",
        "sampling": "4_authentic_4_tampered_without_replacement",
        "train_sha256": train_hash,
        "val_sha256": val_hash,
    }
    saved = None
    if RESUME_PATH is None:
        run_dir = OUTPUT_ROOT / (
            "train256_val128_"
            + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
        )
        run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    else:
        resume = Path(RESUME_PATH)
        if resume.name != "last.pt":
            raise ValueError("Resume requires last.pt.")
        saved = torch.load(resume, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
        if saved.get("kind") != "train256_val128_v1":
            raise ValueError("Checkpoint không thuộc cell này.")
        if saved["config"] != config:
            raise ValueError("Configuration hoặc dữ liệu khác checkpoint.")
        if saved["epoch"] > TOTAL_EPOCHS:
            raise ValueError("TOTAL_EPOCHS nhỏ hơn epoch đã lưu.")
        run_dir = resume.parent
    for table, name in [
        (train_table, "train256.csv"),
        (val_table, "validation128.csv"),
    ]:
        table.to_csv(run_dir / name, index=False)
    (run_dir / "config.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(config, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
--- PAGE 146 ---
    )
    print("\nOutput:", run_dir)
    print("Resume path:", run_dir / "last.pt")
    random.seed(SEED)
    np.random.seed(SEED)
    torch.manual_seed(SEED)
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(SEED)
    model = UNet(
        in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
    ).to(device)
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=1e-4
    )
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_fn(logits, targets):
        logits, targets = logits.float(), targets.float()
        bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        dice_loss = 1 - (
            (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
        )
        return bce + dice_loss
    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate(loader, records, epoch, split):
        model.eval()
        weighted_loss = samples = 0
        tp = fp = fn = 0
        auth_fp = auth_pixels = 0
        details = []
        offset = 0
        for images, masks in loader:
            images = images.to(device)
            masks = masks.to(device)
            logits = model(images)
            loss = loss_fn(logits, masks)
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation loss.")
            weighted_loss += loss.item() * images.shape[0]
            samples += images.shape[0]
            pred = logits.sigmoid() > THRESHOLD
            truth = masks.bool()
            for i in range(images.shape[0]):
                record = records[offset + i]
                p, gt = pred[i], truth[i]
                itp = (p & gt).sum().item()
                ifp = (p & ~gt).sum().item()
                ifn = (~p & gt).sum().item()
                tp += itp
                fp += ifp
                fn += ifn
                tampered = normalized_label(record) == "tampered"
                if tampered:
                    dice = 2 * itp / (2 * itp + ifp + ifn)
                else:
                    dice = None
                    auth_fp += p.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += p.numel()
                details.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "split": split,
                    "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                    "label": normalized_label(record),
--- PAGE 147 ---
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * p.float().mean().item(),
                    "tp": itp, "fp": ifp, "fn": ifn,
                })
            offset += images.shape[0]
        tampered_dice = [
            r["dice_05"] for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"
        ]
        metrics = {
            # Loss is image-weighted mean of batch objectives.
            "loss": weighted_loss / samples,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "mean_tampered_dice": float(np.mean(tampered_dice)),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(d < .1 for d in tampered_dice),
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_fp / max(auth_pixels, 1),
        }
        return metrics, details
    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def save_atomic(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    history, evaluations, per_image = [], [], []
    best_score, best_epoch, best_state = -1.0, None, None
    start_epoch = 1
    if saved is not None:
        model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
        optimizer.load_state_dict(saved["optimizer_state"])
        history = saved["history"]
        evaluations = saved["evaluations"]
        per_image = saved["per_image"]
        best_score = saved["best_score"]
        best_epoch = saved["best_epoch"]
        best_state = saved["best_model_state"]
        start_epoch = saved["epoch"] + 1
        torch.set_rng_state(saved["torch_rng"])
        if device.type == "cuda" and saved["cuda_rng"] is not None:
            torch.cuda.set_rng_state(saved["cuda_rng"], device)
        del saved
        print("Resume from epoch:", start_epoch - 1)
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_deterministic = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    old_algorithms = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        for epoch in range(start_epoch, TOTAL_EPOCHS + 1):
            sampler.epoch = epoch
            model.train()
            total_loss = total_images = 0
            for images, masks in training_loader:
                images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
--- PAGE 148 ---
                logits = model(images)
                loss = loss_fn(logits, masks)
                if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite loss at epoch {epoch}")
                loss.backward()
                for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                    if not parameter.requires_grad:
                        continue
                    if parameter.grad is None:
                        raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                    if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                        raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                optimizer.step()
                total_loss += loss.item() * images.shape[0]
                total_images += images.shape[0]
            history.append({
                "epoch": epoch,
                "optimizer_steps": epoch * 32,
                "training_loss_online": total_loss / total_images,
            })
            message = (
                f"Epoch {epoch:02d}/{TOTAL_EPOCHS} | "
                f"Steps {epoch * 32:04d} | "
                f"Online loss {total_loss / total_images:.4f}"
            )
            if epoch == 1 or epoch % EVAL_EVERY == 0 or epoch == TOTAL_EPOCHS:
                train_metrics, train_details = evaluate(
                    train_eval_loader, train_records, epoch, "train"
                )
                val_metrics, val_details = evaluate(
                    validation_loader, val_records, epoch, "validation"
                )
                evaluations.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    **{f"train_{k}": v for k, v in train_metrics.items()},
                    **{f"val_{k}": v for k, v in val_metrics.items()},
                })
                per_image.extend(train_details + val_details)
                score = val_metrics["mean_tampered_dice"]
                if score > best_score:
                    best_score, best_epoch = score, epoch
                    best_state = cpu_copy(model.state_dict())
                message += (
                    f" | Train Micro {train_metrics['micro_dice']:.4f}"
                    f" | Val Micro {val_metrics['micro_dice']:.4f}"
                    f" | Val Mean Tp {score:.4f}"
                    f" | Val Dice<0.1 "
                    f"{val_metrics['tampered_dice_below_01']}/64"
                )
            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            # Save every epoch; last.pt contains enough state to resume.
            save_atomic({
                "kind": "train256_val128_v1",
                "epoch": epoch,
                "config": config,
                "model_state": cpu_copy(model.state_dict()),
                "optimizer_state": cpu_copy(optimizer.state_dict()),
                "best_model_state": best_state,
                "best_score": best_score,
                "best_epoch": best_epoch,
                "history": history,
                "evaluations": evaluations,
                "per_image": per_image,
                "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                "cuda_rng": (
                    torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                    if device.type == "cuda" else None
--- PAGE 149 ---
                ),
            }, run_dir / "last.pt")
            save_atomic({
                "kind": "train256_val128_best_weights",
                "epoch": best_epoch,
                "config": config,
                "validation_mean_tampered_dice": best_score,
                "model_state": best_state,
            }, run_dir / "best_val_macro.pt")
            pd.DataFrame(history).to_csv(
                run_dir / "history.csv", index=False
            )
            pd.DataFrame(evaluations).to_csv(
                run_dir / "evaluations.csv", index=False
            )
            pd.DataFrame(per_image).to_csv(
                run_dir / "per_image.csv", index=False
            )
            print(message + " | SAVED", flush=True)
    finally:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_deterministic
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_algorithms, warn_only=old_warn
        )
    model.eval()
    print("\nBest validation mean tampered Dice:", best_score)
    print("Selected epoch:", best_epoch)
    print("Resume:", run_dir / "last.pt")
    print("Best weights:", run_dir / "best_val_macro.pt")
    return model, pd.DataFrame(evaluations), run_dir

def diagnose_epoch50():
    if "train_dataset" not in globals() or "val_dataset" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Chạy startup trước để tạo train_dataset và val_dataset."
        )
    checkpoint_path = RUN_DIR / "last.pt"
    saved = torch.load(
        checkpoint_path,
        map_location="cpu",
        weights_only=True,
    )
    if saved.get("kind") != "train256_val128_v1":
        raise RuntimeError("Checkpoint không thuộc thí nghiệm TRAIN256/VAL128.")
    if saved.get("epoch") != 50:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"Checkpoint đang ở epoch {saved.get('epoch')}, không phải 50."
        )
    config = saved["config"]
    expected = {
        "base_channels": 16,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,
        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_batch_global_dice_smooth1",
    }
    for key, value in expected.items():
        if config.get(key) != value:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Cấu hình checkpoint không khớp: {key}")
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    print("Checkpoint:", checkpoint_path)
    print("Epoch:", saved["epoch"])
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Restore saved selections, checking paths/labels/groups.
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    def load_selection(dataset, filename, expected_count):
        table = pd.read_csv(
            RUN_DIR / filename, keep_default_na=False
        )
        ids = table["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
        if len(ids) != expected_count or len(set(ids)) != expected_count:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Invalid selection: {filename}")
        records = []
        for row in table.itertuples(index=False):
            record = dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
            if (
                Path(record["image_path"]).name != row.filename
                or str(record["label"]) != str(row.label)
                or str(record["source_group"]) != str(row.source_group)
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"Selection mismatch: {filename}, index {row.dataset_index}"
                )
            records.append(record)
        return Subset(dataset, ids), records
    train_subset, train_records = load_selection(
        train_dataset, "train256.csv", 256
    )
    val_subset, val_records = load_selection(
        val_dataset, "validation128.csv", 128
--- PAGE 152 ---
    )
    for field in ("image_path", "mask_path", "source_group", "image_hash"):
        def values(records):
            result = set()
            for record in records:
                if field not in record:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Records thiếu {field}")
                value = record[field]
                if value is None or str(value) == "":
                    continue
                result.add(
                    str(Path(value).resolve())
                    if field.endswith("_path")
                    else str(value)
                )
            return result
        overlap = values(train_records) & values(val_records)
        if overlap:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Overlap {field}: {sorted(overlap)[:5]}. Dừng chẩn đoán."
            )
    print("Known TRAIN/VAL overlap checks: PASSED")
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Verify tensors against checkpoint fingerprints.
    #    Also detects changed masks or preprocessing.
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    import hashlib
    def fingerprint(subset, records):
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        for i, record in enumerate(records):
            image, mask = subset[i]
            assert image.shape == (3, 256, 256)
            assert mask.shape == (1, 256, 256)
            assert image.dtype == mask.dtype == torch.float32
            assert torch.isfinite(image).all().item()
            assert torch.isfinite(mask).all().item()
            assert 0 <= image.min().item() <= image.max().item() <= 1
            assert ((mask == 0) | (mask == 1)).all().item()
            label = str(record["label"]).lower()
            label = {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(label, label)
            if label not in ("authentic", "tampered"):
                raise RuntimeError(f"Unknown label: {label}")
            assert mask.any().item() == (label == "tampered")
            identity = {
                "image_path": str(record["image_path"]),
                "mask_path": str(record["mask_path"]),
                "source_group": str(record["source_group"]),
                "label": label,
            }
            digest.update(json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True).encode())
            digest.update(image.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
            digest.update(mask.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
        return digest.hexdigest()
    print("Checking dataset fingerprints...")
    for name, subset, records in [
        ("train", train_subset, train_records),
        ("val", val_subset, val_records),
    ]:
        if fingerprint(subset, records) != config[f"{name}_sha256"]:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"{name}: tensor fingerprint khác checkpoint. "
                "Kiểm tra dữ liệu và preprocessing."
            )
    print("Dataset fingerprints: MATCH")

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 3. Independent evaluation model; no optimizer.
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    model = UNet(
        in_channels=3,
        out_channels=1,
        base_channels=config["base_channels"],
    ).to(device)
    model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
    model.eval()
    pos_weight = torch.tensor(
        [float(config["pos_weight"])], device=device
    )
    def metrics_from_counts(tp, fp, fn):
        both_empty = tp + fp + fn == 0
        return {
            "dice": (
                2 * tp / (2 * tp + fp + fn)
                if not both_empty else 1.0
            ),
            "precision": (
                tp / (tp + fp) if tp + fp else float(both_empty)
            ),
            "recall": (
                tp / (tp + fn) if tp + fn else float(both_empty)
            ),
            "iou": (
                tp / (tp + fp + fn) if not both_empty else 1.0
            ),
        }
    probability_rows = []
    loss_rows = []
    metric_rows = []
    per_image_rows = []
    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate_split(name, subset, records):
        loader = DataLoader(
            subset,
            batch_size=EVAL_BATCH_SIZE,
            shuffle=False,
            num_workers=0,
        )
        stats = {
            t: {
                "tp": 0, "fp": 0, "fn": 0,
                "pred": 0,
                "auth_pred": 0, "auth_pixels": 0,
                "tampered_dice": [],
                "missed_tampered": 0,
            }
            for t in THRESHOLDS
        }
        total_pixels = gt_pixels = 0
        probability_sum_gt = probability_sum_bg = 0.0
        maximum_probability = 0.0
        minimum_probability = 1.0
        weighted_bce_sum = plain_bce_sum = 0.0
        intersection_sum = denominator_sum = 0.0
        batch_dice_weighted_sum = 0.0
        sample_count = 0
        offset = 0
        for images, masks in loader:
            images = images.to(device)
            masks = masks.to(device)
            logits = model(images).float()
            if logits.shape != masks.shape:
--- PAGE 154 ---
                raise RuntimeError("Logit/target shape mismatch.")
            if not torch.isfinite(logits).all().item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite logits.")
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            truth = masks.bool()
            weighted_bce_sum += F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                logits, masks,
                pos_weight=pos_weight,
                reduction="sum",
            ).item()
            plain_bce_sum += F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                logits, masks, reduction="sum"
            ).item()
            intersection = (probs * masks).sum().item()
            denominator = (probs.sum() + masks.sum()).item()
            intersection_sum += intersection
            denominator_sum += denominator
            batch_dice = 1 - (
                (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
            )
            batch_dice_weighted_sum += batch_dice * images.shape[0]
            sample_count += images.shape[0]
            total_pixels += truth.numel()
            gt_pixels += truth.sum().item()
            probability_sum_gt += probs[truth].double().sum().item()
            probability_sum_bg += probs[~truth].double().sum().item()
            maximum_probability = max(
                maximum_probability, probs.max().item()
            )
            minimum_probability = min(
                minimum_probability, probs.min().item()
            )
            for threshold in THRESHOLDS:
                predictions = probs > threshold
                current = stats[threshold]
                current["tp"] += (predictions & truth).sum().item()
                current["fp"] += (predictions & ~truth).sum().item()
                current["fn"] += (~predictions & truth).sum().item()
                current["pred"] += predictions.sum().item()
                for j in range(images.shape[0]):
                    record = records[offset + j]
                    gt = truth[j]
                    pred = predictions[j]
                    tampered = gt.any().item()
                    tp = (pred & gt).sum().item()
                    fp = (pred & ~gt).sum().item()
                    fn = (~pred & gt).sum().item()
                    if tampered:
                        dice = 2 * tp / (2 * tp + fp + fn)
                        current["tampered_dice"].append(dice)
                        current["missed_tampered"] += int(
                            pred.sum().item() == 0
                        )
                    else:
                        dice = np.nan
                        current["auth_pred"] += pred.sum().item()
                        current["auth_pixels"] += pred.numel()
                    per_image_rows.append({
                        "split": name,
                        "threshold": threshold,
                        "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                        "label": "tampered" if tampered else "authentic",
                        "gt_foreground_pct": 100 * masks[j].mean().item(),
--- PAGE 155 ---
                        "predicted_positive_pct": (
                            100 * pred.float().mean().item()
                        ),
                        "dice": dice,
                        "max_probability": probs[j].max().item(),
                        "mean_probability_in_mask": (
                            probs[j][gt].mean().item()
                            if tampered else np.nan
                        ),
                        "mean_probability_background": (
                            probs[j][~gt].mean().item()
                            if (~gt).any().item() else np.nan
                        ),
                        "tp": tp, "fp": fp, "fn": fn,
                    })
            offset += images.shape[0]
        assert offset == len(subset)
        background_pixels = total_pixels - gt_pixels
        probability_rows.append({
            "split": name,
            "images": sample_count,
            "gt_foreground_pct": 100 * gt_pixels / total_pixels,
            "mean_probability_in_mask": (
                probability_sum_gt / gt_pixels
                if gt_pixels else np.nan
            ),
            "mean_probability_background": (
                probability_sum_bg / background_pixels
                if background_pixels else np.nan
            ),
            "min_probability": minimum_probability,
            "max_probability": maximum_probability,
        })
        weighted_bce = weighted_bce_sum / total_pixels
        batch_dice_loss = batch_dice_weighted_sum / sample_count
        # Matches the previous evaluation's batch-wise Dice convention.
        # Global Dice is additionally reported for clarity.
        global_dice_loss = 1 - (
            (2 * intersection_sum + 1)
            / (denominator_sum + 1)
        )
        loss_rows.append({
            "split": name,
            "weighted_bce_pos_weight_3": weighted_bce,
            "plain_bce_reference": plain_bce_sum / total_pixels,
            "mean_batch_soft_dice_loss": batch_dice_loss,
            "combined_loss_matching_previous_eval": (
                weighted_bce + batch_dice_loss
            ),
            "global_soft_dice_loss_reference": global_dice_loss,
            "evaluation_batch_size": EVAL_BATCH_SIZE,
        })
        for threshold, current in stats.items():
            dice_values = current["tampered_dice"]
            metric_rows.append({
                "split": name,
                "threshold": threshold,
                **metrics_from_counts(
                    current["tp"], current["fp"], current["fn"]
                ),
                "mean_tampered_dice": float(np.mean(dice_values)),
                "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(
                    d < 0.1 for d in dice_values
                ),
                "tampered_with_zero_predicted_pixels": (
                    current["missed_tampered"]
                ),
                "predicted_positive_pct": (
                    100 * current["pred"] / total_pixels
--- PAGE 156 ---
                ),
                "authentic_false_positive_pct": (
                    100 * current["auth_pred"]
                    / max(current["auth_pixels"], 1)
                ),
                "tp": current["tp"],
                "fp": current["fp"],
                "fn": current["fn"],
            })
        print(f"{name}: evaluated {sample_count} images.")
    try:
        evaluate_split("TRAIN", train_subset, train_records)
        evaluate_split("VALIDATION", val_subset, val_records)
    finally:
        # Release this independent model's GPU memory.
        model.cpu()
    probabilities_df = pd.DataFrame(probability_rows)
    losses_df = pd.DataFrame(loss_rows)
    metrics_df = pd.DataFrame(metric_rows)
    details_df = pd.DataFrame(per_image_rows)
    output = RUN_DIR / (
        "diagnosis_epoch50_"
        + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
    )
    output.mkdir(exist_ok=False)
    probabilities_df.to_csv(output / "probabilities.csv", index=False)
    losses_df.to_csv(output / "losses.csv", index=False)
    metrics_df.to_csv(output / "metrics.csv", index=False)
    details_df.to_csv(output / "per_image.csv", index=False)
    (output / "evaluation_info.json").write_text(
        json.dumps({
            "checkpoint": str(checkpoint_path),
            "checkpoint_epoch": saved["epoch"],
            "threshold_rule": "sigmoid(logits) > threshold",
            "thresholds": list(THRESHOLDS),
            "torch_version": str(torch.__version__),
            "device": str(device),
            "dataset_fingerprints_matched": True,
            "training_performed": False,
        }, indent=2),
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    print("\nPROBABILITY STATISTICS")
    display(probabilities_df.round(8))
    print("\nBCE / SOFT DICE LOSS")
    display(losses_df.round(8))
    print("\nMETRICS — THRESHOLDS 0.1 / 0.5")
    display(metrics_df.round(8))
    print("\nSaved:", output)
    print("Không train thêm, không sửa checkpoint, không dùng test set.")
    return probabilities_df, losses_df, metrics_df, details_df

def run_sampling_comparison():
    if "train_dataset" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError("Chạy startup để tạo train_dataset trước.")
    if not Path("/content/drive/MyDrive").is_dir():
        raise RuntimeError("Google Drive chưa được mount.")
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )

    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))

    # 1. Restore exact samples and validate actual tensors.
    # --------------------------------------------------------

        SOURCE_RUN / "selected_samples.csv", keep_default_na=False

    expected_indices = [
        2347, 3403, 856, 3032, 2462, 2241, 2008, 1985,

    for row in selection.itertuples(index=False):
        record = train_dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
        if (
            Path(record["image_path"]).name != row.filename
            or str(record["label"]) != str(row.label)
            or str(record["source_group"]) != str(row.source_group)
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Selection mismatch at index {row.dataset_index}"

    samples = [train_dataset[i] for i in indices]
    images_cpu = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).float()
    masks_cpu = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).float()
    del samples
    assert images_cpu.shape == (32, 3, 256, 256)
    assert masks_cpu.shape == (32, 1, 256, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(images_cpu).all().item()
    assert torch.isfinite(masks_cpu).all().item()
    assert 0 <= images_cpu.min().item() <= images_cpu.max().item() <= 1
    assert ((masks_cpu == 0) | (masks_cpu == 1)).all().item()
    positive = masks_cpu.flatten(1).sum(1) > 0
    assert not positive[:16].any().item()
    assert positive[16:].all().item()
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    digest.update(images_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    digest.update(masks_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    tensor_hash = digest.hexdigest()
    original = torch.load(
        SOURCE_RUN / "last.pt", map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
    )
    if original.get("kind") != "fixed32_drive_v1":

    del original

        "base_channels": 16,

        "indices": indices,
        "tensor_sha256": tensor_hash,
        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_batch_global_dice_smooth1",
        "evaluation": "global32_loss_and_micro_macro_dice",
    }
    if RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR is None:

            (experiment_dir / "config.json").read_text()

    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def save_atomic(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    initial_path = experiment_dir / "initial_weights.pt"
    if RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR is None:
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
            torch.manual_seed(SEED)
            initial_model = UNet(
                in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
            )
            initial_state = cpu_copy(initial_model.state_dict())
            del initial_model
        save_atomic(initial_state, initial_path)

            experiment_dir / "selected_samples.csv", index=False

            json.dumps(config, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
        )
    initial_state = torch.load(
        initial_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
    )
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        bce_sum = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight, reduction="sum"
        )
        probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probabilities * targets).sum()
        denominator = probabilities.sum() + targets.sum()
        return bce_sum, intersection, denominator
--- PAGE 160 ---
    fixed_batches = [
        list(range(start, start + 4))
        + list(range(16 + start, 20 + start))
        for start in (0, 4, 8, 12)
    ]
    def get_batches(branch, epoch):
        if branch == "A_FIXED":
            return fixed_batches
        rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED + epoch)
        auth = rng.permutation(16)
        tamp = rng.permutation(np.arange(16, 32))
        batches = []
        for start in range(0, 16, 4):
            batch = np.concatenate([
                auth[start:start + 4],
                tamp[start:start + 4],
            ])
            rng.shuffle(batch)
            batches.append(batch.tolist())
        rng.shuffle(batches)
        return batches
    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate(model, branch, epoch):
        model.eval()
        bce_total = intersection_total = denominator_total = 0.0
        tp = fp = fn = 0
        authentic_fp = 0
        details = []
        for start in range(0, 32, 8):
            targets = masks[start:start + 8]
            logits = model(images[start:start + 8])
            if not torch.isfinite(logits).all().item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation logits.")
            bce, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(logits, targets)
            bce_total += bce.item()
            intersection_total += intersection.item()
            denominator_total += denominator.item()
            probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
            predictions = probabilities > 0.5
            truth = targets.bool()
            tp += (predictions & truth).sum().item()
            fp += (predictions & ~truth).sum().item()
            fn += (~predictions & truth).sum().item()
            for local in range(8):
                position = start + local
                pred, gt = predictions[local], truth[local]
                is_tampered = position >= 16
                if is_tampered:
                    dice = (
                        2 * (pred & gt).sum().item()
                        / (pred.sum().item() + gt.sum().item())
                    )
                else:
                    dice = None
                    authentic_fp += pred.sum().item()
                details.append({
                    "branch": branch,
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "dataset_index": indices[position],
                    "filename": selection.iloc[position]["filename"],
                    "label": "tampered" if is_tampered else "authentic",
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": (
                        100 * pred.float().mean().item()
                    ),
--- PAGE 161 ---
                })
        tampered_rows = [r for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        lookup = {r["dataset_index"]: r["dice_05"] for r in tampered_rows}
        bce = bce_total / masks.numel()
        soft_dice = 1 - (
            (2 * intersection_total + 1) / (denominator_total + 1)
        )
        metrics = {
            "branch": branch,
            "epoch": epoch,
            "optimizer_steps": epoch * 4,
            "eval_loss": bce + soft_dice,
            "bce": bce,
            "global_soft_dice_loss": soft_dice,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "mean_tampered_dice": float(
                np.mean([r["dice_05"] for r in tampered_rows])
            ),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(
                r["dice_05"] < .1 for r in tampered_rows
            ),
            "authentic_fp_pct": (
                100 * authentic_fp / masks[:16].numel()
            ),
            "dice_7700": lookup[7700],
            "dice_8693": lookup[8693],
        }
        return metrics, details
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_deterministic = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    old_algorithms = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    combined_history = []
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        for branch in ("A_FIXED", "B_SHUFFLED"):
            branch_dir = experiment_dir / branch
            branch_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            last_path = branch_dir / "last.pt"
            with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
                torch.manual_seed(SEED)
                model = UNet(
                    in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
                ).to(device)
                model.load_state_dict(initial_state)
                optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                    model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=1e-4
                )
                history, per_image = [], []
                best_score = -1.0
                best_state = None
                best_epoch = None
                start_epoch = 0
                if last_path.exists():
                    saved = torch.load(
                        last_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
                    )
                    if (
                        saved.get("kind") != "sampling32_v1"
                        or saved["config"] != config

                        or saved["branch"] != branch
                    ):
                        raise RuntimeError("Invalid resume checkpoint.")
                    if saved["epoch"] > EPOCHS:
                        raise RuntimeError("EPOCHS nhỏ hơn epoch đã lưu.")
                    model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
                    optimizer.load_state_dict(saved["optimizer_state"])
                    history = saved["history"]
                    per_image = saved["per_image"]
                    best_score = saved["best_score"]
                    best_state = saved["best_model_state"]
                    best_epoch = saved["best_epoch"]
                    start_epoch = saved["epoch"] + 1
                    torch.set_rng_state(saved["torch_rng"])
                    if device.type == "cuda" and saved["cuda_rng"] is not None:
                        torch.cuda.set_rng_state(saved["cuda_rng"], device)
                    del saved
                print(f"\n=== {branch} | Start epoch {start_epoch} ===")
                for epoch in range(start_epoch, EPOCHS + 1):
                    if epoch > 0:
                        model.train()
                        batches = get_batches(branch, epoch)
                        # Confirm each sample appears exactly once.
                        assert sorted(i for b in batches for i in b) == list(range(32))
                        for batch in batches:
                            assert sum(i < 16 for i in batch) == 4
                            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                            targets = masks[batch]
                            logits = model(images[batch])
                            bce, intersection, denominator = loss_parts(
                                logits, targets
                            )
                            loss = (
                                bce / targets.numel()
                                + 1
                                - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
                            )
                            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite loss.")
                            loss.backward()
                            for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                                if not parameter.requires_grad:
                                    continue
                                if parameter.grad is None:
                                    raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                                if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                                    raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                            optimizer.step()
                            del logits, loss, bce, intersection, denominator
                    # Same evaluation schedule for both branches.
                    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == EPOCHS:
                        metrics, details = evaluate(model, branch, epoch)
                        history.append(metrics)
                        per_image.extend(details)
                        if metrics["mean_tampered_dice"] > best_score:
                            best_score = metrics["mean_tampered_dice"]
                            best_epoch = epoch
                            best_state = cpu_copy(model.state_dict())
                        print(
                            f"{branch} | Epoch {epoch:03d} | "

                            f"Dice<0.1 {metrics['tampered_dice_below_01']}/16 | "
                            f"7700 {metrics['dice_7700']:.4f} | "
                            f"8693 {metrics['dice_8693']:.4f}",
                            flush=True,

                        )
                    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                    # Persist latest state every epoch.
                    save_atomic({
                        "kind": "sampling32_v1",
                        "branch": branch,
                        "epoch": epoch,
                        "config": config,
                        "model_state": cpu_copy(model.state_dict()),
                        "optimizer_state": cpu_copy(optimizer.state_dict()),
                        "best_model_state": best_state,
                        "best_score": best_score,
                        "best_epoch": best_epoch,
                        "history": history,
                        "per_image": per_image,
                        "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                        "cuda_rng": (
                            torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                            if device.type == "cuda" else None
                        ),
                    }, last_path)
                    if epoch % 10 == 0 or epoch == EPOCHS:
                        save_atomic({
                            "kind": "sampling32_best_train_weights",
                            "branch": branch,
                            "epoch": best_epoch,
                            "config": config,
                            "train_mean_tampered_dice": best_score,
                            "model_state": best_state,
                        }, branch_dir / "best_train_macro.pt")
                    pd.DataFrame(history).to_csv(
                        branch_dir / "history.csv", index=False
                    )
                    pd.DataFrame(per_image).to_csv(
                        branch_dir / "per_image.csv", index=False
                    )
                combined_history.extend(history)
                del model, optimizer, best_state
                gc.collect()
                if device.type == "cuda":
                    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    finally:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_deterministic
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_algorithms, warn_only=old_warn
        )
    result = pd.DataFrame(combined_history)
    result.to_csv(experiment_dir / "comparison.csv", index=False)
    return result, experiment_dir
