TOTAL_EPOCHS = 100
def resume_train256():
    if "train_dataset" not in globals() or "val_dataset" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError("Chạy startup trước.")
    last_path = RUN_DIR / "last.pt"
    saved = torch.load(
        last_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
    )
    if saved.get("kind") != "train256_val128_v1":
        raise RuntimeError("Sai loại checkpoint.")
    checkpoint_epoch = int(saved["epoch"])
    if not 50 <= checkpoint_epoch <= TOTAL_EPOCHS:
        raise RuntimeError(f"Unexpected checkpoint epoch: {checkpoint_epoch}")
    config = saved["config"]
    expected = {
        "seed": 42,
        "lr": 1e-3,
        "weight_decay": 1e-4,
        "base_channels": 16,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,
        "threshold": 0.5,
        "eval_every": 5,
        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_batch_global_dice_smooth1",
        "sampling": "4_authentic_4_tampered_without_replacement",
    }
    for key, value in expected.items():
        if config.get(key) != value:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Configuration mismatch: {key}")
    device = torch.device(
        "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
    )
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    print("Resume epoch:", checkpoint_epoch)
    print("Target total epochs:", TOTAL_EPOCHS)
    def label(record):
        value = str(record["label"]).lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(value, value)
    def restore(dataset, filename, count):
        table = pd.read_csv(RUN_DIR / filename, keep_default_na=False)
        ids = table["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
        assert len(ids) == len(set(ids)) == count
        records = []
        for row in table.itertuples(index=False):
            record = dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
            if (
                Path(record["image_path"]).name != row.filename
                or str(record["label"]) != str(row.label)
                or str(record["source_group"]) != str(row.source_group)
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(f"Selection mismatch: {filename}")
            records.append(record)
        return Subset(dataset, ids), records
    train_subset, train_records = restore(
        train_dataset, "train256.csv", 256
    )
    val_subset, val_records = restore(
        val_dataset, "validation128.csv", 128
    )
    # Full TRAIN256/VAL128 overlap checks.
    for field in ("image_path", "mask_path", "source_group", "image_hash"):
--- PAGE 167 ---
        def field_values(records):
            values = set()
            for record in records:
                if field not in record:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Missing field: {field}")
                value = record[field]
                if value is None or str(value) == "":
                    continue
                values.add(
                    str(Path(value).resolve())
                    if field.endswith("_path") else str(value)
                )
            return values
        overlap = field_values(train_records) & field_values(val_records)
        if overlap:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Cross-split overlap: {field}")
    # Match exactly the fingerprint algorithm used by the original cell.
    def fingerprint(subset, records):
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        for i, record in enumerate(records):
            image, mask = subset[i]
            assert image.shape == (3, 256, 256)
            assert mask.shape == (1, 256, 256)
            assert image.dtype == mask.dtype == torch.float32
            assert torch.isfinite(image).all().item()
            assert torch.isfinite(mask).all().item()
            assert 0 <= image.min().item() <= image.max().item() <= 1
            assert ((mask == 0) | (mask == 1)).all().item()
            assert mask.any().item() == (label(record) == "tampered")
            identity = {
                "image_path": str(record["image_path"]),
                "mask_path": str(record["mask_path"]),
                "source_group": str(record["source_group"]),
                "label": label(record),
            }
            digest.update(json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True).encode())
            digest.update(image.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
            digest.update(mask.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
        return digest.hexdigest()
    for name, subset, records in [
        ("train", train_subset, train_records),
        ("val", val_subset, val_records),
    ]:
        if fingerprint(subset, records) != config[f"{name}_sha256"]:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{name}: fingerprint mismatch.")
    print("Overlap checks: PASSED | Dataset fingerprints: MATCH")
    auth_ids = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records) if label(r) == "authentic"
    ]
    tamp_ids = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records) if label(r) == "tampered"
    ]
    assert len(auth_ids) == len(tamp_ids) == 128
    assert sum(label(r) == "tampered" for r in val_records) == 64
    train_eval_loader = DataLoader(
        train_subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
    )
    val_eval_loader = DataLoader(
        val_subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
    )
    def balanced_batches(epoch):
        # Same seed, grouping and shuffle algorithm as original training.
        rng = np.random.default_rng(config["seed"] + epoch)
        auth = rng.permutation(auth_ids)
        tamp = rng.permutation(tamp_ids)
        batches = []
        for start in range(0, 128, 4):
--- PAGE 168 ---
            batch = np.concatenate([
                auth[start:start + 4], tamp[start:start + 4]
            ])
            rng.shuffle(batch)
            batches.append(batch.tolist())
        rng.shuffle(batches)
        return batches
    model = UNet(
        in_channels=3, out_channels=1,
        base_channels=config["base_channels"],
    ).to(device)
    model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
        model.parameters(),
        lr=config["lr"],
        weight_decay=config["weight_decay"],
    )
    optimizer.load_state_dict(saved["optimizer_state"])
    for group in optimizer.param_groups:
        if (
            group["lr"] != config["lr"]
            or group["weight_decay"] != config["weight_decay"]
        ):
            raise RuntimeError("Optimizer settings differ from configuration.")
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([config["pos_weight"]], device=device)
    def loss_fn(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        targets = targets.float()
        bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
        )
        probabilities = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probabilities * targets).sum()
        denominator = probabilities.sum() + targets.sum()
        return bce + 1 - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate(loader, records, epoch, split):
        model.eval()
        loss_sum = sample_count = 0
        tp = fp = fn = 0
        auth_fp = auth_pixels = 0
        details = []
        offset = 0
        for images, masks in loader:
            images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
            logits = model(images)
            loss = loss_fn(logits, masks)
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation loss.")
            loss_sum += loss.item() * images.shape[0]
            sample_count += images.shape[0]
            predictions = logits.sigmoid() > config["threshold"]
            truth = masks.bool()
            for j in range(images.shape[0]):
                record = records[offset + j]
                pred, gt = predictions[j], truth[j]
                itp = (pred & gt).sum().item()
                ifp = (pred & ~gt).sum().item()
                ifn = (~pred & gt).sum().item()
                tp += itp
                fp += ifp
                fn += ifn
                if label(record) == "tampered":
                    dice = 2 * itp / (2 * itp + ifp + ifn)
                else:
                    dice = None
                    auth_fp += pred.sum().item()
--- PAGE 169 ---
                    auth_pixels += pred.numel()
                details.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "split": split,
                    "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                    "label": label(record),
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * pred.float().mean().item(),
                    "tp": itp, "fp": ifp, "fn": ifn,
                })
            offset += images.shape[0]
        dice_values = [
            r["dice_05"] for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"
        ]
        return {
            "loss": loss_sum / sample_count,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "mean_tampered_dice": float(np.mean(dice_values)),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(d < .1 for d in dice_values),
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_fp / max(auth_pixels, 1),
        }, details
    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def save_atomic(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    # Preserve the epoch-50 checkpoint before modifying last.pt.
    if checkpoint_epoch == 50:
        backup = RUN_DIR / "epoch050_backup.pt"
        if not backup.exists():
            shutil.copy2(last_path, backup)
        print("Epoch-50 backup:", backup)
    history = saved["history"]
    evaluations = saved["evaluations"]
    per_image = saved["per_image"]
    best_score = saved["best_score"]
    best_epoch = saved["best_epoch"]
    best_state = saved["best_model_state"]
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_det = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    old_algorithms = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
            torch.set_rng_state(saved["torch_rng"])
            if device.type == "cuda" and saved["cuda_rng"] is not None:
                torch.cuda.set_rng_state(saved["cuda_rng"], device)
            del saved
            for epoch in range(checkpoint_epoch + 1, TOTAL_EPOCHS + 1):
--- PAGE 170 ---
                loader = DataLoader(
                    train_subset,
                    batch_sampler=balanced_batches(epoch),
                    num_workers=0,
                    pin_memory=device.type == "cuda",
                )
                model.train()
                total_loss = total_images = 0
                for images, masks in loader:
                    images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
                    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                    logits = model(images)
                    loss = loss_fn(logits, masks)
                    if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                        raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite training loss.")
                    loss.backward()
                    for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                        if not parameter.requires_grad:
                            continue
                        if parameter.grad is None:
                            raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                        if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                            raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                    optimizer.step()
                    total_loss += loss.item() * images.shape[0]
                    total_images += images.shape[0]
                history.append({
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "optimizer_steps": epoch * 32,
                    "training_loss_online": total_loss / total_images,
                })
                message = (
                    f"Epoch {epoch:03d}/{TOTAL_EPOCHS} | "
                    f"Steps {epoch * 32:04d} | "
                    f"Online loss {total_loss / total_images:.4f}"
                )
                if epoch % config["eval_every"] == 0 or epoch == TOTAL_EPOCHS:
                    train_metrics, train_details = evaluate(
                        train_eval_loader, train_records, epoch, "train"
                    )
                    val_metrics, val_details = evaluate(
                        val_eval_loader, val_records, epoch, "validation"
                    )
                    evaluations.append({
                        "epoch": epoch,
                        **{f"train_{k}": v for k, v in train_metrics.items()},
                        **{f"val_{k}": v for k, v in val_metrics.items()},
                    })
                    per_image.extend(train_details + val_details)
                    score = val_metrics["mean_tampered_dice"]
                    if score > best_score:
                        best_score, best_epoch = score, epoch
                        best_state = cpu_copy(model.state_dict())
                    message += (
                        f" | Train Micro {train_metrics['micro_dice']:.4f}"
                        f" | Train Mean Tp "
                        f"{train_metrics['mean_tampered_dice']:.4f}"
                        f" | Val Micro {val_metrics['micro_dice']:.4f}"
                        f" | Val Mean Tp {score:.4f}"
                        f" | Val Dice<0.1 "
                        f"{val_metrics['tampered_dice_below_01']}/64"
                    )
                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                save_atomic({
                    "kind": "train256_val128_v1",
--- PAGE 171 ---
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "config": config,
                    "model_state": cpu_copy(model.state_dict()),
                    "optimizer_state": cpu_copy(optimizer.state_dict()),
                    "best_model_state": best_state,
                    "best_score": best_score,
                    "best_epoch": best_epoch,
                    "history": history,
                    "evaluations": evaluations,
                    "per_image": per_image,
                    "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                    "cuda_rng": (
                        torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                        if device.type == "cuda" else None
                    ),
                }, last_path)
                save_atomic({
                    "kind": "train256_val128_best_weights",
                    "epoch": best_epoch,
                    "config": config,
                    "validation_mean_tampered_dice": best_score,
                    "model_state": best_state,
                }, RUN_DIR / "best_val_macro.pt")
                pd.DataFrame(history).to_csv(
                    RUN_DIR / "history.csv", index=False
                )
                pd.DataFrame(evaluations).to_csv(
                    RUN_DIR / "evaluations.csv", index=False
                )
                pd.DataFrame(per_image).to_csv(
                    RUN_DIR / "per_image.csv", index=False
                )
                print(message + " | SAVED", flush=True)
    finally:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_det
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_algorithms, warn_only=old_warn
        )
    model.eval()
    print("\nBest validation mean tampered Dice:", best_score)
    print("Selected epoch:", best_epoch)
    print("Latest checkpoint:", last_path)
    print("Test split was not loaded.")
    return model, pd.DataFrame(evaluations)

import copy

SOURCE_RUN = Path(

    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/checkpoints/"
    "train256_val128_20261116_112642_199797"
)
OUTPUT_ROOT = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/diagnostics"
)
# First run: None.
# Resume: paste the experiment directory printed by this cell.
RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR = None
END_EPOCH = 125
BRANCHES = {"A_LR1e3": 1e-3, "B_LR3e4": 3e-4}
def run_epoch100_fork():
    if "train_dataset" not in globals() or "val_dataset" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError("Chạy startup trước.")
    if not Path("/content/drive/MyDrive").is_dir():
        raise RuntimeError("Google Drive chưa được mount.")
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def atomic_save(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    # Freeze a source copy for both branches and later resumes.
    if RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR is None:
        source = torch.load(
            SOURCE_RUN / "last.pt",
            map_location="cpu",
            weights_only=True,
        )
        if (
            source.get("kind") != "train256_val128_v1"
            or source.get("epoch") != 100
        ):
            raise RuntimeError("Cần đúng last.pt epoch 100.")
        experiment_dir = OUTPUT_ROOT / (
            "fork256_epoch100_"
            + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
        )
        experiment_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        atomic_save(source, experiment_dir / "source_epoch100.pt")
        for filename in ("train256.csv", "validation128.csv"):
            (experiment_dir / filename).write_bytes(
                (SOURCE_RUN / filename).read_bytes()
            )
    else:
        experiment_dir = Path(RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR)
        source = torch.load(
            experiment_dir / "source_epoch100.pt",
            map_location="cpu",
            weights_only=True,
        )
    if source.get("kind") != "train256_val128_v1" or source["epoch"] != 100:
        raise RuntimeError("Invalid source checkpoint.")
--- PAGE 174 ---
    config = source["config"]
    expected = {
        "seed": 42,
        "lr": 1e-3,
        "weight_decay": 1e-4,
        "base_channels": 16,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,
        "threshold": 0.5,
        "eval_every": 5,
        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_batch_global_dice_smooth1",
        "sampling": "4_authentic_4_tampered_without_replacement",
    }
    for key, value in expected.items():
        if config.get(key) != value:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Source configuration mismatch: {key}")
    print("EXPERIMENT DIRECTORY:", experiment_dir, flush=True)
    def label(record):
        value = str(record["label"]).lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(value, value)
    def restore(dataset, filename, count):
        table = pd.read_csv(
            experiment_dir / filename, keep_default_na=False
        )
        ids = table["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
        assert len(ids) == len(set(ids)) == count
        records = []
        for row in table.itertuples(index=False):
            record = dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
            if (
                Path(record["image_path"]).name != row.filename
                or str(record["label"]) != str(row.label)
                or str(record["source_group"]) != str(row.source_group)
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(f"Selection mismatch: {filename}")
            records.append(record)
        return Subset(dataset, ids), records
    train_subset, train_records = restore(
        train_dataset, "train256.csv", 256
    )
    val_subset, val_records = restore(
        val_dataset, "validation128.csv", 128
    )
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # Check overlap and exact preprocessed tensor fingerprints.
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    for field in ("image_path", "mask_path", "source_group", "image_hash"):
        def values(records):
            result = set()
            for record in records:
                if field not in record:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Missing field: {field}")
                value = record[field]
                if value is None or str(value) == "":
                    continue
                result.add(
                    str(Path(value).resolve())
                    if field.endswith("_path") else str(value)
                )
            return result
        if values(train_records) & values(val_records):
            raise RuntimeError(f"Cross-split overlap: {field}")
    def fingerprint(subset, records):
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        for i, record in enumerate(records):
            image, mask = subset[i]
            assert image.shape == (3, 256, 256)
            assert mask.shape == (1, 256, 256)
            assert image.dtype == mask.dtype == torch.float32
--- PAGE 175 ---
            assert torch.isfinite(image).all().item()
            assert torch.isfinite(mask).all().item()
            assert 0 <= image.min().item() <= image.max().item() <= 1
            assert ((mask == 0) | (mask == 1)).all().item()
            assert mask.any().item() == (label(record) == "tampered")
            identity = {
                "image_path": str(record["image_path"]),
                "mask_path": str(record["mask_path"]),
                "source_group": str(record["source_group"]),
                "label": label(record),
            }
            digest.update(json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True).encode())
            digest.update(image.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
            digest.update(mask.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
        return digest.hexdigest()
    for name, subset, records in [
        ("train", train_subset, train_records),
        ("val", val_subset, val_records),
    ]:
        if fingerprint(subset, records) != config[f"{name}_sha256"]:
            raise RuntimeError(f"{name}: fingerprint mismatch.")
    print("Overlap checks: PASSED | Fingerprints: MATCH")
    auth_ids = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records) if label(r) == "authentic"
    ]
    tamp_ids = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records) if label(r) == "tampered"
    ]
    assert len(auth_ids) == len(tamp_ids) == 128
    assert sum(label(r) == "tampered" for r in val_records) == 64
    # Precompute one common schedule for both branches.
    schedules = {}
    for epoch in range(101, END_EPOCH + 1):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(config["seed"] + epoch)
        auth = rng.permutation(auth_ids)
        tamp = rng.permutation(tamp_ids)
        batches = []
        for start in range(0, 128, 4):
            batch = np.concatenate([
                auth[start:start + 4], tamp[start:start + 4]
            ])
            rng.shuffle(batch)
            batches.append(batch.tolist())
        rng.shuffle(batches)
        assert sorted(i for b in batches for i in b) == list(range(256))
        assert all(sum(i in auth_ids for i in b) == 4 for b in batches)
        schedules[epoch] = batches
    schedule_json = json.dumps(schedules, sort_keys=True)
    schedule_hash = hashlib.sha256(schedule_json.encode()).hexdigest()
    (experiment_dir / "batch_schedule.json").write_text(schedule_json)
    eval_loaders = {
        "train": DataLoader(
            train_subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
        ),
        "val": DataLoader(
            val_subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
        ),
    }
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        dice_loss = 1 - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
--- PAGE 176 ---
        return bce + dice_loss, bce, dice_loss
    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate(model, loader, records, branch, epoch, split):
        model.eval()
        total_loss = total_bce = total_dice = 0.0
        samples = tp = fp = fn = predicted = pixels = gt_pixels = 0
        auth_fp = auth_pixels = 0
        probability_gt = probability_bg = 0.0
        max_probability = 0.0
        details = []
        offset = 0
        for images, masks in loader:
            images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
            logits = model(images)
            loss, bce, dice_loss = loss_parts(logits, masks)
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation loss.")
            n = images.shape[0]
            samples += n
            total_loss += loss.item() * n
            total_bce += bce.item() * n
            total_dice += dice_loss.item() * n
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            truth = masks.bool()
            pred = probs > 0.5
            pixels += truth.numel()
            gt_pixels += truth.sum().item()
            predicted += pred.sum().item()
            probability_gt += probs[truth].double().sum().item()
            probability_bg += probs[~truth].double().sum().item()
            max_probability = max(max_probability, probs.max().item())
            for j in range(n):
                record = records[offset + j]
                p, gt = pred[j], truth[j]
                itp = (p & gt).sum().item()
                ifp = (p & ~gt).sum().item()
                ifn = (~p & gt).sum().item()
                tp += itp
                fp += ifp
                fn += ifn
                if label(record) == "tampered":
                    dice = 2 * itp / (2 * itp + ifp + ifn)
                else:
                    dice = None
                    auth_fp += p.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += p.numel()
                details.append({
                    "branch": branch,
                    "epoch": epoch,
                    "split": split,
                    "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                    "label": label(record),
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * p.float().mean().item(),
                    "tp": itp, "fp": ifp, "fn": ifn,
                })
            offset += n
        dices = [r["dice_05"] for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        metrics = {
            "loss": total_loss / samples,
            "weighted_bce": total_bce / samples,
            "mean_batch_dice_loss": total_dice / samples,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "mean_tampered_dice": float(np.mean(dices)),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(d < .1 for d in dices),
            "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * predicted / pixels,
--- PAGE 177 ---
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_fp / max(auth_pixels, 1),
            "mean_probability_in_mask": probability_gt / gt_pixels,
            "mean_probability_background": probability_bg / (pixels - gt_pixels),
            "max_probability": max_probability,
        }
        if not all(np.isfinite(v) for v in metrics.values()):
            raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation metrics.")
        return metrics, details
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_det = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    old_algorithms = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    combined = []
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        for branch, lr in BRANCHES.items():
            branch_dir = experiment_dir / branch
            branch_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            last_path = branch_dir / "last.pt"
            with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
                model = UNet(
                    in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
                ).to(device)
                optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                    model.parameters(), lr=lr, weight_decay=1e-4
                )
                history, details_history, online_history = [], [], []
                best_score, best_epoch, best_state = -1.0, None, None
                if last_path.exists():
                    state = torch.load(
                        last_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
                    )
                    if (
                        state.get("kind") != "epoch100_lr_fork_v1"
                        or state["branch"] != branch
                        or state["branch_lr"] != lr
                        or state["config"] != config
                        or state["schedule_sha256"] != schedule_hash
                    ):
                        raise RuntimeError("Invalid branch resume checkpoint.")
                    history = state["history"]
                    details_history = state["details_history"]
                    online_history = state["online_history"]
                    best_score = state["best_score"]
                    best_epoch = state["best_epoch"]
                    best_state = state["best_model_state"]
                    start_epoch = state["epoch"] + 1
                else:
                    state = source
                    start_epoch = 100
                model.load_state_dict(state["model_state"])
                optimizer.load_state_dict(state["optimizer_state"])
                # Set LR AFTER restoring AdamW state.
                for group in optimizer.param_groups:
                    group["lr"] = lr
                    assert group["weight_decay"] == 1e-4
                torch.set_rng_state(state["torch_rng"])
                if device.type == "cuda" and state["cuda_rng"] is not None:
                    torch.cuda.set_rng_state(state["cuda_rng"], device)
                if state is not source:
                    del state
--- PAGE 178 ---
                print(f"\n=== {branch} | LR={lr} | Start={start_epoch} ===")
                for epoch in range(start_epoch, END_EPOCH + 1):
                    if epoch > 100:
                        model.train()
                        loader = DataLoader(
                            train_subset,
                            batch_sampler=schedules[epoch],
                            num_workers=0,
                            pin_memory=device.type == "cuda",
                        )
                        loss_sum = count = 0
                        for images, masks in loader:
                            images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
                            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                            logits = model(images)
                            loss, _, _ = loss_parts(logits, masks)
                            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite training loss.")
                            loss.backward()
                            for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                                if not parameter.requires_grad:
                                    continue
                                if parameter.grad is None:
                                    raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                                if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                                    raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                            optimizer.step()
                            loss_sum += loss.item() * images.shape[0]
                            count += images.shape[0]
                        online_history.append({
                            "epoch": epoch,
                            "online_training_loss": loss_sum / count,
                        })
                    if epoch % 5 == 0:
                        train_metrics, td = evaluate(
                            model, eval_loaders["train"], train_records,
                            branch, epoch, "train"
                        )
                        val_metrics, vd = evaluate(
                            model, eval_loaders["val"], val_records,
                            branch, epoch, "val"
                        )
                        history.append({
                            "branch": branch,
                            "epoch": epoch,
                            "lr": lr,
                            "total_optimizer_steps": epoch * 32,
                            "additional_steps": (epoch - 100) * 32,
                            **{f"train_{k}": v for k, v in train_metrics.items()},
                            **{f"val_{k}": v for k, v in val_metrics.items()},
                        })
                        details_history.extend(td + vd)
                        score = val_metrics["mean_tampered_dice"]
                        if score > best_score:
                            best_score, best_epoch = score, epoch
                            best_state = cpu_copy(model.state_dict())
                        print(
                            f"{branch} | Epoch {epoch} | "
                            f"Train Dice {train_metrics['micro_dice']:.4f} | "
                            f"Val Dice {val_metrics['micro_dice']:.4f} | "
                            f"Val Mean Tp {score:.4f} | "
                            f"Train p(mask/bg) "
                            f"{train_metrics['mean_probability_in_mask']:.4f}/"
                            f"{train_metrics['mean_probability_background']:.4f}",
                            flush=True,
                        )
--- PAGE 179 ---
                    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                    atomic_save({
                        "kind": "epoch100_lr_fork_v1",
                        "branch": branch,
                        "branch_lr": lr,
                        "epoch": epoch,
                        "config": config,
                        "schedule_sha256": schedule_hash,
                        "model_state": cpu_copy(model.state_dict()),
                        "optimizer_state": cpu_copy(optimizer.state_dict()),
                        "history": history,
                        "details_history": details_history,
                        "online_history": online_history,
                        "best_score": best_score,
                        "best_epoch": best_epoch,
                        "best_model_state": best_state,
                        "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                        "cuda_rng": (
                            torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                            if device.type == "cuda" else None
                        ),
                    }, last_path)
                    if epoch % 5 == 0:
                        atomic_save({
                            "kind": "epoch100_lr_fork_best_weights",
                            "branch": branch,
                            "epoch": best_epoch,
                            "branch_lr": lr,
                            "config": config,
                            "validation_mean_tampered_dice": best_score,
                            "model_state": best_state,
                        }, branch_dir / "best_val_macro.pt")
                    pd.DataFrame(history).to_csv(
                        branch_dir / "evaluations.csv", index=False
                    )
                    pd.DataFrame(details_history).to_csv(
                        branch_dir / "per_image.csv", index=False
                    )
                    pd.DataFrame(online_history).to_csv(
                        branch_dir / "online_history.csv", index=False
                    )
                combined.extend(history)
                del model, optimizer, best_state
                gc.collect()
                if torch.cuda.is_available():
                    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    finally:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_det
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_algorithms, warn_only=old_warn
        )
    results = pd.DataFrame(combined)
    results.to_csv(experiment_dir / "comparison.csv", index=False)
    return results, experiment_dir
# Move previous diagnostic models off GPU.
for name in ("diag32_model", "model256", "evaluation_model"):

        globals()[name].cpu()
gc.collect()

# ============================================================
# READ-ONLY DIAGNOSTIC:
# epoch 100 + A epoch 125 + B epoch 125
# Output head, activation, gradients and possible logit ceiling.
# ============================================================
import gc
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from IPython.display import display
from forgery_localization.models.unet import UNet
EXPERIMENT_DIR = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/diagnostics/"
    "fork256_epoch100_20261116_120030_757311"
)
CHECKPOINTS = {
    "SOURCE_E100": EXPERIMENT_DIR / "source_epoch100.pt",
    "A_E125": EXPERIMENT_DIR / "A_LR1e3/last.pt",
    "B_E125": EXPERIMENT_DIR / "B_LR3e4/last.pt",
}
def diagnose_heads():
    if "train_dataset" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError("Chạy startup để tạo train_dataset trước.")
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. One identical, fixed, balanced batch for all checkpoints.
    # --------------------------------------------------------
    table = pd.read_csv(
        EXPERIMENT_DIR / "train256.csv",
        keep_default_na=False,
    )
    assert len(table) == 256
    def normalized_label(value):
        value = str(value).lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(value, value)
    auth = table[
        table["label"].map(normalized_label) == "authentic"
    ].head(4)
    tamp = table[
        table["label"].map(normalized_label) == "tampered"
    ].head(4)
    chosen = pd.concat([auth, tamp], ignore_index=True)
    assert len(chosen) == 8
    samples = []
    for row in chosen.itertuples(index=False):
        record = train_dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
        if (
            Path(record["image_path"]).name != row.filename
            or str(record["label"]) != str(row.label)
            or str(record["source_group"]) != str(row.source_group)
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Sample identity mismatch: {row.dataset_index}"
            )
        samples.append(train_dataset[int(row.dataset_index)])
    images_cpu = torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]).float()
--- PAGE 182 ---
    masks_cpu = torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]).float()
    del samples
    assert images_cpu.shape == (8, 3, 256, 256)
    assert masks_cpu.shape == (8, 1, 256, 256)
    assert torch.isfinite(images_cpu).all().item()
    assert torch.isfinite(masks_cpu).all().item()
    assert ((masks_cpu == 0) | (masks_cpu == 1)).all().item()
    assert not masks_cpu[:4].any().item()
    assert masks_cpu[4:].flatten(1).any(1).all().item()
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    digest.update(images_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    digest.update(masks_cpu.numpy().tobytes())
    print("Batch tensor SHA256:", digest.hexdigest())
    print(f"Batch GT foreground: {100 * masks_cpu.mean().item():.4f}%")
    display(chosen[["dataset_index", "filename", "label"]])
    images = images_cpu.to(device)
    masks = masks_cpu.to(device)
    # Fingerprint all state tensors before/after backward.
    def state_digest(model):
        result = hashlib.sha256()
        for name, tensor in model.state_dict().items():
            result.update(name.encode())
            result.update(
                tensor.detach().cpu().contiguous().numpy().tobytes()
            )
        return result.hexdigest()
    def tensor_stats(tensor):
        values = tensor.detach().float()
        return {
            "min": values.min().item(),
            "max": values.max().item(),
            "mean": values.mean().item(),
            "std": values.std(unbiased=False).item(),
            "zero_pct": 100 * (values == 0).float().mean().item(),
            "positive_pct": 100 * (values > 0).float().mean().item(),
            "negative_pct": 100 * (values < 0).float().mean().item(),
            "finite": bool(torch.isfinite(values).all().item()),
        }
    head_rows = []
    activation_rows = []
    channel_rows = []
    gradient_rows = []
    summary_rows = []
    for name, path in CHECKPOINTS.items():
        saved = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
        expected_epoch = 100 if name == "SOURCE_E100" else 125
        if saved.get("epoch") != expected_epoch:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"{name}: checkpoint epoch={saved.get('epoch')}, "
                f"expected={expected_epoch}"
            )
        model = UNet(
            in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
        ).to(device)
        model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
        del saved
        # eval() freezes any stateful training behavior, but still allows
        # autograd when not inside no_grad()/inference_mode().
        model.eval()
        model.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        before = state_digest(model)
        captured = {}
        def capture_head_input(module, inputs):
            activation = inputs[0]
            activation.retain_grad()
--- PAGE 183 ---
            captured["activation"] = activation
        hook = model.head.register_forward_pre_hook(capture_head_input)
        try:
            logits = model(images)
            activation = captured["activation"]
            assert logits.shape == masks.shape
            assert model.head.kernel_size == (1, 1)
            assert model.head.out_channels == 1
            pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
            bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                logits.float(), masks, pos_weight=pos_weight
            )
            probabilities = logits.float().sigmoid()
            intersection = (probabilities * masks).sum()
            denominator = probabilities.sum() + masks.sum()
            dice_loss = 1 - (
                (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
            )
            loss = bce + dice_loss
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError(f"{name}: nonfinite loss")
            # Parameter statistics.
            for parameter_name, parameter in model.head.named_parameters():
                head_rows.append({
                    "checkpoint": name,
                    "parameter": parameter_name,
                    **tensor_stats(parameter),
                })
            activation_rows.append({
                "checkpoint": name,
                "shape": str(tuple(activation.shape)),
                **tensor_stats(activation),
            })
            # Channel statistics: zero here means zero on THIS batch only.
            channel_max = activation.detach().amax(dim=(0, 2, 3))
            channel_min = activation.detach().amin(dim=(0, 2, 3))
            channel_zero_pct = (
                100 * (activation.detach() == 0).float().mean(dim=(0, 2, 3))
            )
            weights = model.head.weight.detach().flatten()
            for channel in range(activation.shape[1]):
                channel_rows.append({
                    "checkpoint": name,
                    "channel": channel,
                    "head_weight": weights[channel].item(),
                    "activation_min": channel_min[channel].item(),
                    "activation_max": channel_max[channel].item(),
                    "activation_zero_pct": channel_zero_pct[channel].item(),
                })
            bias = model.head.bias.detach()[0].item()
            all_weights_nonpositive = bool((weights <= 0).all().item())
            activation_nonnegative = bool((activation >= 0).all().item())
            # Exact reconstruction for the existing 1x1, single-output head.
            reconstructed = (
                activation.detach()
                * model.head.weight.detach()[0, :, 0, 0][None, :, None, None]
            ).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True) + bias
            reconstruction_error = (
                reconstructed - logits.detach()
            ).abs().max().item()
            # This is a mathematical ceiling only when BOTH conditions hold.
            ceiling_applies = (
                all_weights_nonpositive and activation_nonnegative
            )
--- PAGE 184 ---
            bias_probability = torch.sigmoid(
                torch.tensor(bias, dtype=torch.float64)
            ).item()
            # Backward only. No optimizer exists and no update is performed.
            loss.backward()
            watched_modules = (
                "head.",
                "decoder1.",
                "bottleneck.",
                "encoder1.",
            )
            for parameter_name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                if not parameter_name.startswith(watched_modules):
                    continue
                gradient = parameter.grad
                gradient_rows.append({
                    "checkpoint": name,
                    "tensor": parameter_name,
                    "gradient_missing": gradient is None,
                    "gradient_finite": (
                        bool(torch.isfinite(gradient).all().item())
                        if gradient is not None else False
                    ),
                    "gradient_l2": (
                        gradient.detach().float().norm().item()
                        if gradient is not None else np.nan
                    ),
                    "gradient_abs_mean": (
                        gradient.detach().float().abs().mean().item()
                        if gradient is not None else np.nan
                    ),
                    "gradient_abs_max": (
                        gradient.detach().float().abs().max().item()
                        if gradient is not None else np.nan
                    ),
                    "gradient_nonzero_pct": (
                        100 * (gradient != 0).float().mean().item()
                        if gradient is not None else np.nan
                    ),
                    "parameter_l2": parameter.detach().float().norm().item(),
                })
            activation_gradient = activation.grad
            gradient_rows.append({
                "checkpoint": name,
                "tensor": "activation_before_head",
                "gradient_missing": activation_gradient is None,
                "gradient_finite": (
                    bool(torch.isfinite(activation_gradient).all().item())
                    if activation_gradient is not None else False
                ),
                "gradient_l2": (
                    activation_gradient.float().norm().item()
                    if activation_gradient is not None else np.nan
                ),
                "gradient_abs_mean": (
                    activation_gradient.float().abs().mean().item()
                    if activation_gradient is not None else np.nan
                ),
                "gradient_abs_max": (
                    activation_gradient.float().abs().max().item()
                    if activation_gradient is not None else np.nan
                ),
                "gradient_nonzero_pct": (
                    100 * (activation_gradient != 0).float().mean().item()
                    if activation_gradient is not None else np.nan
                ),
                "parameter_l2": np.nan,
            })
            unchanged = before == state_digest(model)
            assert unchanged, "Unexpected change to model state."
--- PAGE 185 ---
            summary_rows.append({
                "checkpoint": name,
                "epoch": expected_epoch,
                "batch_bce": bce.item(),
                "batch_dice_loss": dice_loss.item(),
                "batch_loss": loss.item(),
                "head_bias": bias,
                "sigmoid_head_bias": bias_probability,
                "all_head_weights_nonpositive": all_weights_nonpositive,
                "head_input_nonnegative": activation_nonnegative,
                "bias_ceiling_applies_on_batch": ceiling_applies,
                "channels_all_zero_on_batch": int((channel_max == 0).sum().item()),
                "total_channels": activation.shape[1],
                "batch_max_logit": logits.detach().max().item(),
                "batch_max_probability": probabilities.detach().max().item(),
                "batch_predicted_positive_pct": (
                    100 * (probabilities.detach() > .5).float().mean().item()
                ),
                "head_reconstruction_max_abs_error": reconstruction_error,
                "model_state_unchanged": unchanged,
            })
            print(
                f"{name} | bias={bias:.6f} | "
                f"sigmoid(bias)={bias_probability:.6f} | "
                f"max probability={probabilities.detach().max().item():.6f} | "
                f"bias ceiling applies={ceiling_applies} | "
                f"state unchanged={unchanged}"
            )
        finally:
            hook.remove()
            model.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        del model, logits, activation, probabilities, loss, bce, dice_loss
        del reconstructed, captured, activation_gradient
        gc.collect()
        if device.type == "cuda":
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    return (
        pd.DataFrame(summary_rows),
        pd.DataFrame(head_rows),
        pd.DataFrame(activation_rows),
        pd.DataFrame(channel_rows),
        pd.DataFrame(gradient_rows),
    )
# Move earlier diagnostic models to CPU to free GPU memory.
for variable_name in (
    "model256", "diag32_model", "evaluation_model",
):
    if variable_name in globals():
        globals()[variable_name].cpu()
gc.collect()
if torch.cuda.is_available():
    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
(
    head_summary,
    head_parameters,
    head_activations,
    head_channels,
    head_gradients,
) = diagnose_heads()
print("\n1. SUMMARY / POSSIBLE OUTPUT CEILING")
display(head_summary.round(8))
print("\n2. OUTPUT HEAD PARAMETERS")
display(head_parameters.round(8))

    # Eight disjoint balanced batches: 32 Au + 32 Tp in total.
    auth = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(records)
        if normalized_label(r) == "authentic"
    ]
    tamp = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(records)
        if normalized_label(r) == "tampered"
    ]
    assert len(auth) == len(tamp) == 128
    rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
    auth = rng.permutation(auth)
    tamp = rng.permutation(tamp)
    gradient_batches = []
    for start in range(0, 32, 4):
        batch = np.concatenate([
            auth[start:start + 4], tamp[start:start + 4]
        ])
        rng.shuffle(batch)
        gradient_batches.append(batch.tolist())
    # Build these tensors once, guaranteeing identical inputs for both models.
    gradient_inputs = []
    gradient_selection = []
    for batch_id, batch in enumerate(gradient_batches):
        samples = [subset[i] for i in batch]
        gradient_inputs.append((
            torch.stack([x for x, _ in samples]),
            torch.stack([y for _, y in samples]),

        for position in batch:

                "label": normalized_label(records[position]),

        subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
    )
    def state_hash(model):
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        for name, tensor in model.state_dict().items():
            digest.update(name.encode())
            digest.update(
                tensor.detach().cpu().contiguous().numpy().tobytes()
            )
        return digest.hexdigest()
    def safe_div(numerator, denominator):
        return numerator / denominator if denominator else np.nan

Không tạo optimizer, không cập nhật trọng số hoặc ghi file.

    channel_rows = []
    contribution_rows = []
    gradient_rows = []
    model_rows = []
    for model_name, path in CHECKPOINTS.items():
        saved = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
        expected_epoch = 100 if model_name == "MODEL256_E100" else 300
        expected_kind = (
            "train256_val128_v1"
            if model_name == "MODEL256_E100"
            else "fixed32_drive_v1"
        )
        if (
            saved.get("epoch") != expected_epoch
            or saved.get("kind") != expected_kind
        ):
            raise RuntimeError(f"Unexpected checkpoint: {model_name}")
        model = UNet(
            in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
        ).to(device)
        model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
        del saved
        model.eval()
        before_hash = state_hash(model)
        captured = {}
        def capture_activation(module, inputs):
            captured["activation"] = inputs[0]
        hook = model.head.register_forward_pre_hook(capture_activation)
        weights = model.head.weight.detach().flatten()
        bias = model.head.bias.detach()[0].item()
        channels = len(weights)
        active_pixels = np.zeros(channels, dtype=np.int64)
        active_images = np.zeros(channels, dtype=np.int64)
        activation_sum = np.zeros(channels, dtype=np.float64)
        activation_max = np.zeros(channels, dtype=np.float64)
        gt_activation_sum = np.zeros(channels, dtype=np.float64)
        bg_activation_sum = np.zeros(channels, dtype=np.float64)
        region_sums = {
            "mask": {"positive": 0., "negative": 0., "logit": 0., "prob": 0.},
            "background": {"positive": 0., "negative": 0., "logit": 0., "prob": 0.},
        }
        pixels = gt_pixels = bg_pixels = 0
        max_probability = 0.0
        reconstruction_error = 0.0
        nonnegative_everywhere = True
        try:
            # ------------------------------------------------
            # Full TRAIN256 scan, no computation graph.
            # ------------------------------------------------
            with torch.inference_mode():
                for images, masks in scan_loader:
                    images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
                    logits = model(images)
                    activation = captured.pop("activation")
                    if not (
                        torch.isfinite(logits).all().item()
                        and torch.isfinite(activation).all().item()
                    ):
                        raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite activation/logits.")
                    nonnegative_everywhere &= bool(
                        (activation >= 0).all().item()
                    )
                    truth = masks[:, 0].bool()
                    gt_count = truth.sum().item()
--- PAGE 190 ---
                    bg_count = (~truth).sum().item()
                    gt_pixels += gt_count
                    bg_pixels += bg_count
                    pixels += truth.numel()
                    active_pixels += (
                        (activation != 0).sum((0, 2, 3)).cpu().numpy()
                    )
                    active_images += (
                        (activation != 0).flatten(2).any(2)
                        .sum(0).cpu().numpy()
                    )
                    activation_sum += (
                        activation.double().sum((0, 2, 3)).cpu().numpy()
                    )
                    activation_max = np.maximum(
                        activation_max,
                        activation.amax((0, 2, 3)).cpu().numpy()
                    )
                    # Channel-major indexing for foreground/background sums.
                    channel_first = activation.permute(1, 0, 2, 3)
                    gt_activation_sum += (
                        channel_first[:, truth].double().sum(1).cpu().numpy()
                    )
                    bg_activation_sum += (
                        channel_first[:, ~truth].double().sum(1).cpu().numpy()
                    )
                    # Each channel's signed contribution to the output logit.
                    contributions = activation * weights[None, :, None, None]
                    positive = contributions.clamp_min(0).sum(1)
                    negative = contributions.clamp_max(0).sum(1)
                    logit_map = logits[:, 0]
                    probability = logit_map.sigmoid()
                    error = (
                        positive + negative + bias - logit_map
                    ).abs().max().item()
                    reconstruction_error = max(reconstruction_error, error)
                    max_probability = max(
                        max_probability, probability.max().item()
                    )
                    for region, selector in [
                        ("mask", truth), ("background", ~truth)
                    ]:
                        for key, values in [
                            ("positive", positive),
                            ("negative", negative),
                            ("logit", logit_map),
                            ("prob", probability),
                        ]:
                            region_sums[region][key] += (
                                values[selector].double().sum().item()
                            )
                    del activation, channel_first, contributions
            weight_cpu = weights.cpu().numpy()
            for channel in range(channels):
                mask_mean = safe_div(gt_activation_sum[channel], gt_pixels)
                bg_mean = safe_div(bg_activation_sum[channel], bg_pixels)
                channel_rows.append({
                    "model": model_name,
                    "channel": channel,
                    "head_weight": float(weight_cpu[channel]),
                    "active_pixel_pct": 100 * active_pixels[channel] / pixels,
                    "images_active": int(active_images[channel]),
                    "images_total": 256,
                    "activation_mean": activation_sum[channel] / pixels,
                    "activation_max": activation_max[channel],
                    "activation_mean_mask": mask_mean,
                    "activation_mean_background": bg_mean,
                    "mean_logit_contribution_mask": weight_cpu[channel] * mask_mean,
                    "mean_logit_contribution_background": weight_cpu[channel] * bg_mean,
                })
--- PAGE 191 ---
            for region, count in [
                ("mask", gt_pixels), ("background", bg_pixels)
            ]:
                sums = region_sums[region]
                contribution_rows.append({
                    "model": model_name,
                    "region": region,
                    "pixels": count,
                    "mean_positive_contribution": sums["positive"] / count,
                    "mean_negative_contribution": sums["negative"] / count,
                    "head_bias": bias,
                    "mean_logit": sums["logit"] / count,
                    "mean_probability": sums["prob"] / count,
                })
            print(f"{model_name}: full TRAIN256 scan completed.")
            # ------------------------------------------------
            # Backward on the same 8 balanced batches.
            # No optimizer or parameter updates.
            # ------------------------------------------------
            pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
            for batch_id, (images_cpu, masks_cpu) in enumerate(gradient_inputs):
                images = images_cpu.to(device)
                masks = masks_cpu.to(device)
                model.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                logits = model(images)
                activation = captured.pop("activation")
                activation.retain_grad()
                probs = logits.float().sigmoid()
                bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                    logits.float(), masks, pos_weight=pos_weight
                )
                intersection = (probs * masks).sum()
                denominator = probs.sum() + masks.sum()
                dice_loss = 1 - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
                loss = bce + dice_loss
                if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                    raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite diagnostic loss.")
                loss.backward()
                watched = (
                    "encoder1.", "bottleneck.", "decoder1.", "head."
                )
                tensors = [
                    (name, parameter.grad, parameter)
                    for name, parameter in model.named_parameters()
                    if name.startswith(watched)
                ]
                tensors.append((
                    "activation_before_head", activation.grad, None
                ))
                for tensor_name, grad, parameter in tensors:
                    gradient_rows.append({
                        "model": model_name,
                        "batch_id": batch_id,
                        "tensor": tensor_name,
                        "batch_loss": loss.item(),
                        "gradient_missing": grad is None,
                        "gradient_finite": (
                            bool(torch.isfinite(grad).all().item())
                            if grad is not None else False
                        ),
                        "gradient_l2": (
                            grad.float().norm().item()
                            if grad is not None else np.nan
                        ),
                        "gradient_abs_mean": (
                            grad.float().abs().mean().item()
                            if grad is not None else np.nan
                        ),
--- PAGE 192 ---
                        "gradient_nonzero_pct": (
                            100 * (grad != 0).float().mean().item()
                            if grad is not None else np.nan
                        ),
                        "parameter_l2": (
                            parameter.detach().float().norm().item()
                            if parameter is not None else np.nan
                        ),
                    })
                del logits, activation, probs, bce, dice_loss, loss
                del tensors
            model.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            unchanged = before_hash == state_hash(model)
            assert unchanged, "Model state unexpectedly changed."
            model_rows.append({
                "model": model_name,
                "epoch": expected_epoch,
                "all_head_inputs_nonnegative": nonnegative_everywhere,
                "head_positive_weights": int((weight_cpu > 0).sum()),
                "channels_zero_on_all_train_images": int((active_images == 0).sum()),
                "head_bias": bias,
                "max_probability_train256": max_probability,
                "max_reconstruction_error": reconstruction_error,
                "model_state_unchanged": unchanged,
            })
            print(f"{model_name}: 8 backward batches completed; state unchanged.")
        finally:
            hook.remove()
            model.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        captured.clear()
        del model, weights
        gc.collect()
        if device.type == "cuda":
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    channel_df = pd.DataFrame(channel_rows)
    contribution_df = pd.DataFrame(contribution_rows)
    gradient_df = pd.DataFrame(gradient_rows)
    model_df = pd.DataFrame(model_rows)
    # Summaries retain batch variation, rather than reporting one gradient.
    gradient_summary = gradient_df.groupby(
        ["model", "tensor"], as_index=False
    ).agg(
        batches=("batch_id", "count"),
        any_missing=("gradient_missing", "max"),
        all_finite=("gradient_finite", "min"),
        grad_l2_min=("gradient_l2", "min"),
        grad_l2_median=("gradient_l2", "median"),
        grad_l2_max=("gradient_l2", "max"),
        grad_nonzero_pct_mean=("gradient_nonzero_pct", "mean"),
    )
    output = OUTPUT_ROOT / (
        "activation_gradient_scan_"
        + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
    )
    output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
    for frame, filename in [
        (model_df, "models.csv"),
        (channel_df, "channels.csv"),
        (contribution_df, "head_contributions.csv"),
        (gradient_df, "gradients_all_batches.csv"),
        (gradient_summary, "gradients_summary.csv"),
        (pd.DataFrame(gradient_selection), "gradient_batch_selection.csv"),
        (table, "train256_selection.csv"),
    ]:
        frame.to_csv(output / filename, index=False)
    print("\n1. MODEL SUMMARY")
    display(model_df)
--- PAGE 193 ---
    print("\n2. CHANNEL ACTIVATION — ALL TRAIN256")
    display(channel_df.round(8))
    print("\n3. HEAD CONTRIBUTIONS — MASK VS BACKGROUND")
    display(contribution_df.round(8))
    print("\n4. GRADIENT SUMMARY — 8 BALANCED BATCHES")
    with pd.option_context("display.float_format", "{:.5e}".format):
        display(gradient_summary)
    print("\nDiagnostic CSVs:", output)
    print("No optimizer, weight updates or checkpoint writes.")
    return model_df, channel_df, contribution_df, gradient_df, gradient_summary

def run_relu_comparison():
    if "train_dataset" not in globals() or "val_dataset" not in globals():

    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")

    if device.type == "cuda":

    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def atomic_save(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    if RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR is None:
        source = torch.load(
            SOURCE_DIR / "source_epoch100.pt",

            source.get("kind") != "train256_val128_v1"
            or source.get("epoch") != 100

            raise RuntimeError("Cần đúng source checkpoint epoch 100.")
        output = OUTPUT_ROOT / (
            "relu_vs_leaky_"

            "model_state": source["model_state"],

    def label(record):
        value = str(record["label"]).lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(value, value)
    def restore(dataset, filename, count):
        table = pd.read_csv(output / filename, keep_default_na=False)
        ids = table["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
        assert len(ids) == len(set(ids)) == count
        records = []
        for row in table.itertuples(index=False):
            record = dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
            if (
                Path(record["image_path"]).name != row.filename
                or str(record["label"]) != str(row.label)
                or str(record["source_group"]) != str(row.source_group)
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(f"Selection mismatch: {filename}")
            records.append(record)
        return Subset(dataset, ids), records

        def values(records):
            result = set()
            for record in records:
                if field not in record:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Missing field: {field}")
                value = record[field]
                if value is None or str(value) == "":
                    continue
                result.add(
                    str(Path(value).resolve())
                    if field.endswith("_path") else str(value)
                )
            return result
        if values(train_records) & values(val_records):
            raise RuntimeError(f"Cross-split overlap: {field}")
    def fingerprint(subset, records):
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        for i, record in enumerate(records):
            image, mask = subset[i]
            assert image.shape == (3, 256, 256)
            assert mask.shape == (1, 256, 256)
            assert image.dtype == mask.dtype == torch.float32
            assert torch.isfinite(image).all().item()
            assert torch.isfinite(mask).all().item()
            assert 0 <= image.min().item() <= image.max().item() <= 1
            assert ((mask == 0) | (mask == 1)).all().item()
            assert mask.any().item() == (label(record) == "tampered")
            identity = {
                "image_path": str(record["image_path"]),
                "mask_path": str(record["mask_path"]),
                "source_group": str(record["source_group"]),
                "label": label(record),
            }
            digest.update(json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True).encode())
            digest.update(image.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
            digest.update(mask.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())

        return digest.hexdigest()
    for name, subset, records in [

    auth = [

    # Match the previous schedule for source epochs 101–125.
    schedules = {}
    for local_epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):

        a, t = rng.permutation(auth), rng.permutation(tamp)

        for start in range(0, 128, 4):
