    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
--- PAGE 198 ---
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        dice_loss = 1 - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
        return bce + dice_loss, bce, dice_loss
    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate(model, loader, records, captured, branch, epoch, split):
        model.eval()
        loss_sum = bce_sum = dice_sum = 0.0
        samples = pixels = gt_pixels = predicted_pixels = 0
        tp = fp = fn = auth_fp = auth_pixels = 0
        probability_gt = probability_bg = 0.0
        positive_gt = positive_bg = negative_gt = negative_bg = 0.0
        max_probability = 0.0
        details = []
        offset = 0
        weights = model.head.weight.detach()[0, :, 0, 0]
        for images, masks in loader:
            images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
            logits = model(images)
            activation = captured.pop("activation")
            loss, bce, dice_loss = loss_parts(logits, masks)
            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation loss.")
            n = images.shape[0]
            samples += n
            loss_sum += loss.item() * n
            bce_sum += bce.item() * n
            dice_sum += dice_loss.item() * n
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            pred = probs > 0.5
            truth = masks.bool()
            truth2d = truth[:, 0]
            pixels += truth.numel()
            gt_pixels += truth.sum().item()
            predicted_pixels += pred.sum().item()
            probability_gt += probs[truth].double().sum().item()
            probability_bg += probs[~truth].double().sum().item()
            max_probability = max(max_probability, probs.max().item())
            # Signed contributions remain valid for negative activations too.
            contribution = activation * weights[None, :, None, None]
            positive_map = contribution.clamp_min(0).sum(1)
            negative_map = contribution.clamp_max(0).sum(1)
            positive_gt += positive_map[truth2d].double().sum().item()
            positive_bg += positive_map[~truth2d].double().sum().item()
            negative_gt += negative_map[truth2d].double().sum().item()
            negative_bg += negative_map[~truth2d].double().sum().item()
            for j in range(n):
                record = records[offset + j]
                p, gt = pred[j], truth[j]
                itp = (p & gt).sum().item()
                ifp = (p & ~gt).sum().item()
                ifn = (~p & gt).sum().item()
                tp += itp
                fp += ifp
                fn += ifn
                if label(record) == "tampered":
                    dice = 2 * itp / (2 * itp + ifp + ifn)
                else:
                    dice = None
                    auth_fp += p.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += p.numel()
                details.append({
                    "branch": branch,
                    "local_epoch": epoch,
--- PAGE 199 ---
                    "split": split,
                    "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                    "label": label(record),
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * p.float().mean().item(),
                    "tp": itp, "fp": ifp, "fn": ifn,
                })
            offset += n
        dices = [r["dice_05"] for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        bg_pixels = pixels - gt_pixels
        metrics = {
            "loss": loss_sum / samples,
            "bce": bce_sum / samples,
            "dice_loss": dice_sum / samples,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "mean_tampered_dice": float(np.mean(dices)),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(d < .1 for d in dices),
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_fp / max(auth_pixels, 1),
            "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * predicted_pixels / pixels,
            "prob_in_mask": probability_gt / gt_pixels,
            "prob_background": probability_bg / bg_pixels,
            "max_probability": max_probability,
            "positive_contribution_mask": positive_gt / gt_pixels,
            "negative_contribution_mask": negative_gt / gt_pixels,
            "positive_contribution_background": positive_bg / bg_pixels,
            "negative_contribution_background": negative_bg / bg_pixels,
        }
        return metrics, details
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_det = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    old_algorithms = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    combined = []
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        for branch in ("A_RELU", "B_LEAKY"):
            branch_dir = output / branch
            branch_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            last_path = branch_dir / "last.pt"
            with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
                torch.manual_seed(SEED)
                model = UNet(
                    in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
                ).to(device)
                model.load_state_dict(initial["model_state"])
                # Verify the exact module being changed.
                final_activation = model.decoder1.conv.layers[-1]
                if not isinstance(final_activation, nn.ReLU):
                    raise RuntimeError("Unexpected decoder1 architecture.")
                if branch == "B_LEAKY":
                    model.decoder1.conv.layers[-1] = nn.LeakyReLU(
                        negative_slope=0.01, inplace=False
                    )
                # Fresh optimizer for BOTH branches; do not load source AdamW.
                optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                    model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=1e-4
                )
                captured = {}
                def capture(module, inputs):
                    captured["activation"] = inputs[0]
--- PAGE 200 ---
                hook = model.head.register_forward_pre_hook(capture)
                history, details_history, online_history, gradient_history = [], [], [], []
                best_score, best_epoch, best_state = -1.0, None, None
                start_epoch = 0
                if last_path.exists():
                    state = torch.load(
                        last_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
                    )
                    if (
                        state.get("kind") != "relu_leaky_fork_v1"
                        or state["branch"] != branch
                        or state["config"] != experiment_config
                    ):
                        raise RuntimeError("Resume mismatch.")
                    if state["local_epoch"] > EPOCHS:
                        raise RuntimeError("EPOCHS smaller than saved epoch.")
                    model.load_state_dict(state["model_state"])
                    optimizer.load_state_dict(state["optimizer_state"])
                    history = state["history"]
                    details_history = state["details_history"]
                    online_history = state["online_history"]
                    gradient_history = state["gradient_history"]
                    best_score = state["best_score"]
                    best_epoch = state["best_epoch"]
                    best_state = state["best_model_state"]
                    start_epoch = state["local_epoch"] + 1
                    torch.set_rng_state(state["torch_rng"])
                    if device.type == "cuda" and state["cuda_rng"] is not None:
                        torch.cuda.set_rng_state(state["cuda_rng"], device)
                    del state
                print(
                    f"\n=== {branch} | Start local epoch {start_epoch} ===",
                    flush=True
                )
                try:
                    for epoch in range(start_epoch, EPOCHS + 1):
                        if epoch > 0:
                            model.train()
                            loader = DataLoader(
                                train_subset,
                                batch_sampler=schedules[epoch],
                                num_workers=0,
                                pin_memory=device.type == "cuda",
                            )
                            online_sum = count = 0
                            grad_sums = {}
                            grad_counts = {}
                            for images, masks in loader:
                                images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
                                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                                logits = model(images)
                                captured.pop("activation")
                                loss, _, _ = loss_parts(logits, masks)
                                if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                                    raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite training loss.")
                                loss.backward()
                                for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                                    if not parameter.requires_grad:
                                        continue
                                    if parameter.grad is None:
                                        raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                                    if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                                        raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                                    # Mean batch gradient norms for final decoder conv.
                                    if name.startswith("decoder1.conv.layers.2."):
                                        grad_sums[name] = grad_sums.get(name, 0.) + (
                                            parameter.grad.float().norm().item()
                                        )
--- PAGE 201 ---
                                        grad_counts[name] = grad_counts.get(name, 0) + 1
                                optimizer.step()
                                online_sum += loss.item() * images.shape[0]
                                count += images.shape[0]
                            online_history.append({
                                "local_epoch": epoch,
                                "online_loss": online_sum / count,
                            })
                            for name in grad_sums:
                                gradient_history.append({
                                    "branch": branch,
                                    "local_epoch": epoch,
                                    "parameter": name,
                                    "mean_batch_gradient_l2": (
                                        grad_sums[name] / grad_counts[name]
                                    ),
                                })
                        if epoch % 5 == 0 or epoch == EPOCHS:
                            train_metrics, td = evaluate(
                                model, eval_loaders["train"], train_records,
                                captured, branch, epoch, "train"
                            )
                            val_metrics, vd = evaluate(
                                model, eval_loaders["val"], val_records,
                                captured, branch, epoch, "val"
                            )
                            history.append({
                                "branch": branch,
                                "local_epoch": epoch,
                                "additional_optimizer_steps": epoch * 32,
                                **{f"train_{k}": v for k, v in train_metrics.items()},
                                **{f"val_{k}": v for k, v in val_metrics.items()},
                            })
                            details_history.extend(td + vd)
                            score = val_metrics["mean_tampered_dice"]
                            if score > best_score:
                                best_score, best_epoch = score, epoch
                                best_state = cpu_copy(model.state_dict())
                            print(
                                f"{branch} | Local epoch {epoch:02d} | "
                                f"Train Dice {train_metrics['micro_dice']:.4f} | "
                                f"Train Mean Tp "
                                f"{train_metrics['mean_tampered_dice']:.4f} | "
                                f"Val Dice {val_metrics['micro_dice']:.4f} | "
                                f"Val Mean Tp {score:.4f} | "
                                f"Val authentic FP "
                                f"{val_metrics['authentic_fp_pct']:.3f}%",
                                flush=True,
                            )
                        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                        atomic_save({
                            "kind": "relu_leaky_fork_v1",
                            "branch": branch,
                            "local_epoch": epoch,
                            "config": experiment_config,
                            "model_state": cpu_copy(model.state_dict()),
                            "optimizer_state": cpu_copy(optimizer.state_dict()),
                            "history": history,
                            "details_history": details_history,
                            "online_history": online_history,
                            "gradient_history": gradient_history,
                            "best_score": best_score,
                            "best_epoch": best_epoch,
                            "best_model_state": best_state,
                            "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                            "cuda_rng": (
                                torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                                if device.type == "cuda" else None
                            ),
                        }, last_path)
--- PAGE 202 ---
                        if epoch % 5 == 0 or epoch == EPOCHS:
                            atomic_save({
                                "kind": "relu_leaky_best_weights",
                                "branch": branch,
                                "local_epoch": best_epoch,
                                "config": experiment_config,
                                "validation_mean_tampered_dice": best_score,
                                "model_state": best_state,
                            }, branch_dir / "best_val_macro.pt")
                        for data, filename in [
                            (history, "evaluations.csv"),
                            (details_history, "per_image.csv"),
                            (online_history, "online_history.csv"),
                            (gradient_history, "gradients.csv"),
                        ]:
                            pd.DataFrame(data).to_csv(
                                branch_dir / filename, index=False
                            )
                    combined.extend(history)
                finally:
                    hook.remove()
                    captured.clear()
                    model.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                del model, optimizer, best_state
                gc.collect()
                if device.type == "cuda":
                    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    finally:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_det
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_algorithms, warn_only=old_warn
        )
    results = pd.DataFrame(combined)
    results.to_csv(output / "comparison.csv", index=False)
    return results, output

# ============================================================
# FRESH INITIALIZATION: BCE + GLOBAL DICE vs BCE ONLY
# TRAIN256 / VAL128, balanced batches, 50 epochs.
# Exact pixel Average Precision (AP), not trapezoidal PR area.
# ============================================================
import copy
import gc
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from sklearn.metrics import average_precision_score
from torch.utils.data import DataLoader, Subset
--- PAGE 204 ---
from IPython.display import display
from forgery_localization.models.unet import UNet
SELECTION_DIR = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/diagnostics/"
    "fork256_epoch100_20261116_120030_757311"
)
OUTPUT_ROOT = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/diagnostics"
)
EPOCHS = 50
SEED = 42
LR = 1e-3
EVAL_EVERY = 5
# First run: None.
# Resume: paste the NEW experiment directory printed below.
RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR = None
BRANCHES = {
    "A_BCE_DICE": True,
    "B_BCE_ONLY": False,
}
def run_loss_comparison():
    if "train_dataset" not in globals() or "val_dataset" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError("Chạy startup trước.")
    if not Path("/content/drive/MyDrive").is_dir():
        raise RuntimeError("Google Drive chưa được mount.")
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def save_atomic(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    if RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR is None:
        output = OUTPUT_ROOT / (
            "bce_vs_dice_" + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
        )
        output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        for filename in ("train256.csv", "validation128.csv"):
            (output / filename).write_bytes(
                (SELECTION_DIR / filename).read_bytes()
            )
    else:
        output = Path(RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR)
    def label(record):
        value = str(record["label"]).lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(value, value)
    def restore(dataset, filename, count):
        table = pd.read_csv(output / filename, keep_default_na=False)
        indices = table["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
        assert len(indices) == len(set(indices)) == count
        records = []
--- PAGE 205 ---
        for row in table.itertuples(index=False):
            record = dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
            if (
                Path(record["image_path"]).name != row.filename
                or str(record["label"]) != str(row.label)
                or str(record["source_group"]) != str(row.source_group)
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(f"Selection mismatch: {filename}")
            records.append(record)
        return Subset(dataset, indices), records
    train_subset, train_records = restore(
        train_dataset, "train256.csv", 256
    )
    val_subset, val_records = restore(
        val_dataset, "validation128.csv", 128
    )
    for field in ("image_path", "mask_path", "source_group", "image_hash"):
        def values(records):
            result = set()
            for record in records:
                if field not in record:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Missing field: {field}")
                value = record[field]
                if value is None or str(value) == "":
                    continue
                result.add(
                    str(Path(value).resolve())
                    if field.endswith("_path") else str(value)
                )
            return result
        if values(train_records) & values(val_records):
            raise RuntimeError(f"Cross-split overlap: {field}")
    def fingerprint(subset, records):
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        for i, record in enumerate(records):
            image, mask = subset[i]
            assert image.shape == (3, 256, 256)
            assert mask.shape == (1, 256, 256)
            assert image.dtype == mask.dtype == torch.float32
            assert torch.isfinite(image).all().item()
            assert torch.isfinite(mask).all().item()
            assert 0 <= image.min().item() <= image.max().item() <= 1
            assert ((mask == 0) | (mask == 1)).all().item()
            assert mask.any().item() == (label(record) == "tampered")
            identity = {
                "image_path": str(record["image_path"]),
                "mask_path": str(record["mask_path"]),
                "source_group": str(record["source_group"]),
                "label": label(record),
            }
            digest.update(json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True).encode())
            digest.update(image.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
            digest.update(mask.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
        return digest.hexdigest()
    train_hash = fingerprint(train_subset, train_records)
    val_hash = fingerprint(val_subset, val_records)
    # Ensure these are also the same data as the earlier experiment.
    source = torch.load(
        SELECTION_DIR / "source_epoch100.pt",
        map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
    )
    if (
        source["config"]["train_sha256"] != train_hash
        or source["config"]["val_sha256"] != val_hash
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("Data differs from the previous TRAIN256/VAL128.")
    del source
    auth_ids = [
--- PAGE 206 ---
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records) if label(r) == "authentic"
    ]
    tamp_ids = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records) if label(r) == "tampered"
    ]
    assert len(auth_ids) == len(tamp_ids) == 128
    assert sum(label(r) == "tampered" for r in val_records) == 64
    # Deterministic schedule shared by both branches.
    def batches_for_epoch(epoch):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(SEED + epoch)
        auth = rng.permutation(auth_ids)
        tamp = rng.permutation(tamp_ids)
        batches = []
        for start in range(0, 128, 4):
            batch = np.concatenate([
                auth[start:start + 4], tamp[start:start + 4]
            ])
            rng.shuffle(batch)
            batches.append(batch.tolist())
        rng.shuffle(batches)
        return batches
    config = {
        "seed": SEED,
        "lr": LR,
        "weight_decay": 1e-4,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,
        "base_channels": 16,
        "eval_every": EVAL_EVERY,
        "threshold": 0.5,
        "train_sha256": train_hash,
        "val_sha256": val_hash,
        "sampling": "4Au_4Tp_seed_plus_epoch",
        "ap": "exact_pixel_average_precision_using_raw_logits",
    }
    config_path = output / "config.json"
    if config_path.exists():
        if json.loads(config_path.read_text()) != config:
            raise RuntimeError("Resume configuration mismatch.")
    else:
        config_path.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2))
    initial_path = output / "initial_weights.pt"
    if RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR is None:
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
            torch.manual_seed(SEED)
            initial_model = UNet(
                in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
            )
            save_atomic(cpu_copy(initial_model.state_dict()), initial_path)
            del initial_model
    initial_state = torch.load(
        initial_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
    )
    print("Overlap checks: PASSED | Fingerprints: MATCH")
    print("EXPERIMENT DIRECTORY:", output, flush=True)
    eval_loaders = {
        "train": DataLoader(
            train_subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
        ),
        "val": DataLoader(
            val_subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
        ),
    }
    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate(model, loader, records, include_dice, branch, epoch, split):
        model.eval()
        # Preallocate CPU arrays. Raw logits preserve ranking without
        # sigmoid saturation; AP is invariant under strictly monotone maps.
        n_pixels = len(records) * 256 * 256
--- PAGE 207 ---
        scores = np.empty(n_pixels, dtype=np.float32)
        targets_cpu = np.empty(n_pixels, dtype=np.bool_)
        pixel_offset = image_offset = 0
        bce_sum = foreground_bce_sum = background_bce_sum = 0.0
        batch_dice_sum = 0.0
        foreground_probability = background_probability = 0.0
        fg_pixels = bg_pixels = sample_count = 0
        tp = fp = fn = auth_fp = auth_pixels = pred_pixels = 0
        max_probability = 0.0
        details = []
        for images, masks in loader:
            images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
            logits = model(images).float()
            if not torch.isfinite(logits).all().item():
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite logits.")
            truth = masks.bool()
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            pred = probs > .5
            pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
            bce_map = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                logits, masks, pos_weight=pos_weight, reduction="none"
            )
            bce_sum += bce_map.double().sum().item()
            foreground_bce_sum += bce_map[truth].double().sum().item()
            background_bce_sum += bce_map[~truth].double().sum().item()
            intersection = (probs * masks).sum().item()
            denominator = probs.sum().item() + masks.sum().item()
            batch_dice_loss = 1 - (
                (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
            )
            batch_dice_sum += batch_dice_loss * images.shape[0]
            sample_count += images.shape[0]
            fg_pixels += truth.sum().item()
            bg_pixels += (~truth).sum().item()
            foreground_probability += probs[truth].double().sum().item()
            background_probability += probs[~truth].double().sum().item()
            max_probability = max(max_probability, probs.max().item())
            pred_pixels += pred.sum().item()
            length = logits.numel()
            scores[pixel_offset:pixel_offset + length] = (
                logits.cpu().numpy().reshape(-1)
            )
            targets_cpu[pixel_offset:pixel_offset + length] = (
                truth.cpu().numpy().reshape(-1)
            )
            pixel_offset += length
            for j in range(images.shape[0]):
                record = records[image_offset + j]
                p, gt = pred[j], truth[j]
                itp = (p & gt).sum().item()
                ifp = (p & ~gt).sum().item()
                ifn = (~p & gt).sum().item()
                tp += itp
                fp += ifp
                fn += ifn
                if label(record) == "tampered":
                    dice = 2 * itp / (2 * itp + ifp + ifn)
                else:
                    dice = None
                    auth_fp += p.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += p.numel()
                details.append({
                    "branch": branch, "epoch": epoch, "split": split,
                    "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                    "label": label(record),
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * p.float().mean().item(),
--- PAGE 208 ---
                    "tp": itp, "fp": ifp, "fn": ifn,
                })
            image_offset += images.shape[0]
        assert pixel_offset == n_pixels
        print(f"  {branch} epoch {epoch}: calculating {split} pixel AP...",
              flush=True)
        ap = float(average_precision_score(targets_cpu, scores))
        del scores, targets_cpu
        gc.collect()
        dices = [r["dice_05"] for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        bce = bce_sum / n_pixels
        dice_loss = batch_dice_sum / sample_count
        metrics = {
            "objective_loss": bce + (dice_loss if include_dice else 0),
            "weighted_bce": bce,
            # These two are conditional means, not additive components.
            "foreground_bce_mean": foreground_bce_sum / fg_pixels,
            "background_bce_mean": background_bce_sum / bg_pixels,
            "dice_loss_reference": dice_loss,
            "micro_dice_05": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "mean_tampered_dice_05": float(np.mean(dices)),
            "precision_05": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "recall_05": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(d < .1 for d in dices),
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_fp / max(auth_pixels, 1),
            "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * pred_pixels / n_pixels,
            "prob_in_mask": foreground_probability / fg_pixels,
            "prob_background": background_probability / bg_pixels,
            "max_probability": max_probability,
            "pixel_AP": ap,
            "foreground_prevalence_AP_baseline": fg_pixels / n_pixels,
        }
        return metrics, details
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_det = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    old_algorithms = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    combined = []
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        for branch, include_dice in BRANCHES.items():
            branch_dir = output / branch
            branch_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            last_path = branch_dir / "last.pt"
            with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
                torch.manual_seed(SEED)
                model = UNet(
                    in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
                ).to(device)
                model.load_state_dict(initial_state)
                optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                    model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=1e-4
                )
                pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
                history, per_image, online_history = [], [], []
                best_score, best_epoch, best_state = -1.0, None, None
                start_epoch = 0
                if last_path.exists():
                    saved = torch.load(
--- PAGE 209 ---
                        last_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
                    )
                    if (
                        saved.get("kind") != "bce_dice_comparison_v1"
                        or saved["branch"] != branch
                        or saved["include_dice"] != include_dice
                        or saved["config"] != config
                    ):
                        raise RuntimeError("Invalid resume checkpoint.")
                    if saved["epoch"] > EPOCHS:
                        raise RuntimeError("EPOCHS smaller than saved epoch.")
                    model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
                    optimizer.load_state_dict(saved["optimizer_state"])
                    history = saved["history"]
                    per_image = saved["per_image"]
                    online_history = saved["online_history"]
                    best_score = saved["best_score"]
                    best_epoch = saved["best_epoch"]
                    best_state = saved["best_model_state"]
                    start_epoch = saved["epoch"] + 1
                    torch.set_rng_state(saved["torch_rng"])
                    if device.type == "cuda" and saved["cuda_rng"] is not None:
                        torch.cuda.set_rng_state(saved["cuda_rng"], device)
                    del saved
                print(f"\n=== {branch} | Start epoch {start_epoch} ===")
                for epoch in range(start_epoch, EPOCHS + 1):
                    if epoch > 0:
                        loader = DataLoader(
                            train_subset,
                            batch_sampler=batches_for_epoch(epoch),
                            num_workers=0,
                            pin_memory=device.type == "cuda",
                        )
                        model.train()
                        online_sum = count = 0
                        for images, masks in loader:
                            images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
                            optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                            logits = model(images).float()
                            bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
                                logits, masks, pos_weight=pos_weight
                            )
                            if include_dice:
                                probs = logits.sigmoid()
                                intersection = (probs * masks).sum()
                                denominator = probs.sum() + masks.sum()
                                dice_loss = 1 - (
                                    (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
                                )
                                loss = bce + dice_loss
                            else:
                                loss = bce
                            if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite loss.")
                            loss.backward()
                            for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                                if not parameter.requires_grad:
                                    continue
                                if parameter.grad is None:
                                    raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                                if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                                    raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
                            optimizer.step()
                            online_sum += loss.item() * images.shape[0]
                            count += images.shape[0]
                        online_history.append({
                            "epoch": epoch,
--- PAGE 210 ---
                            "online_objective_loss": online_sum / count,
                        })
                    if epoch % EVAL_EVERY == 0 or epoch == EPOCHS:
                        train_metrics, td = evaluate(
                            model, eval_loaders["train"], train_records,
                            include_dice, branch, epoch, "train"
                        )
                        val_metrics, vd = evaluate(
                            model, eval_loaders["val"], val_records,
                            include_dice, branch, epoch, "val"
                        )
                        history.append({
                            "branch": branch,
                            "epoch": epoch,
                            "optimizer_steps": epoch * 32,
                            **{f"train_{k}": v for k, v in train_metrics.items()},
                            **{f"val_{k}": v for k, v in val_metrics.items()},
                        })
                        per_image.extend(td + vd)
                        # Keep the same checkpoint-selection rule as before.
                        score = val_metrics["mean_tampered_dice_05"]
                        if score > best_score:
                            best_score, best_epoch = score, epoch
                            best_state = cpu_copy(model.state_dict())
                        print(
                            f"{branch} | Epoch {epoch:02d} | "
                            f"Train Dice {train_metrics['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                            f"Train AP {train_metrics['pixel_AP']:.4f} | "
                            f"Val Dice {val_metrics['micro_dice_05']:.4f} | "
                            f"Val AP {val_metrics['pixel_AP']:.4f}",
                            flush=True,
                        )
                    optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                    save_atomic({
                        "kind": "bce_dice_comparison_v1",
                        "branch": branch,
                        "include_dice": include_dice,
                        "epoch": epoch,
                        "config": config,
                        "model_state": cpu_copy(model.state_dict()),
                        "optimizer_state": cpu_copy(optimizer.state_dict()),
                        "history": history,
                        "per_image": per_image,
                        "online_history": online_history,
                        "best_score": best_score,
                        "best_epoch": best_epoch,
                        "best_model_state": best_state,
                        "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                        "cuda_rng": (
                            torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                            if device.type == "cuda" else None
                        ),
                    }, last_path)
                    if epoch % EVAL_EVERY == 0 or epoch == EPOCHS:
                        save_atomic({
                            "kind": "bce_dice_best_weights",
                            "branch": branch,
                            "epoch": best_epoch,
                            "config": config,
                            "validation_mean_tampered_dice": best_score,
                            "model_state": best_state,
                        }, branch_dir / "best_val_macro.pt")
                    for data, filename in [
                        (history, "evaluations.csv"),
                        (per_image, "per_image.csv"),
                        (online_history, "online_history.csv"),
                    ]:
                        pd.DataFrame(data).to_csv(
                            branch_dir / filename, index=False
                        )
--- PAGE 211 ---
                combined.extend(history)
                del model, optimizer, best_state
                gc.collect()
                if device.type == "cuda":
                    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    finally:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_det
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_algorithms, warn_only=old_warn
        )
    results = pd.DataFrame(combined)
    results.to_csv(output / "comparison.csv", index=False)
    return results, output

SOURCE_DIR = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/diagnostics/"
    "fork256_epoch100_20261116_120030_757311"
)
OUTPUT_ROOT = Path(
    "/content/drive/MyDrive/ImageForgeryResearch/diagnostics"
)
SEEDS = [7, 21, 123]
EPOCHS = 50
LR = 1e-3
BATCH_SCHEDULE_SEED = 42
EVAL_EVERY = 5
# First run: None.
# Resume: paste the experiment directory printed by this cell.
RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR = None
def run_seed_study():
    if "train_dataset" not in globals() or "val_dataset" not in globals():
        raise RuntimeError("Chạy startup trước.")
    if not Path("/content/drive/MyDrive").is_dir():
        raise RuntimeError("Google Drive chưa được mount.")
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    print("Device:", device)
    if device.type == "cuda":
        print("GPU:", torch.cuda.get_device_name(device))
    def cpu_copy(value):
        if torch.is_tensor(value):
            return value.detach().cpu().clone()
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return {k: cpu_copy(v) for k, v in value.items()}
        if isinstance(value, list):
            return [cpu_copy(v) for v in value]
        if isinstance(value, tuple):
            return tuple(cpu_copy(v) for v in value)
        return copy.deepcopy(value)
    def save_atomic(payload, path):
        temporary = path.with_suffix(".pt.tmp")
        torch.save(payload, temporary)
        temporary.replace(path)
    if RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR is None:
        output = OUTPUT_ROOT / (
            "seed_study_" + datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")
        )
        output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=False)
        for filename in ("train256.csv", "validation128.csv"):
            (output / filename).write_bytes(
                (SOURCE_DIR / filename).read_bytes()
            )
    else:
        output = Path(RESUME_EXPERIMENT_DIR)
    def label(record):
        value = str(record["label"]).lower()
        return {"au": "authentic", "tp": "tampered"}.get(value, value)
    def restore(dataset, filename, count):
        table = pd.read_csv(output / filename, keep_default_na=False)
        indices = table["dataset_index"].astype(int).tolist()
        assert len(indices) == len(set(indices)) == count
        records = []
        for row in table.itertuples(index=False):
            record = dataset.records[int(row.dataset_index)]
            if (
                Path(record["image_path"]).name != row.filename
                or str(record["label"]) != str(row.label)
                or str(record["source_group"]) != str(row.source_group)
            ):
                raise RuntimeError(f"Selection mismatch: {filename}")
            records.append(record)
--- PAGE 214 ---
        return Subset(dataset, indices), records
    train_subset, train_records = restore(
        train_dataset, "train256.csv", 256
    )
    val_subset, val_records = restore(
        val_dataset, "validation128.csv", 128
    )
    for field in ("image_path", "mask_path", "source_group", "image_hash"):
        def values(records):
            result = set()
            for record in records:
                if field not in record:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Missing field: {field}")
                value = record[field]
                if value is None or str(value) == "":
                    continue
                result.add(
                    str(Path(value).resolve())
                    if field.endswith("_path") else str(value)
                )
            return result
        if values(train_records) & values(val_records):
            raise RuntimeError(f"Cross-split overlap: {field}")
    def fingerprint(subset, records):
        digest = hashlib.sha256()
        for i, record in enumerate(records):
            image, mask = subset[i]
            assert image.shape == (3, 256, 256)
            assert mask.shape == (1, 256, 256)
            assert image.dtype == mask.dtype == torch.float32
            assert torch.isfinite(image).all().item()
            assert torch.isfinite(mask).all().item()
            assert 0 <= image.min().item() <= image.max().item() <= 1
            assert ((mask == 0) | (mask == 1)).all().item()
            assert mask.any().item() == (label(record) == "tampered")
            identity = {
                "image_path": str(record["image_path"]),
                "mask_path": str(record["mask_path"]),
                "source_group": str(record["source_group"]),
                "label": label(record),
            }
            digest.update(json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True).encode())
            digest.update(image.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
            digest.update(mask.contiguous().numpy().tobytes())
        return digest.hexdigest()
    train_hash = fingerprint(train_subset, train_records)
    val_hash = fingerprint(val_subset, val_records)
    source = torch.load(
        SOURCE_DIR / "source_epoch100.pt",
        map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
    )
    if (
        source["config"]["train_sha256"] != train_hash
        or source["config"]["val_sha256"] != val_hash
    ):
        raise RuntimeError("Data fingerprint differs from earlier experiment.")
    del source
    auth = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records) if label(r) == "authentic"
    ]
    tamp = [
        i for i, r in enumerate(train_records) if label(r) == "tampered"
    ]
    assert len(auth) == len(tamp) == 128
    assert sum(label(r) == "tampered" for r in val_records) == 64
    schedules = {}
    for epoch in range(1, EPOCHS + 1):
        rng = np.random.default_rng(BATCH_SCHEDULE_SEED + epoch)
        a, t = rng.permutation(auth), rng.permutation(tamp)
--- PAGE 215 ---
        batches = []
        for start in range(0, 128, 4):
            batch = np.concatenate([
                a[start:start + 4], t[start:start + 4]
            ])
            rng.shuffle(batch)
            batches.append(batch.tolist())
        rng.shuffle(batches)
        assert sorted(i for b in batches for i in b) == list(range(256))
        schedules[epoch] = batches
    # Hash the schedule rule, allowing an interrupted run to resume.
    config = {
        "seeds": SEEDS,
        "lr": LR,
        "weight_decay": 1e-4,
        "pos_weight": 3.0,
        "base_channels": 16,
        "batch_schedule_seed": BATCH_SCHEDULE_SEED,
        "sampling": "4Au_4Tp_seed_plus_epoch",
        "loss": "weighted_bce_plus_batch_global_dice_smooth1",
        "eval_every": EVAL_EVERY,
        "threshold": 0.5,
        "train_sha256": train_hash,
        "val_sha256": val_hash,
    }
    config_path = output / "config.json"
    if config_path.exists():
        if json.loads(config_path.read_text()) != config:
            raise RuntimeError("Resume configuration mismatch.")
    else:
        config_path.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2))
    (output / "batch_schedule.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(schedules, sort_keys=True)
    )
    print("Overlap checks: PASSED | Fingerprints: MATCH")
    print("EXPERIMENT DIRECTORY:", output, flush=True)
    eval_loaders = {
        "train": DataLoader(
            train_subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
        ),
        "val": DataLoader(
            val_subset, batch_size=8, shuffle=False, num_workers=0
        ),
    }
    pos_weight = torch.tensor([3.0], device=device)
    def loss_parts(logits, targets):
        logits = logits.float()
        bce = F.binary_cross_entropy_with_logits(
            logits, targets, pos_weight=pos_weight
        )
        probs = logits.sigmoid()
        intersection = (probs * targets).sum()
        denominator = probs.sum() + targets.sum()
        dice_loss = 1 - (2 * intersection + 1) / (denominator + 1)
        return bce + dice_loss, bce, dice_loss
    @torch.inference_mode()
    def evaluate(model, loader, records, captured, seed, epoch, split):
        model.eval()
        n_pixels = len(records) * 256 * 256
        scores = np.empty(n_pixels, np.float32)
        truth_cpu = np.empty(n_pixels, np.bool_)
        pixel_offset = image_offset = 0
        loss_sum = bce_sum = dice_sum = 0.0
        samples = fg_pixels = bg_pixels = 0
        tp = fp = fn = pred_pixels = auth_fp = auth_pixels = 0
        prob_fg = prob_bg = 0.0
        max_prob = 0.0
        channel_active_pixels = np.zeros(16, np.int64)
        channel_active_images = np.zeros(16, np.int64)
        activation_max = 0.0
--- PAGE 216 ---
        positive_fg = positive_bg = negative_fg = negative_bg = 0.0
        details = []
        head_weights = model.head.weight.detach()[0, :, 0, 0]
        for images, masks in loader:
            images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
            logits = model(images).float()
            activation = captured.pop("activation")
            loss, bce, dice_loss = loss_parts(logits, masks)
            if not (
                torch.isfinite(loss).item()
                and torch.isfinite(activation).all().item()
            ):
                raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite evaluation values.")
            n = images.shape[0]
            samples += n
            loss_sum += loss.item() * n
            bce_sum += bce.item() * n
            dice_sum += dice_loss.item() * n
            probs = logits.sigmoid()
            gt = masks.bool()
            pred = probs > .5
            fg_pixels += gt.sum().item()
            bg_pixels += (~gt).sum().item()
            pred_pixels += pred.sum().item()
            prob_fg += probs[gt].double().sum().item()
            prob_bg += probs[~gt].double().sum().item()
            max_prob = max(max_prob, probs.max().item())
            channel_active_pixels += (
                (activation != 0).sum((0, 2, 3)).cpu().numpy()
            )
            channel_active_images += (
                (activation != 0).flatten(2).any(2).sum(0).cpu().numpy()
            )
            activation_max = max(activation_max, activation.max().item())
            contribution = activation * head_weights[None, :, None, None]
            positive_map = contribution.clamp_min(0).sum(1)
            negative_map = contribution.clamp_max(0).sum(1)
            foreground = gt[:, 0]
            positive_fg += positive_map[foreground].double().sum().item()
            positive_bg += positive_map[~foreground].double().sum().item()
            negative_fg += negative_map[foreground].double().sum().item()
            negative_bg += negative_map[~foreground].double().sum().item()
            length = logits.numel()
            scores[pixel_offset:pixel_offset + length] = (
                logits.cpu().numpy().reshape(-1)
            )
            truth_cpu[pixel_offset:pixel_offset + length] = (
                gt.cpu().numpy().reshape(-1)
            )
            pixel_offset += length
            for j in range(n):
                record = records[image_offset + j]
                p, truth = pred[j], gt[j]
                itp = (p & truth).sum().item()
                ifp = (p & ~truth).sum().item()
                ifn = (~p & truth).sum().item()
                tp += itp
                fp += ifp
                fn += ifn
                if label(record) == "tampered":
                    dice = 2 * itp / (2 * itp + ifp + ifn)
                else:
                    dice = None
                    auth_fp += p.sum().item()
                    auth_pixels += p.numel()
                details.append({
--- PAGE 217 ---
                    "seed": seed, "epoch": epoch, "split": split,
                    "filename": Path(record["image_path"]).name,
                    "label": label(record),
                    "dice_05": dice,
                    "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * p.float().mean().item(),
                    "tp": itp, "fp": ifp, "fn": ifn,
                })
            image_offset += n
        assert pixel_offset == n_pixels
        print(f"  Seed {seed}, epoch {epoch}: computing {split} AP...",
              flush=True)
        ap = float(average_precision_score(truth_cpu, scores))
        del scores, truth_cpu
        gc.collect()
        dices = [r["dice_05"] for r in details if r["label"] == "tampered"]
        metrics = {
            "loss": loss_sum / samples,
            "bce": bce_sum / samples,
            "dice_loss": dice_sum / samples,
            "micro_dice": 2 * tp / max(2 * tp + fp + fn, 1),
            "mean_tampered_dice": float(np.mean(dices)),
            "precision": tp / max(tp + fp, 1),
            "recall": tp / max(tp + fn, 1),
            "tampered_dice_below_01": sum(d < .1 for d in dices),
            "authentic_fp_pct": 100 * auth_fp / max(auth_pixels, 1),
            "predicted_positive_pct": 100 * pred_pixels / n_pixels,
            "pixel_AP": ap,
            "AP_baseline_prevalence": fg_pixels / n_pixels,
            "prob_in_mask": prob_fg / fg_pixels,
            "prob_background": prob_bg / bg_pixels,
            "max_probability": max_prob,
            "activation_zero_pct": (
                100 * (1 - channel_active_pixels.sum() / (16 * n_pixels))
            ),
            "channels_zero_all_images": int((channel_active_images == 0).sum()),
            "activation_max": activation_max,
            "positive_contribution_mask": positive_fg / fg_pixels,
            "negative_contribution_mask": negative_fg / fg_pixels,
            "positive_contribution_background": positive_bg / bg_pixels,
            "negative_contribution_background": negative_bg / bg_pixels,
        }
        channel_details = [{
            "seed": seed, "epoch": epoch, "split": split,
            "channel": channel,
            "head_weight": head_weights[channel].item(),
            "active_pixel_pct": 100 * channel_active_pixels[channel] / n_pixels,
            "images_active": int(channel_active_images[channel]),
            "images_total": len(records),
        } for channel in range(16)]
        return metrics, details, channel_details
    old_benchmark = torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark
    old_cudnn_det = torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic
    old_algorithms = torch.are_deterministic_algorithms_enabled()
    old_warn = torch.is_deterministic_algorithms_warn_only_enabled()
    cuda_devices = (
        [torch.cuda.current_device()] if device.type == "cuda" else []
    )
    combined = []
    try:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = True
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
        for seed in SEEDS:
            seed_dir = output / f"seed_{seed}"
            seed_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
            last_path = seed_dir / "last.pt"
            with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=cuda_devices):
                # Initialization seed only; schedules use a separate NumPy RNG.
                torch.manual_seed(seed)
--- PAGE 218 ---
                model = UNet(
                    in_channels=3, out_channels=1, base_channels=16
                ).to(device)
                optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW(
                    model.parameters(), lr=LR, weight_decay=1e-4
                )
                captured = {}
                def capture(module, inputs):
                    captured["activation"] = inputs[0]
                hook = model.head.register_forward_pre_hook(capture)
                history, per_image, channel_history, online_history = [], [], [], []
                best_score, best_epoch, best_state = -1.0, None, None
                start_epoch = 0
                if last_path.exists():
                    saved = torch.load(
                        last_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True
                    )
                    if (
                        saved.get("kind") != "initialization_study_v1"
                        or saved["seed"] != seed
                        or saved["config"] != config
                    ):
                        raise RuntimeError("Invalid resume checkpoint.")
                    if saved["epoch"] > EPOCHS:
                        raise RuntimeError("EPOCHS smaller than saved epoch.")
                    model.load_state_dict(saved["model_state"])
                    optimizer.load_state_dict(saved["optimizer_state"])
                    history = saved["history"]
                    per_image = saved["per_image"]
                    channel_history = saved["channel_history"]
                    online_history = saved["online_history"]
                    best_score = saved["best_score"]
                    best_epoch = saved["best_epoch"]
                    best_state = saved["best_model_state"]
                    start_epoch = saved["epoch"] + 1
                    torch.set_rng_state(saved["torch_rng"])
                    if device.type == "cuda" and saved["cuda_rng"] is not None:
                        torch.cuda.set_rng_state(saved["cuda_rng"], device)
                    del saved
                print(f"\n=== SEED {seed} | Start epoch {start_epoch} ===")
                try:
                    for epoch in range(start_epoch, EPOCHS + 1):
                        if epoch > 0:
                            loader = DataLoader(
                                train_subset,
                                batch_sampler=schedules[epoch],
                                num_workers=0,
                                pin_memory=device.type == "cuda",
                            )
                            model.train()
                            total_loss = count = 0
                            for images, masks in loader:
                                images, masks = images.to(device), masks.to(device)
                                optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                                logits = model(images)
                                captured.pop("activation")
                                loss, _, _ = loss_parts(logits, masks)
                                if not torch.isfinite(loss).item():
                                    raise RuntimeError("Nonfinite training loss.")
                                loss.backward()
                                for name, parameter in model.named_parameters():
                                    if not parameter.requires_grad:
                                        continue
                                    if parameter.grad is None:
                                        raise RuntimeError(f"Missing gradient: {name}")
                                    if not torch.isfinite(parameter.grad).all().item():
                                        raise RuntimeError(f"Nonfinite gradient: {name}")
--- PAGE 219 ---
                                optimizer.step()
                                total_loss += loss.item() * images.shape[0]
                                count += images.shape[0]
                            online_history.append({
                                "epoch": epoch,
                                "online_loss": total_loss / count,
                            })
                        if epoch % EVAL_EVERY == 0 or epoch == EPOCHS:
                            tm, td, tc = evaluate(
                                model, eval_loaders["train"], train_records,
                                captured, seed, epoch, "train"
                            )
                            vm, vd, vc = evaluate(
                                model, eval_loaders["val"], val_records,
                                captured, seed, epoch, "val"
                            )
                            history.append({
                                "seed": seed,
                                "epoch": epoch,
                                "optimizer_steps": epoch * 32,
                                **{f"train_{k}": v for k, v in tm.items()},
                                **{f"val_{k}": v for k, v in vm.items()},
                            })
                            per_image.extend(td + vd)
                            channel_history.extend(tc + vc)
                            score = vm["mean_tampered_dice"]
                            if score > best_score:
                                best_score, best_epoch = score, epoch
                                best_state = cpu_copy(model.state_dict())
                            print(
                                f"Seed {seed} | Epoch {epoch:02d} | "
                                f"Train Dice {tm['micro_dice']:.4f} | "
                                f"Train AP {tm['pixel_AP']:.4f} | "
                                f"Val Dice {vm['micro_dice']:.4f} | "
                                f"Val AP {vm['pixel_AP']:.4f} | "
                                f"Train zero channels "
                                f"{tm['channels_zero_all_images']}/16",
                                flush=True,
                            )
                        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                        save_atomic({
                            "kind": "initialization_study_v1",
                            "seed": seed,
                            "epoch": epoch,
                            "config": config,
                            "model_state": cpu_copy(model.state_dict()),
                            "optimizer_state": cpu_copy(optimizer.state_dict()),
                            "history": history,
                            "per_image": per_image,
                            "channel_history": channel_history,
                            "online_history": online_history,
                            "best_score": best_score,
                            "best_epoch": best_epoch,
                            "best_model_state": best_state,
                            "torch_rng": torch.get_rng_state(),
                            "cuda_rng": (
                                torch.cuda.get_rng_state(device)
                                if device.type == "cuda" else None
                            ),
                        }, last_path)
                        if epoch % EVAL_EVERY == 0 or epoch == EPOCHS:
                            save_atomic({
                                "kind": "initialization_study_best_weights",
                                "seed": seed,
                                "epoch": best_epoch,
                                "config": config,
                                "validation_mean_tampered_dice": best_score,
                                "model_state": best_state,
                            }, seed_dir / "best_val_macro.pt")
                        for data, filename in [
--- PAGE 220 ---
                            (history, "evaluations.csv"),
                            (per_image, "per_image.csv"),
                            (channel_history, "channels.csv"),
                            (online_history, "online_history.csv"),
                        ]:
                            pd.DataFrame(data).to_csv(
                                seed_dir / filename, index=False
                            )
                    combined.extend(history)
                finally:
                    hook.remove()
                    captured.clear()
                    model.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
                del model, optimizer, best_state
                gc.collect()
                if device.type == "cuda":
                    torch.cuda.empty_cache()
    finally:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = old_benchmark
        torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = old_cudnn_det
        torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(
            old_algorithms, warn_only=old_warn
        )
    results = pd.DataFrame(combined)
    results.to_csv(output / "comparison.csv", index=False)
    return results, output

class SyntheticRectangles(Dataset):
    """Clearly labeled synthetic fixtures; no real images or annotations."""
    def __init__(self, count, seed):
        assert count % 2 == 0
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.images = np.empty((count, 256, 256, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        self.masks = np.zeros((count, 256, 256), dtype=np.uint8)
        for i in range(count):
            # Dark background, varying by image and RGB channel.
            base = rng.integers(20, 85, size=(1, 1, 3))
            noise = rng.normal(0, 5, size=(256, 256, 3))
            image = np.clip(base + noise, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
            if i >= count // 2:
                # About 2–16% foreground on positive images.
                height = int(rng.integers(36, 103))
                width = int(rng.integers(36, 103))
                top = int(rng.integers(0, 257 - height))
                left = int(rng.integers(0, 257 - width))
                color = rng.integers(175, 246, size=(1, 1, 3))
                patch_noise = rng.normal(0, 4, size=(height, width, 3))
                image[top:top + height, left:left + width] = np.clip(
                    color + patch_noise, 0, 255
                ).astype(np.uint8)
                self.masks[i, top:top + height, left:left + width] = 1
            self.images[i] = image
    def __len__(self):
        return len(self.images)
    def __getitem__(self, index):
        image = torch.from_numpy(
            self.images[index].transpose(2, 0, 1).copy()
        ).float() / 255.0
        mask = torch.from_numpy(
            self.masks[index][None].copy()
        ).float()
        return image, mask
